# UGEKAZNA S1-reja: «Обзор» + «Казначейство» + 1C konnektori — implementatsiya rejasi

> **For agentic workers:** REQUIRED SUB-SKILL: Use superpowers:subagent-driven-development (recommended) or superpowers:executing-plans to implement this plan task-by-task. Steps use checkbox (`- [ ]`) syntax for tracking.

**Goal:** TZ 1-bosqichining qolgan qismini yopish: «Обзор» (5 sahifa) va «Казначейство» (5 sahifa) витрина агрегатлари ustida, «Требует внимания» dvigateli, `.xlsx` eksport va mavjud 1C HTTP-servislaridan ma'lumot oluvchi `OneCConnector`.

**Architecture:** Backend 2-reja freymvorkiga 6 ta yangi `mart.agg_*` agregatini (`register(Aggregate)`) va `pub` view'larini qo'shadi; bo'lim endpointlari `router → app/sections/queries → pub view` zanjiri, `Cache.respond` (Redis + ETag) va router darajasidagi `require_perm` bilan. «Требует внимания» — `app/alerts` reyestri: `core.alert_rules` dagi chegaralar har so'rovda filtrlar bilan tekshiriladi. `OneCConnector` — `SourceConnector` protokoli, httpx (TLS, BOM, o'lcham chegarasi) va qat'iy pydantic modellari; faqat `worker` (egress tarmog'i). Frontend 3-reja qobig'iga `src/sections/<bo'lim>/<sahifa>.tsx` fayllarini qo'shadi; umumiy dashboard bloklari `src/dash/`, grafiklar Chart.js 4 (bundle) o'rami orqali.

**Tech Stack:** Python 3.12, FastAPI, SQLAlchemy 2 (sync) + psycopg 3, PostgreSQL 16, Redis 7, httpx, openpyxl, pydantic 2, pytest; React 18 + TypeScript strict, TanStack Query 5, Chart.js 4, Vitest + Testing Library + axe, Playwright 1.49.1.

**Spec:** [docs/superpowers/specs/2026-09-25-s1-obzor-kazna-design.md](../specs/2026-09-25-s1-obzor-kazna-design.md). Asos: 1-reja [2026-09-24-01-backend-yadro.md](2026-09-24-01-backend-yadro.md) (`Ctx`, `require_perm`, `AuditAction`, `record`/`log_event`, `Meta`, `ValidationFailed`, `ratelimit`), 2-reja [2026-09-24-02-vitrina-etl.md](2026-09-24-02-vitrina-etl.md) (`TARGETS`, `merge_*`, `register(Aggregate)`, `BuildParams`, `builds`, `Cache`, `SyntheticConnector`, `FAMILIES`, `make_connector`, `DEFAULT_SETTINGS`, `validate_setting`, `tests.etl_helpers`, `published_synthetic`), 3-reja [2026-09-24-03-frontend.md](2026-09-24-03-frontend.md) (qobiq, `SectionPageProps`, `api()`, `Card`, `KpiTile`, `Table`, `Modal`, `EmptyState`, `ErrorBanner`, `mockApi`, `renderWithProviders`, E2E fixture'lari), 4-reja [2026-09-25-04-ops-security.md](2026-09-25-04-ops-security.md) (`compose.prod.yml`, `egress` tarmog'i, `prod_config.sh`). 1–4-rejalar bajarilgan deb hisoblanadi.

## Global Constraints

- 1–4-rejalarning barcha Global Constraints'lari amal qiladi (Python `>=3.12`, hash bilan qulflangan bog'liqliklar, sirlar faqat Docker secrets, UTC, `/api/v1`, xato formati `{"error": {code, message, request_id, params?}}`, ruscha UI matnlari, o'zbekcha reja prozasi va izohlar).
- Backend testlari: `TEST="docker compose -f deploy/compose.test.yml run --rm test"`, keyin `$TEST pytest …` — haqiqiy PostgreSQL 16 va Redis; DB mock qilinmaydi.
- Frontend: `NODE="docker compose -f deploy/compose.frontend.yml run --rm node"`, keyin `$NODE npm …`; host Node ishlatilmaydi. Git Bash'da bind-mount'li `docker run` oldidan `MSYS_NO_PATHCONV=1`.
- Summalar `numeric(20,2)` **mln so'm**; `date_id` = `YYYYMMDD`, `month_id` = `YYYYMM`; `branch_id = 0` — faqat `mart`'da "barcha filiallar jami", faqat `scope_all` ko'radi.
- UI va AI faqat `pub.*` view'larni o'qiydi; faktlarga so'rov yo'q (TZ 9). Yangi ko'rsatkich — faqat `register(Aggregate(...))` + migratsiyadagi `mart` jadvali + `pub` view.
- Har yangi `mart` jadvali: `build_id → core.builds ON DELETE CASCADE`, `month_id` ustuni (2-reja carry-forward talabi), `ENABLE` + `FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY`, policy `core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id)`; `pub` view `security_invoker = true`, `WHERE build_id = (SELECT active_build_id FROM core.data_version WHERE id = 1)`.
- Har bo'lim endpointi: `GET`, `br: int ≥ 1 | None`, `per ∈ ytd|q1..q4|m01..m12` (joriy yil, `build_periods(today)` da bo'lishi shart, aks holda 422), `ctx.assert_branch(br)` (doiradan tashqari → 403 `scope_violation` + audit), router darajasida `require_perm(section:<id>:view)`, javob `cache.respond("<bo'lim>.<sahifa>", q.cache_filters, loader)`; `cache_filters` = `{"br", "per", "today"}`.
- Formulalar — spec 4-bo'limdagi jadval (TZ 4) aynan; «Отклонение по статье» = план − факт; yopilmagan oy o'tgan yil bilan shuncha kun bo'yicha solishtiriladi.
- «Требует внимания»: chegaralar, operator va daraja faqat `core.alert_rules` dan (kodda emas); ma'lumot yo'q bo'lsa qoida o'tkazib yuboriladi.
- 1C: faqat `worker`; `httpx.Timeout(connect=5, read=180, write=30, pool=5)`, `verify` yoqilgan, `trust_env=False`, javob ≤ 64 MiB, BOM olib tashlanadi; `http://` faqat `ONEC_ALLOW_HTTP=true` bilan; testlarda faqat `httpx.MockTransport`. Credential fayllari (`secrets.php`, `auth.php`, `auth.js`, `apache-proxy.conf`, `.env`) ochilmaydi va hech qayerga ko'chirilmaydi.
- Eksport: `.xlsx` — `export:xlsx`, ≤ 10 s, audit `data.exported`, matn kataklari formula sifatida talqin qilinmaydi; brauzerdagi CSV/PNG — `export:csv`, avval `POST /exports/log` (204), keyin yuklab olish; CSV UTF-8 BOM, `;`, formula prefiksli qiymatlar oldiga `'`.
- Frontend: Chart.js faqat npm bundle (CDN yo'q), CSP o'zgarmaydi (`style-src 'self'`) — `setAttribute('style')`, inline `<style>` yo'q; `src/sections/<bo'lim>/*.tsx` — faqat sahifalar (reyestr glob'i), yordamchi komponentlar `src/sections/<bo'lim>/parts/` va `src/dash/` da; barcha matnlar `t()` orqali (`src/i18n/ru.ts` oxiriga qo'shiladi).
- Commit xabari oxirida bo'sh qatordan keyin: `Co-Authored-By: Claude Opus 5.5 (1M context) <noreply@anthropic.com>`.
- Spec'dan ongli chetlanishlar yo'q; S1 qarorlari (spec 3–8): `Target.forward`, `doc_type=payment_schedule`, `agg_calendar_day` nol qatorlari, `onec.income_enabled=false`, 1C kodlari nom xeshidan, `onec.orgs` — CLI orqali.

## Review Focus

1. **Bir nechta filialga bog'langan (lekin `scope_all` bo'lmagan) foydalanuvchi `br`siz so'raydi** — jami `branch_id = 0` qatoridan EMAS, faqat o'z filiallari yig'indisidan chiqishi kerak (aks holda boshqa filiallar summasi sizadi): 5-vazifa `test_multi_branch_scope_sums_only_own_rows`, 7-vazifa `test_limited_scope_without_br_sees_own_totals`.
2. **Qoldiqlar umuman yo'q (prod'da 1C bo'shlig'i G2)** — KPI'lar `null`, sahifa yiqilmaydi, qoldiqqa bog'liq qoidalar yolg'on signal bermaydi: 9-vazifa `test_balance_rules_skip_without_balances`, 7-vazifa `test_svod_without_balances_returns_nulls`.
3. **1C javobi buzilgan** (BOM, HTML xato sahifasi, `limit` ga teng qator soni, noto'g'ri sana) — qayta urinilmaydigan tushunarli xato, hech narsa publish qilinmaydi: 11-vazifa `test_html_error_page_is_format_error`, 12-vazifa `test_payments_at_limit_raise_truncated`, `test_runner_fails_without_publish_on_bad_payload`.
4. **1C'dan kelgan nom `=`/`+`/`-`/`@` bilan boshlanadi** (kontragent, statya) — xlsx/CSV'da formula emas, matn: 10-vazifa `test_formula_like_names_are_text`, 14-vazifa `escapes formula-like cells`.
5. **Yopilmagan oy tanlangan (`m09`, 24-sentabr)** — o'tgan yil bilan solishtirish 1–24-sentabr 2025 bo'yicha, qoldiq ham 24-sentabrdagi: 5-vazifa `test_yoy_unclosed_month_uses_same_days`.

`backend/tests/test_s1_prereq.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.dwh import builds
from app.dwh.aggregates import BuildParams, build_all
from app.dwh.reference import DEFAULT_SETTINGS, validate_setting
from app.dwh.targets import SCHEDULE_DOC_TYPE, TARGETS
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from tests.etl_helpers import (
    TEST_AS_OF,
    branch_row,
    merge_all,
    new_source,
    new_tag,
    open_run,
    purge_source,
    stage,
)


@pytest.fixture
def source() -> Iterator[str]:
    name = new_source()
    yield name
    purge_source(name)


def _plan(src_id: str, day: int, branch: str, amount: float,
          doc_type: str = "payment_plan") -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"doc_type": doc_type, "src_id": src_id, "date_id": day, "branch_code": branch,
            "item_code": "PKM54-O09", "amount": amount}


def _src_ids(source: str, table: str) -> set[str]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        return set(conn.execute(
            text(f"SELECT src_id FROM dwh.{table} WHERE src_system = :s"), {"s": source}).scalars())


def test_s1_settings_defaults_and_validation() -> None:
    assert DEFAULT_SETTINGS["kazna.calendar_kinds"] == {
        "taxes": ["PKM54-O03"], "salary": ["PKM54-O05"], "loans": ["PKM54-O04"]}
    assert DEFAULT_SETTINGS["onec.orgs"] == []
    assert DEFAULT_SETTINGS["onec.income_enabled"] is False
    assert DEFAULT_SETTINGS["onec.page_limit"] == 100000
    assert validate_setting("etl.onec_full_cron", "0 2 * * *") == "0 2 * * *"
    assert validate_setting("etl.onec_incremental_cron", "0 10,13,16 * * *")
    orgs = [{"branch_code": "UGE-01", "org_uid": "a1b2-c3", "org_name": "АО «Узбекгидроэнерго»"}]
    assert validate_setting("onec.orgs", orgs) == orgs
    bad_values: list[tuple[str, Any]] = [
        ("onec.orgs", orgs + orgs),
        ("onec.orgs", [{"branch_code": "UGE-01", "org_uid": "a b", "org_name": "X"}]),
        ("onec.orgs", [{**orgs[0], "extra": 1}]),
        ("kazna.calendar_kinds", {"rent": ["X"]}),
        ("kazna.calendar_kinds", {"taxes": ["A"], "salary": ["A"]}),
        ("onec.income_enabled", "yes"),
        ("onec.page_limit", 10),
        ("etl.onec_full_cron", "every night"),
    ]
    for key, value in bad_values:
        with pytest.raises(ValueError):
            validate_setting(key, value)


def test_forward_flags() -> None:
    assert TARGETS["fact_payment_plan"].forward
    assert TARGETS["fact_loan_schedule"].forward
    assert not TARGETS["fact_cashflow"].forward
    assert not TARGETS["fact_target_funds"].forward


def test_forward_target_replaces_future_rows(source: str) -> None:
    branch = branch_row(new_tag())
    code = branch["code"]
    first = open_run(source)
    stage(first, source, {"dim_branch": [branch], "fact_payment_plan": [
        _plan("p-past", 20260801, code, 50),
        _plan("s-future", 20261005, code, 7, SCHEDULE_DOC_TYPE)]})
    merge_all(first, source, complete={"fact_payment_plan"})
    assert _src_ids(source, "fact_payment_plan") == {"p-past", "s-future"}
    second = open_run(source)
    stage(second, source, {"fact_payment_plan": [_plan("p-past", 20260801, code, 50)]})
    merge_all(second, source, complete={"fact_payment_plan"})
    assert _src_ids(source, "fact_payment_plan") == {"p-past"}


def test_non_forward_target_keeps_rows_after_window(source: str) -> None:
    tag = new_tag()
    branch = branch_row(tag)
    src = {"code": f"S-{tag}", "name": "Тестовый источник", "kind": "own"}
    fund = {"doc_type": "target_funds", "src_id": "tf-future", "date_id": 20261101,
            "branch_code": branch["code"], "source_code": src["code"], "allocated": 10, "used": 4}
    first = open_run(source)
    stage(first, source, {"dim_branch": [branch], "dim_source": [src], "fact_target_funds": [fund]})
    merge_all(first, source, complete={"fact_target_funds"})
    second = open_run(source)
    stage(second, source, {"fact_target_funds": []})
    merge_all(second, source, complete={"fact_target_funds"})
    assert _src_ids(source, "fact_target_funds") == {"tf-future"}


def test_payment_schedule_rows_excluded_from_plan_aggregate(source: str) -> None:
    branch = branch_row(new_tag())
    code = branch["code"]
    run = open_run(source)
    stage(run, source, {"dim_branch": [branch], "fact_payment_plan": [
        _plan("p1", 20260801, code, 50), _plan("s1", 20260815, code, 7, SCHEDULE_DOC_TYPE)]})
    merge_all(run, source)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        branch_id = conn.execute(text("SELECT branch_id FROM dwh.dim_branch WHERE code = :c"),
                                 {"c": code}).scalar_one()
        build_id = builds.create_build(conn, run)
        build_all(conn, build_id, BuildParams(as_of=TEST_AS_OF, date_from=date(2026, 1, 1)))
        plan = conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT plan_amount FROM mart.agg_payments_month WHERE build_id = :b "
            "AND branch_id = :br AND month_id = 202608"), {"b": build_id, "br": branch_id}).scalar_one()
        builds.mark_failed(conn, build_id)
    assert float(plan) == 50.0
```

`_fact` funksiyasini to'liq almashtiring:
```python
def _fact(name: str, measures: tuple[str, ...], lookups: tuple[Lookup, ...],
          partition: Literal["month", "year"] | None = None, *, forward: bool = False) -> Target:
    conflict = ("src_system", "doc_type", "src_id") + (("date_id",) if partition else ())
    return Target(name, "fact", _FACT_TECH + measures, conflict, lookups, partition,
                  windowed=True, forward=forward)
```

```python
          (BRANCH, Lookup("item_id", "dim_item", fallback_sql=_ITEM_OUT)), forward=True),
```

- [ ] **Step 4: `merge.py` — forward almashtirish**

`backend/app/etl/merge.py` — `_replace_sql` funksiyasini to'liq almashtiring:
```python
@cache
def _replace_sql(name: str) -> str:
    # forward maqsad: manba oynadan keyingi (kelajak) qatorlarni ham beradi — yuqori chegara yo'q.
    upper = "" if TARGETS[name].forward else " AND t.date_id <= :to_id"
    return (
        f"DELETE FROM dwh.{name} t WHERE t.src_system = :source "
        f"AND t.date_id >= :from_id{upper} AND NOT EXISTS ("
        "SELECT 1 FROM stg.rows s WHERE s.load_id = :load_id AND s.target = :target "
        "AND s.payload->>'src_id' = t.src_id AND s.payload->>'doc_type' = t.doc_type)"
    )
```

`backend/app/dwh/aggregates.py` — `_PAYMENTS` ichidagi `plan AS (…)` CTE'ni almashtiring:

```python
plan AS (

  FROM dwh.fact_payment_plan p
  JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = p.date_id
  JOIN months m ON m.month_id = d.month_id
  WHERE p.doc_type <> 'payment_schedule'
  GROUP BY p.branch_id, d.month_id, p.item_id),
```

- [ ] **Step 6: `reference.py` — S1 sozlamalari**

`backend/app/dwh/reference.py` — importlarga qo'shing (mavjudlari saqlanadi):
```python
from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict
```
`DEFAULT_SETTINGS` dan OLDIN qo'shing:
```python
CALENDAR_KINDS = ("taxes", "salary", "loans")  # qolgan statyalar — "contracts"


def _calendar_kinds(value: dict[str, list[str]]) -> dict[str, list[str]]:
    unknown = set(value) - set(CALENDAR_KINDS)
    if unknown:
        raise ValueError(f"noma'lum kalendar turi: {sorted(unknown)}")
    seen: set[str] = set()
    for codes in value.values():
        for code in codes:
            if not code or len(code) > 32 or code in seen:
                raise ValueError(f"noto'g'ri yoki takrorlangan statya kodi: {code!r}")
            seen.add(code)
    return {kind: list(codes) for kind, codes in value.items()}


class OneCOrg(BaseModel):
    """1C tashkiloti ↔ filial (spec 3.1). org_uid — 1C UID (so'rovda `org_uid`)."""

    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid", strict=True)
    branch_code: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, max_length=32)]
    org_uid: Annotated[str, Field(pattern=r"^[0-9A-Za-z-]{1,64}$")]
    org_name: Annotated[str, Field(min_length=1, max_length=300)]


def _onec_orgs(value: list[OneCOrg]) -> list[dict[str, str]]:
    for attr in ("branch_code", "org_uid", "org_name"):
        values = [getattr(org, attr) for org in value]
        if len(set(values)) != len(values):
            raise ValueError(f"onec.orgs: {attr} takrorlangan")
    return [org.model_dump() for org in value]
```

**Files:**

- Modify: `backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/__init__.py` (`FAMILIES`), `backend/app/etl/synthetic/__init__.py` (hisoblagichlar yig'indisi)
- Test: `backend/tests/test_synthetic_s1.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SynthModel` (`seed`, `as_of`), `BRANCHES`, `EXPENSE_ITEMS`, `LOANS`, `share`, `rnd`, `money`, `date_id`, `SCHEDULE_DOC_TYPE`, `StagingSink`, `Window`.
- Produces:
  - `families.calendar`: `HORIZON_DAYS = 30`, `TAX_ITEM = "PKM54-O03"`, `SALARY_ITEM = "PKM54-O05"`, `CONTRACT_ITEMS`, `generate(model, window, sink) -> {"fact_payment_plan": n}` — `doc_type="payment_schedule"`, `src_id="PS-{branch:02d}-{item}-{date_id}"`, faqat as_of+1…as_of+30 ish kunlari; soliq 15 va 25-sanalarda, ish haqi 10 va 25-sanalarda.
  - `families.loans`: `REF_RATES = {"USD": 12500, "EUR": 13500, "CNY": 1736, "UZS": 1}`, `generate(model, window, sink) -> {"fact_loan_schedule": n}` — `doc_type="loan_schedule"`, `src_id="LS-{n:02d}-{YYYYMM}"`, sana — oyning 15-kuni, summalar kredit valyutasida (mln), prototip formulasi (imtiyozdan keyin teng asosiy qarz, foiz = qoldiq × stavka / to'lovlar soni), 2026-yildan so'ndirish yiligacha.

- [ ] **Step 1: Testlarni yozish**

`backend/tests/test_synthetic_s1.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Mapping
from datetime import date, timedelta
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

from app.dwh.dates import date_id
from app.dwh.targets import SCHEDULE_DOC_TYPE
from app.etl.synthetic import SyntheticConnector
from app.etl.synthetic.families import calendar, loans
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel
from app.etl.types import Window
from tests.etl_helpers import TEST_AS_OF


class CollectSink:
    def __init__(self) -> None:
        self.rows: list[tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]] = []

    def add(self, target: str, row: Mapping[str, Any]) -> None:
        self.rows.append((target, dict(row)))


WINDOW = Window(date(2026, 1, 1), TEST_AS_OF)


def _schedule() -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    sink = CollectSink()
    calendar.generate(SynthModel(7, TEST_AS_OF), WINDOW, sink)
    return [row for target, row in sink.rows if target == "fact_payment_plan"]


def test_schedule_rows_are_future_workdays_with_fixed_tax_days() -> None:
    rows = _schedule()
    assert rows
    lo, hi = date_id(TEST_AS_OF + timedelta(days=1)), date_id(TEST_AS_OF + timedelta(days=30))
    for row in rows:
        assert row["doc_type"] == SCHEDULE_DOC_TYPE
        assert lo <= row["date_id"] <= hi
        day = date(row["date_id"] // 10000, row["date_id"] // 100 % 100, row["date_id"] % 100)
        assert day.weekday() < 5
        assert isinstance(row["amount"], Decimal) and row["amount"] > 0
        if row["item_code"] == calendar.TAX_ITEM:
            assert day.day in (15, 25)
        if row["item_code"] == calendar.SALARY_ITEM:
            assert day.day in (10, 25)
    assert {r["item_code"] for r in rows} & set(calendar.CONTRACT_ITEMS)


def test_schedule_is_deterministic() -> None:
    assert _schedule() == _schedule()


def test_loan_schedule_amortises_to_zero() -> None:
    sink = CollectSink()
    loans.generate(SynthModel(7, TEST_AS_OF), WINDOW, sink)
    rows = [row for target, row in sink.rows if target == "fact_loan_schedule"]
    short = sorted((r for r in rows if r["loan_code"] == "LOAN-07"), key=lambda r: r["date_id"])
    assert short[0]["date_id"] == 20260115 and short[-1]["date_id"] == 20271215
    assert abs(short[-1]["balance"]) < Decimal("0.01")
    assert all(r["doc_type"] == "loan_schedule" and r["date_id"] % 100 == 15 for r in rows)
    grace = [r for r in rows if r["loan_code"] == "LOAN-01" and r["date_id"] < 20270101]
    assert grace and all(r["principal"] == 0 and r["interest"] > 0 for r in grace)


def test_extract_sums_counts_of_same_target() -> None:
    sink = CollectSink()
    stats = SyntheticConnector(seed=7, as_of=TEST_AS_OF).extract(WINDOW, sink)
    plans = sum(1 for target, _ in sink.rows if target == "fact_payment_plan")
    assert stats.by_target["fact_payment_plan"] == plans
    assert stats.by_target["fact_loan_schedule"] > 0
    assert stats.rows == len(sink.rows)
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementatsiya — kalendar oilasi**

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/calendar.py`:
```python
"""fact_payment_plan (doc_type=payment_schedule): as_of+1 … as_of+30 kunlar uchun sanali to'lovlar.

Prototip CAL_CATS g'oyasi: soliqlar 15 va 25-sanalarda, ish haqi 10 va 25-sanalarda, shartnomalar
bo'yicha to'lovlar ish kunlarida tasodifiy. Kredit to'lovlari bu yerda EMAS — loans oilasida
(fact_loan_schedule), kalendar agregati ularni ikki marta sanamaydi (spec 4).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date, timedelta

from app.dwh.dates import date_id
from app.dwh.targets import SCHEDULE_DOC_TYPE
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel, money
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import BRANCHES, EXPENSE_ITEMS, BranchRef, share
from app.etl.synthetic.rng import rnd
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window

HORIZON_DAYS = 30
TAX_ITEM = "PKM54-O03"
SALARY_ITEM = "PKM54-O05"
CONTRACT_ITEMS = ("PKM54-O01", "PKM54-O02", "PKM54-O06", "PKM54-O07")
_BASE = {item.code: item for item in EXPENSE_ITEMS}


def _monthly(branch: BranchRef, code: str) -> float:
    item = _BASE[code]
    return item.base * share(branch, item.hq_share)


def _amounts(model: SynthModel, branch: BranchRef, day: date) -> list[tuple[str, float]]:
    r = rnd(model.seed, "sched", branch.id, date_id(day))
    out: list[tuple[str, float]] = []
    if day.day in (15, 25):
        out.append((TAX_ITEM, _monthly(branch, TAX_ITEM) / 2 * r.uniform(0.9, 1.1)))
    if day.day in (10, 25):
        out.append((SALARY_ITEM, _monthly(branch, SALARY_ITEM) / 2 * r.uniform(0.9, 1.1)))
    if r.random() < 0.5:
        code = r.choice(CONTRACT_ITEMS)
        out.append((code, _monthly(branch, code) / 21 * r.uniform(0.6, 1.5)))
    return [(code, amount) for code, amount in out if amount >= 0.01]


def generate(model: SynthModel, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> dict[str, int]:
    # Oyna faqat o'tgan davrni cheklaydi; jadval doim as_of dan keyingi 30 kun (forward maqsad).
    count = 0
    for branch in BRANCHES:
        for n in range(1, HORIZON_DAYS + 1):
            day = model.as_of + timedelta(days=n)
            if day.weekday() >= 5:
                continue
            for code, amount in _amounts(model, branch, day):
                sink.add("fact_payment_plan", {
                    "doc_type": SCHEDULE_DOC_TYPE,
                    "src_id": f"PS-{branch.id:02d}-{code}-{date_id(day)}",
                    "date_id": date_id(day), "branch_code": branch.code, "item_code": code,
                    "amount": money(amount),
                })
                count += 1
    return {"fact_payment_plan": count}
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — kredit grafigi oilasi**

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/loans.py`:
```python
"""fact_loan_schedule: prototip LOANS grafigi (2026 … so'ndirish yili), summalar kredit valyutasida.

Prototipdagi summa (×1000) — mln so'm ekvivalenti; valyutaga REF_RATES bilan o'giriladi.
To'lov oylari prototip `pm` ro'yxatidan (0 — yanvar), sana — oyning 15-kuni.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal

from app.dwh.dates import date_id
from app.etl.synthetic.model import SynthModel, money
from app.etl.synthetic.reference import BRANCHES, LOANS
from app.etl.types import StagingSink, Window

REF_RATES = {"USD": 12500.0, "EUR": 13500.0, "CNY": 1736.0, "UZS": 1.0}
# Prototip `pm` (to'lov oylari) — LOANS tartibida.
PAY_MONTHS: tuple[tuple[int, ...], ...] = (
    (3, 9), (4, 10), (5, 11), (0, 6), (2, 8), (3, 9), tuple(range(12)), (2, 5, 8, 11),
)
FIRST_YEAR = 2026


def generate(model: SynthModel, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> dict[str, int]:
    count = 0
    for n, (loan, months) in enumerate(zip(LOANS, PAY_MONTHS, strict=True), start=1):
        _creditor, _project, b_index, currency, amount, rate, grace, end = loan
        balance = amount * 1000 / REF_RATES[currency]
        for year in range(FIRST_YEAR, end + 1):
            annual = balance / (end - year + 1) if year >= grace else 0.0
            for month in months:
                interest = balance * rate / len(months)
                principal = annual / len(months)
                balance -= principal
                day = date(year, month + 1, 15)
                sink.add("fact_loan_schedule", {
                    "doc_type": "loan_schedule", "src_id": f"LS-{n:02d}-{year}{month + 1:02d}",
                    "date_id": date_id(day), "branch_code": BRANCHES[b_index].code,
                    "loan_code": f"LOAN-{n:02d}", "principal": money(principal),
                    "interest": money(interest),
                    "balance": money(balance) if abs(balance) >= 0.005 else Decimal("0.00"),
                })
                count += 1
    return {"fact_loan_schedule": count}
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Reyestr va hisoblagichlar**

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/families/__init__.py` — import qatorini almashtiring:
```python
from app.etl.synthetic.families import calendar, cash, debt, dims, funds, fx, loans, payments
```
va `FAMILIES` ro'yxati oxiriga qo'shing:
```python
    ("calendar", calendar.generate),
    ("loans", loans.generate),
```

`backend/app/etl/synthetic/__init__.py` — `extract` metodini almashtiring:
```python
    def extract(self, window: Window, sink: StagingSink) -> ExtractStats:
        counts: dict[str, int] = {}
        for _name, family in FAMILIES:
            for target, n in family(self.model, window, sink).items():
                # Bir maqsadni bir nechta oila beradi (payments + calendar → fact_payment_plan).
                counts[target] = counts.get(target, 0) + n
        return ExtractStats(sum(counts.values()), counts)
```

- [ ] **Step 1: Test yordamchisi — ssenariy**

`backend/tests/s1_helpers.py`:
```python
"""S1 testlari uchun qo'lda hisoblanadigan g'aznachilik ssenariysi (jadval — rejaning 3-vazifasida).

Har test o'z `src_system` va test filiali bilan ishlaydi; `purge_source(source)` hammasini o'chiradi.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text

from app.dwh import builds
from app.dwh.aggregates import BuildParams, build_all
from app.dwh.targets import SCHEDULE_DOC_TYPE
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from tests.etl_helpers import TEST_AS_OF, branch_row, merge_all, new_tag, open_run, stage

SCENARIO_PARAMS = BuildParams(as_of=TEST_AS_OF, date_from=date(2025, 1, 1))
IN_ITEM, OUT_ITEM = "PKM54-I06", "PKM54-O09"


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class Scenario:
    source: str
    tag: str
    branch_id: int
    branch_code: str
    run_id: int
    build_id: int
    item_out_id: int
    item_in_id: int


def _cash(src: str, day: int, branch: str, amount: float, direction: str, bank: str,
          **extra: Any) -> dict[str, Any]:
    return {"doc_type": "bank_entry", "src_id": src, "date_id": day, "branch_code": branch,
            "item_code": IN_ITEM if direction == "in" else OUT_ITEM, "direction": direction,
            "amount": amount, "gl_account": "5110", "bank_code": bank, **extra}


def scenario_rows(tag: str) -> dict[str, list[dict[str, Any]]]:
    branch = branch_row(tag)
    code = branch["code"]
    bank_a, bank_b = f"ACC-{tag}-A", f"ACC-{tag}-B"
    cp, ct1, ct2 = f"CP-{tag}", f"CT-{tag}-1", f"CT-{tag}-2"
    src, loan = f"S-{tag}", f"LOAN-{tag}"

    def bal(src_id: str, day: int, bank: str, value: float) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"doc_type": "statement", "src_id": src_id, "date_id": day, "branch_code": code,
                "bank_code": bank, "balance": value}

    def plan(src_id: str, day: int, amount: float, doc_type: str = "payment_plan") -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"doc_type": doc_type, "src_id": src_id, "date_id": day, "branch_code": code,
                "item_code": OUT_ITEM, "amount": amount}

    def order(src_id: str, day: int, amount: float, status: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
        return {"doc_type": "payment_order", "src_id": src_id, "date_id": day,
                "branch_code": code, "item_code": OUT_ITEM, "cp_code": cp, "bank_code": bank_b,
                "amount": amount, "status": status, "status_date_id": day}

    return {
        "dim_branch": [branch],
        "dim_bank": [
            {"code": bank_a, "bank_name": f"Тест-банк А {tag}", "account_number": f"2021{tag}1",
             "currency": "UZS"},
            {"code": bank_b, "bank_name": f"Тест-банк Б {tag}", "account_number": f"2021{tag}2",
             "currency": "UZS"},
        ],

                              "kind": "supplier", "inn": "301234567"}],
        "dim_contract": [
            {"code": ct1, "number": f"№ 1/{tag}", "contract_date": "2025-01-10", "amount": 100,
             "end_date": "2026-12-31", "kind": "payable", "branch_code": code, "cp_code": cp},
            {"code": ct2, "number": f"№ 2/{tag}", "contract_date": "2025-02-01", "amount": 50,
             "end_date": "2026-06-30", "kind": "receivable", "branch_code": code, "cp_code": cp},
        ],
        "dim_source": [{"code": src, "name": f"Тестовый источник {tag}", "kind": "own"}],
        "dim_loan": [{"code": loan, "creditor": "Тест кредитор", "project": "Тест проект",
                      "currency": "XTS", "rate": 0.04, "grace_until": "2026-01-01",
                      "maturity": "2030-12-31", "amount_uzs": 1000, "branch_code": code}],
        "fx_rate": [{"currency": "XTS", "date_id": 20260901, "rate": 1000}],
        "fact_cashflow": [
            _cash("c1", 20260810, code, 100, "in", bank_a),
            _cash("c2", 20260811, code, 40, "out", bank_b, cp_code=cp, contract_code=ct1),
            _cash("c3", 20260910, code, 30, "in", bank_a),
            _cash("c4", 20260920, code, 12, "out", bank_b),
            _cash("ly1", 20250905, code, 9, "in", bank_a),
            _cash("ly2", 20250920, code, 7, "out", bank_b),
            _cash("ly3", 20250928, code, 5, "out", bank_b),
        ],
        "fact_balance": [

            bal("ba3", 20250930, bank_a, 459), bal("ba4", 20260731, bank_a, 600),
            bal("ba5", 20260831, bank_a, 700), bal("ba6", 20260924, bank_a, 730),
            bal("bb1", 20250831, bank_b, 300), bal("bb2", 20250930, bank_b, 288),

             "overdue_days": 95},
            {"doc_type": "debt_snapshot", "src_id": "d3", "date_id": 20260831, "branch_code": code,
             "contract_code": ct2, "cp_code": cp, "receivable": 50, "payable": 0, "overdue": 10,
             "overdue_days": 20},
        ],
        "fact_target_funds": [
            {"doc_type": "target_funds", "src_id": "tf1", "date_id": 20260801, "branch_code": code,
             "source_code": src, "allocated": 10, "used": 4},
            {"doc_type": "target_funds", "src_id": "tf2", "date_id": 20260901, "branch_code": code,
             "source_code": src, "allocated": 10, "used": 6},
        ],
        "fact_loan_schedule": [
            {"doc_type": "loan_schedule", "src_id": "l1", "date_id": 20261002, "branch_code": code,
             "loan_code": loan, "principal": 0.1, "interest": 0.02, "balance": 1},
            {"doc_type": "loan_schedule", "src_id": "l2", "date_id": 20261201, "branch_code": code,
             "loan_code": loan, "principal": 0.1, "interest": 0.02, "balance": 0.9},
        ],
    }


def rebuild(run_id: int, *, publish: bool = False) -> int:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        build_id = builds.create_build(conn, run_id)
        build_all(conn, build_id, SCENARIO_PARAMS)
        builds.mark_ready(conn, build_id)
        if publish:
            builds.publish(conn, build_id, keep=3)
    return build_id


def treasury_scenario(source: str, *, publish: bool = False) -> Scenario:
    tag = new_tag()
    run_id = open_run(source)
    stage(run_id, source, scenario_rows(tag))
    merge_all(run_id, source)
    build_id = rebuild(run_id, publish=publish)
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        branch_id = int(conn.execute(text("SELECT branch_id FROM dwh.dim_branch WHERE code = :c"),
                                     {"c": f"T-{tag}"}).scalar_one())
        items = dict(conn.execute(text(
            "SELECT code, item_id FROM dwh.dim_item WHERE code IN (:i, :o)"),
            {"i": IN_ITEM, "o": OUT_ITEM}).all())
    return Scenario(source, tag, branch_id, f"T-{tag}", run_id, build_id, int(items[OUT_ITEM]),
                    int(items[IN_ITEM]))
```

```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.db import apply_rls, new_session
from app.dwh import builds
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from tests.etl_helpers import new_source, purge_source
from tests.s1_helpers import Scenario, treasury_scenario


@pytest.fixture
def scenario() -> Iterator[Scenario]:
    source = new_source()
    yield treasury_scenario(source)
    purge_source(source)


def _rows(sql: str, **params: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        return [dict(r) for r in conn.execute(text(sql), params).mappings().all()]


def test_cash_by_bank_flows_and_balances(scenario: Scenario) -> None:
    rows = _rows("SELECT month_id, bank_name, inflow, outflow, closing_balance, closing_cmp "
                 "FROM mart.agg_cash_bank_month WHERE build_id = :b AND branch_id = :br "
                 "ORDER BY month_id, bank_name", b=scenario.build_id, br=scenario.branch_id)
    by = {(r["month_id"], r["bank_name"].split()[1]): r for r in rows}
    aug_a, aug_b = by[(202608, "А")], by[(202608, "Б")]
    assert (float(aug_a["inflow"]), float(aug_a["outflow"]), float(aug_a["closing_balance"])) == \
        (100.0, 0.0, 700.0)
    assert (float(aug_b["inflow"]), float(aug_b["outflow"]), float(aug_b["closing_balance"])) == \
        (0.0, 40.0, 360.0)
    sep_ly_a, sep_ly_b = by[(202509, "А")], by[(202509, "Б")]
    # cmp_day = 24: A — 2025-09-20 dagi 455, B — 2025-08-31 dagi 300.
    assert (float(sep_ly_a["closing_balance"]), float(sep_ly_a["closing_cmp"])) == (459.0, 455.0)
    assert (float(sep_ly_b["closing_balance"]), float(sep_ly_b["closing_cmp"])) == (288.0, 300.0)
    total = _rows("SELECT sum(inflow) AS i FROM mart.agg_cash_bank_month WHERE build_id = :b "
                  "AND branch_id = 0 AND month_id = 202608", b=scenario.build_id)[0]
    assert float(total["i"]) >= 100


def test_payment_orders_by_status(scenario: Scenario) -> None:
    rows = _rows("SELECT month_id, status, doc_count, amount FROM mart.agg_payment_orders_month "
                 "WHERE build_id = :b AND branch_id = :br", b=scenario.build_id,
                 br=scenario.branch_id)
    got = {(r["month_id"], r["status"]): (r["doc_count"], float(r["amount"])) for r in rows}
    assert got == {
        (202608, "исполнено"): (2, 15.0), (202608, "отклонено"): (1, 2.0),
        (202609, "на согласовании"): (1, 8.0), (202609, "утверждено"): (1, 3.0),
    }


def test_target_funds_by_source(scenario: Scenario) -> None:
    rows = _rows("SELECT month_id, source_name, allocated, used FROM mart.agg_target_funds_month "
                 "WHERE build_id = :b AND branch_id = :br ORDER BY month_id",
                 b=scenario.build_id, br=scenario.branch_id)
    assert [(r["month_id"], float(r["allocated"]), float(r["used"])) for r in rows] == [
        (202608, 10.0, 4.0), (202609, 10.0, 6.0)]
    assert rows[0]["source_name"] == f"Тестовый источник {scenario.tag}"


def test_mart_rls_for_app_role(scenario: Scenario) -> None:
    for table in ("agg_cash_bank_month", "agg_payment_orders_month", "agg_target_funds_month"):
        with new_session() as s:
            apply_rls(s, frozenset({scenario.branch_id}))
            seen = set(s.execute(text(f"SELECT DISTINCT branch_id FROM mart.{table} "
                                      "WHERE build_id = :b"), {"b": scenario.build_id}).scalars())
        assert seen == {scenario.branch_id}, table


def test_failed_build_clears_new_tables(scenario: Scenario) -> None:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        builds.mark_failed(conn, scenario.build_id)
        for table in ("agg_cash_bank_month", "agg_payment_orders_month", "agg_target_funds_month"):
            count = conn.execute(text(f"SELECT count(*) FROM mart.{table} WHERE build_id = :b"),
                                 {"b": scenario.build_id}).scalar_one()
            assert count == 0, table
```

`backend/migrations/versions/0009_mart_treasury.py`:
```python
"""S1: banklar kesimidagi oqim/qoldiq, to'lov topshiriqlari holatlari, maqsadli mablag'lar

Revision ID: 0009
Revises: 0008
Create Date: 2026-09-25
"""

from alembic import op

revision = "0009"
down_revision = "0008"  # `alembic heads` boshqa bo'lsa — shu qiymatni joriy head'ga moslang
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None

MONEY = "numeric(20,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"
ACTIVE = "(SELECT d.active_build_id FROM core.data_version d WHERE d.id = 1)"
BUILD = "build_id bigint NOT NULL REFERENCES core.builds (build_id) ON DELETE CASCADE"
TABLES = ("agg_cash_bank_month", "agg_payment_orders_month", "agg_target_funds_month")

STATEMENTS = [
    f"""
    CREATE TABLE mart.agg_cash_bank_month (
      {BUILD},
      branch_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (branch_id >= 0),
      month_id integer NOT NULL,
      bank_name varchar(200) NOT NULL,
      inflow {MONEY},
      outflow {MONEY},
      closing_balance numeric(20,2),
      closing_cmp numeric(20,2),
      PRIMARY KEY (build_id, branch_id, month_id, bank_name)
    )
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE TABLE mart.agg_payment_orders_month (
      {BUILD},
      branch_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (branch_id >= 0),
      month_id integer NOT NULL,
      status varchar(24) NOT NULL,
      doc_count integer NOT NULL,
      amount {MONEY},
      PRIMARY KEY (build_id, branch_id, month_id, status)
    )
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE TABLE mart.agg_target_funds_month (
      {BUILD},
      branch_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (branch_id >= 0),
      month_id integer NOT NULL,
      source_id integer NOT NULL,
      source_name varchar(200) NOT NULL,
      allocated {MONEY},
      used {MONEY},
      PRIMARY KEY (build_id, branch_id, month_id, source_id)
    )
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE VIEW pub.agg_cash_bank_month WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT a.branch_id, a.month_id, a.bank_name, a.inflow, a.outflow, a.closing_balance,
           a.closing_cmp
    FROM mart.agg_cash_bank_month a WHERE a.build_id = {ACTIVE}
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE VIEW pub.agg_payment_orders_month WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT a.branch_id, a.month_id, a.status, a.doc_count, a.amount
    FROM mart.agg_payment_orders_month a WHERE a.build_id = {ACTIVE}
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE VIEW pub.agg_target_funds_month WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT a.branch_id, a.month_id, a.source_id, a.source_name, a.allocated, a.used
    FROM mart.agg_target_funds_month a WHERE a.build_id = {ACTIVE}
    """,
    # Hisob × oy bo'yicha oxirgi qoldiqni LATERAL ... LIMIT 1 bilan tez topish uchun.
    "CREATE INDEX fact_balance_bank_id_date_id_idx ON dwh.fact_balance (bank_id, date_id)",
]


def upgrade() -> None:
    for statement in STATEMENTS:
        op.execute(statement)
    for table in TABLES:
        op.execute(f"ALTER TABLE mart.{table} ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
        op.execute(f"ALTER TABLE mart.{table} FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
        op.execute(f"CREATE POLICY branch_scope ON mart.{table} USING (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id)) "
                   "WITH CHECK (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id))")
        op.execute(f"GRANT SELECT ON mart.{table} TO ugk_app")
        op.execute(f"GRANT SELECT, INSERT, DELETE ON mart.{table} TO ugk_etl")
        op.execute(f"GRANT SELECT ON pub.{table} TO ugk_app")


def downgrade() -> None:
    op.execute("DROP INDEX dwh.fact_balance_bank_id_date_id_idx")
    for table in reversed(TABLES):
        op.execute(f"DROP VIEW pub.{table}")
        op.execute(f"DROP TABLE mart.{table}")
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implementatsiya — agregatlar**

`backend/app/dwh/treasury_aggregates.py`:
```python
"""S1 g'aznachilik agregatlari (spec 5) — 2-reja freymvorkida ro'yxatdan o'tadi.

`app.dwh.aggregates` modulining oxirida import qilinadi, shuning uchun build_all tartibi:
2-reja agregatlari → shu fayldagilar (ro'yxatdan o'tish tartibida). Parametrlar
`BuildParams.bind` dan: :build_id, :from_id, :as_of_id, :cmp_day, :from_month, :as_of.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from app.dwh.aggregates import _MONTHS, Aggregate, register

NO_BANK = "Банк не указан"

_CASH_BANK = f"""
INSERT INTO mart.agg_cash_bank_month (build_id, branch_id, month_id, bank_name, inflow, outflow,
                                      closing_balance, closing_cmp)
WITH {_MONTHS},
flows AS (
  SELECT f.branch_id, d.month_id, coalesce(b.bank_name, '{NO_BANK}') AS bank_name,
         coalesce(sum(f.amount) FILTER (WHERE f.direction = 'in'), 0) AS inflow,
         coalesce(sum(f.amount) FILTER (WHERE f.direction = 'out'), 0) AS outflow
  FROM dwh.fact_cashflow f
  JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = f.date_id
  JOIN months m ON m.month_id = d.month_id
  LEFT JOIN dwh.dim_bank b ON b.bank_id = f.bank_id
  WHERE f.date_id BETWEEN :from_id AND :as_of_id
  GROUP BY f.branch_id, d.month_id, coalesce(b.bank_name, '{NO_BANK}')),
accounts AS (SELECT DISTINCT branch_id, bank_id FROM dwh.fact_balance),
bal AS (
  SELECT a.branch_id, m.month_id, bk.bank_name, sum(c.balance) AS closing,
         sum(p.balance) AS closing_cmp
  FROM accounts a
  CROSS JOIN months m
  JOIN dwh.dim_bank bk ON bk.bank_id = a.bank_id
  LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT fb.balance FROM dwh.fact_balance fb
    WHERE fb.bank_id = a.bank_id AND fb.branch_id = a.branch_id AND fb.date_id <= m.end_id
    ORDER BY fb.date_id DESC LIMIT 1) c ON true
  LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT fb.balance FROM dwh.fact_balance fb
    WHERE fb.bank_id = a.bank_id AND fb.branch_id = a.branch_id
      AND fb.date_id <= LEAST(m.end_id, m.first_id + :cmp_day - 1)
    ORDER BY fb.date_id DESC LIMIT 1) p ON true
  GROUP BY a.branch_id, m.month_id, bk.bank_name),
j AS (
  SELECT coalesce(f.branch_id, b.branch_id) AS branch_id,
         coalesce(f.month_id, b.month_id) AS month_id,
         coalesce(f.bank_name, b.bank_name) AS bank_name,
         coalesce(f.inflow, 0) AS inflow, coalesce(f.outflow, 0) AS outflow,
         b.closing, b.closing_cmp
  FROM flows f FULL JOIN bal b
    ON b.branch_id = f.branch_id AND b.month_id = f.month_id AND b.bank_name = f.bank_name
  WHERE f.branch_id IS NOT NULL OR b.closing IS NOT NULL OR b.closing_cmp IS NOT NULL)
SELECT :build_id, coalesce(j.branch_id, 0), j.month_id, j.bank_name, sum(j.inflow),
       sum(j.outflow), sum(j.closing), sum(j.closing_cmp)
FROM j
GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((j.branch_id, j.month_id, j.bank_name), (j.month_id, j.bank_name))
"""

_ORDERS = f"""
INSERT INTO mart.agg_payment_orders_month (build_id, branch_id, month_id, status, doc_count,
                                           amount)
WITH {_MONTHS}
SELECT :build_id, coalesce(o.branch_id, 0), d.month_id, o.status, count(*), sum(o.amount)
FROM dwh.fact_payment_order o
JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = o.date_id
JOIN months m ON m.month_id = d.month_id
WHERE o.date_id BETWEEN :from_id AND :as_of_id
GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((o.branch_id, d.month_id, o.status), (d.month_id, o.status))
"""

_FUNDS = f"""
INSERT INTO mart.agg_target_funds_month (build_id, branch_id, month_id, source_id, source_name,
                                         allocated, used)
WITH {_MONTHS}
SELECT :build_id, coalesce(t.branch_id, 0), d.month_id, t.source_id, s.name, sum(t.allocated),
       sum(t.used)
FROM dwh.fact_target_funds t
JOIN dwh.dim_date d ON d.date_id = t.date_id
JOIN months m ON m.month_id = d.month_id
JOIN dwh.dim_source s ON s.source_id = t.source_id
WHERE t.date_id BETWEEN :from_id AND :as_of_id
GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((t.branch_id, d.month_id, t.source_id, s.name),
                        (d.month_id, t.source_id, s.name))
"""

CASH_BANK_MONTH = register(Aggregate("agg_cash_bank_month", (_CASH_BANK,)))
PAYMENT_ORDERS_MONTH = register(Aggregate("agg_payment_orders_month", (_ORDERS,)))
TARGET_FUNDS_MONTH = register(Aggregate("agg_target_funds_month", (_FUNDS,)))
```

### Task 4: Agregatlar II — shartnomalar qarzdorligi va 14 kunlik to'lov kalendari (0010)

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/migrations/versions/0010_mart_debt_calendar.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/dwh/treasury_aggregates.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_debt_calendar_aggregates.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `_MONTHS`, `register`, `Aggregate`, `tests.s1_helpers.treasury_scenario/rebuild`, `core.settings` (`kazna.calendar_kinds`).
- Produces:
  - `mart|pub.agg_debt_contracts (branch_id > 0, month_id, contract_id, contract_number, cp_name, kind, end_date, receivable, payable, overdue, overdue_days)` — har shartnoma uchun oy ichidagi oxirgi snapshot; oyda snapshot yo'q shartnoma — yopilgan.
  - `mart|pub.agg_debt_month (branch_id, month_id, receivable, payable, overdue_receivable, overdue_payable, contracts)`.
  - `mart|pub.agg_calendar_day (branch_id, month_id, as_of_id, date_id, kind ∈ taxes|salary|loans|contracts, amount)` — as_of+1…as_of+14 kun × 4 tur × har filial (nol ham) + `branch_id = 0`.
  - `DEBT_CONTRACTS`, `DEBT_MONTH`, `CALENDAR_DAY` (ro'yxat tartibi: contracts → month → calendar); `CALENDAR_KIND_VALUES = ("taxes", "salary", "loans", "contracts")`.

```python
import json
from collections.abc import Iterator
from typing import Any

import pytest
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.etl.db import etl_transaction
from tests.etl_helpers import new_source, purge_source
from tests.s1_helpers import Scenario, rebuild, treasury_scenario


@pytest.fixture
def scenario() -> Iterator[Scenario]:
    source = new_source()
    yield treasury_scenario(source)
    purge_source(source)


def _rows(sql: str, **params: Any) -> list[dict[str, Any]]:
    with etl_transaction() as conn:
        return [dict(r) for r in conn.execute(text(sql), params).mappings().all()]


def test_debt_contracts_latest_snapshot_per_month(scenario: Scenario) -> None:
    rows = _rows("SELECT month_id, contract_number, kind, receivable, payable, overdue, "
                 "overdue_days, cp_name FROM mart.agg_debt_contracts WHERE build_id = :b "
                 "AND branch_id = :br ORDER BY month_id, contract_number",
                 b=scenario.build_id, br=scenario.branch_id)
    got = [(r["month_id"], r["kind"], float(r["receivable"]), float(r["payable"]),
            float(r["overdue"]), r["overdue_days"]) for r in rows]
    assert got == [
        (202608, "payable", 0.0, 100.0, 0.0, 0),
        (202608, "receivable", 50.0, 0.0, 10.0, 20),
        (202609, "payable", 0.0, 120.0, 60.0, 95),
    ]
    assert rows[0]["cp_name"] == f"ООО «Тест Поставщик {scenario.tag}»"


def test_debt_month_totals(scenario: Scenario) -> None:
    rows = _rows("SELECT month_id, receivable, payable, overdue_receivable, overdue_payable, "
                 "contracts FROM mart.agg_debt_month WHERE build_id = :b AND branch_id = :br "
                 "ORDER BY month_id", b=scenario.build_id, br=scenario.branch_id)
    assert [(r["month_id"], float(r["receivable"]), float(r["payable"]),
             float(r["overdue_receivable"]), float(r["overdue_payable"]), r["contracts"])
            for r in rows] == [(202608, 50.0, 100.0, 10.0, 0.0, 2), (202609, 0.0, 120.0, 0.0, 60.0, 1)]


def test_calendar_14_days_by_kind(scenario: Scenario) -> None:
    rows = _rows("SELECT date_id, kind, amount, as_of_id, month_id FROM mart.agg_calendar_day "
                 "WHERE build_id = :b AND branch_id = :br", b=scenario.build_id,
                 br=scenario.branch_id)
    assert len(rows) == 14 * 4
    assert {r["as_of_id"] for r in rows} == {20260924}
    assert {r["month_id"] for r in rows} == {202609}
    assert min(r["date_id"] for r in rows) == 20260925
    assert max(r["date_id"] for r in rows) == 20261008
    nonzero = {(r["date_id"], r["kind"]): float(r["amount"]) for r in rows if r["amount"]}
    # s3 (2026-10-09) — 15-kun, gorizontdan tashqarida; kredit: (0,1 + 0,02) × 1000.
    assert nonzero == {(20260925, "contracts"): 6.0, (20261001, "contracts"): 4.0,
                       (20261002, "loans"): 120.0}


@pytest.fixture
def taxes_kind() -> Iterator[None]:
    def put(value: object) -> None:
        with get_engine("app").begin() as conn:
            version = conn.execute(text(
                "SELECT max(version) FROM core.settings WHERE key = 'kazna.calendar_kinds'"
            )).scalar_one()
            conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.settings SET is_current = false "
                              "WHERE key = 'kazna.calendar_kinds' AND is_current"))
            conn.execute(text("INSERT INTO core.settings (key, version, value) "
                              "VALUES ('kazna.calendar_kinds', :v, CAST(:val AS jsonb))"),
                         {"v": version + 1, "val": json.dumps(value)})

    put({"taxes": ["PKM54-O09"]})
    yield
    put({"taxes": ["PKM54-O03"], "salary": ["PKM54-O05"], "loans": ["PKM54-O04"]})


def test_calendar_kind_follows_setting(scenario: Scenario, taxes_kind: None) -> None:
    build_id = rebuild(scenario.run_id)
    rows = _rows("SELECT date_id, kind, amount FROM mart.agg_calendar_day WHERE build_id = :b "
                 "AND branch_id = :br AND amount <> 0", b=build_id, br=scenario.branch_id)
    assert {(r["date_id"], r["kind"]) for r in rows} == {
        (20260925, "taxes"), (20261001, "taxes"), (20261002, "loans")}
```

```python
"""S1: shartnomalar qarzdorligi (snapshot), oylik qarzdorlik, 14 kunlik to'lov kalendari

Revision ID: 0010
Revises: 0009
Create Date: 2026-09-25
"""

from alembic import op

revision = "0010"
down_revision = "0009"
branch_labels = None
depends_on = None

MONEY = "numeric(20,2) NOT NULL DEFAULT 0"
ACTIVE = "(SELECT d.active_build_id FROM core.data_version d WHERE d.id = 1)"
BUILD = "build_id bigint NOT NULL REFERENCES core.builds (build_id) ON DELETE CASCADE"
TABLES = ("agg_debt_contracts", "agg_debt_month", "agg_calendar_day")

STATEMENTS = [
    f"""
    CREATE TABLE mart.agg_debt_contracts (
      {BUILD},
      branch_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (branch_id > 0),
      month_id integer NOT NULL,
      contract_id integer NOT NULL,
      contract_number varchar(64) NOT NULL,
      cp_name varchar(300) NOT NULL,
      kind varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('receivable', 'payable')),
      end_date date,
      receivable {MONEY},
      payable {MONEY},
      overdue {MONEY},
      overdue_days integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      PRIMARY KEY (build_id, branch_id, month_id, contract_id)
    )
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE TABLE mart.agg_debt_month (
      {BUILD},
      branch_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (branch_id >= 0),
      month_id integer NOT NULL,
      receivable {MONEY},
      payable {MONEY},
      overdue_receivable {MONEY},
      overdue_payable {MONEY},
      contracts integer NOT NULL DEFAULT 0,
      PRIMARY KEY (build_id, branch_id, month_id)
    )
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE TABLE mart.agg_calendar_day (
      {BUILD},
      branch_id integer NOT NULL CHECK (branch_id >= 0),
      month_id integer NOT NULL,
      as_of_id integer NOT NULL,
      date_id integer NOT NULL,
      kind varchar(10) NOT NULL CHECK (kind IN ('taxes', 'salary', 'loans', 'contracts')),
      amount {MONEY},
      PRIMARY KEY (build_id, branch_id, date_id, kind)
    )
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE VIEW pub.agg_debt_contracts WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT a.branch_id, a.month_id, a.contract_id, a.contract_number, a.cp_name, a.kind,
           a.end_date, a.receivable, a.payable, a.overdue, a.overdue_days
    FROM mart.agg_debt_contracts a WHERE a.build_id = {ACTIVE}
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE VIEW pub.agg_debt_month WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT a.branch_id, a.month_id, a.receivable, a.payable, a.overdue_receivable,
           a.overdue_payable, a.contracts
    FROM mart.agg_debt_month a WHERE a.build_id = {ACTIVE}
    """,
    f"""
    CREATE VIEW pub.agg_calendar_day WITH (security_invoker = true) AS
    SELECT a.branch_id, a.month_id, a.as_of_id, a.date_id, a.kind, a.amount
    FROM mart.agg_calendar_day a WHERE a.build_id = {ACTIVE}
    """,
]


def upgrade() -> None:
    for statement in STATEMENTS:
        op.execute(statement)
    for table in TABLES:
        op.execute(f"ALTER TABLE mart.{table} ENABLE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
        op.execute(f"ALTER TABLE mart.{table} FORCE ROW LEVEL SECURITY")
        op.execute(f"CREATE POLICY branch_scope ON mart.{table} USING (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id)) "
                   "WITH CHECK (core.rls_branch_ok(branch_id))")
        op.execute(f"GRANT SELECT ON mart.{table} TO ugk_app")
        op.execute(f"GRANT SELECT, INSERT, DELETE ON mart.{table} TO ugk_etl")
        op.execute(f"GRANT SELECT ON pub.{table} TO ugk_app")


def downgrade() -> None:
    for table in reversed(TABLES):
        op.execute(f"DROP VIEW pub.{table}")
        op.execute(f"DROP TABLE mart.{table}")
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — agregatlar**

`backend/app/dwh/treasury_aggregates.py` — fayl oxiriga qo'shing:
```python
CALENDAR_KIND_VALUES = ("taxes", "salary", "loans", "contracts")

# Shartnoma × oy: oy ichidagi eng oxirgi snapshot (oyda snapshot yo'q — shartnoma yopilgan).
_DEBT_CONTRACTS = f"""
INSERT INTO mart.agg_debt_contracts (build_id, branch_id, month_id, contract_id, contract_number,
                                     cp_name, kind, end_date, receivable, payable, overdue,
                                     overdue_days)
WITH {_MONTHS}
SELECT DISTINCT ON (f.contract_id, m.month_id)
       :build_id, f.branch_id, m.month_id, f.contract_id, c.number, cp.name, c.kind, c.end_date,
       f.receivable, f.payable, f.overdue, f.overdue_days
FROM dwh.fact_debt f
JOIN months m ON f.date_id BETWEEN m.first_id AND m.end_id
JOIN dwh.dim_contract c ON c.contract_id = f.contract_id
JOIN dwh.dim_counterparty cp ON cp.cp_id = c.cp_id
WHERE f.date_id BETWEEN :from_id AND :as_of_id
ORDER BY f.contract_id, m.month_id, f.date_id DESC
"""

_DEBT_MONTH = """
INSERT INTO mart.agg_debt_month (build_id, branch_id, month_id, receivable, payable,
                                 overdue_receivable, overdue_payable, contracts)
SELECT :build_id, coalesce(branch_id, 0), month_id, sum(receivable), sum(payable),
       coalesce(sum(overdue) FILTER (WHERE kind = 'receivable'), 0),
       coalesce(sum(overdue) FILTER (WHERE kind = 'payable'), 0), count(*)
FROM mart.agg_debt_contracts
WHERE build_id = :build_id AND month_id >= :from_month
GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((branch_id, month_id), (month_id))
"""

# as_of+1 … as_of+14: reja jadvali (doc_type = payment_schedule) statya turi bo'yicha + kredit
# grafigi (valyuta × as_of gacha oxirgi kurs). Filialda oyna ichida grafik bo'lsa, "loans" turi
# faqat grafikdan (ikki marta sanalmaydi). Har filial × kun × tur uchun qator (nol ham) — as_of
# har doim aniqlanadi.
_CALENDAR = """
INSERT INTO mart.agg_calendar_day (build_id, branch_id, month_id, as_of_id, date_id, kind, amount)
WITH days AS (
  SELECT date_id FROM dwh.dim_date
  WHERE date > CAST(:as_of AS date) AND date <= CAST(:as_of AS date) + 14),
kinds AS (SELECT unnest(ARRAY['taxes', 'salary', 'loans', 'contracts']) AS kind),
cfg AS (
  SELECT coalesce((SELECT value FROM core.settings
                   WHERE key = 'kazna.calendar_kinds' AND is_current), '{}'::jsonb) AS v),
item_kind AS (
  SELECT i.item_id, CASE
      WHEN i.code IN (SELECT jsonb_array_elements_text(coalesce(v->'taxes', '[]')) FROM cfg)
        THEN 'taxes'
      WHEN i.code IN (SELECT jsonb_array_elements_text(coalesce(v->'salary', '[]')) FROM cfg)
        THEN 'salary'
      WHEN i.code IN (SELECT jsonb_array_elements_text(coalesce(v->'loans', '[]')) FROM cfg)
        THEN 'loans'
      ELSE 'contracts' END AS kind
  FROM dwh.dim_item i WHERE i.direction = 'out'),
plan AS (
  SELECT p.branch_id, p.date_id, ik.kind, sum(p.amount) AS amount
  FROM dwh.fact_payment_plan p
  JOIN item_kind ik ON ik.item_id = p.item_id
  JOIN days d ON d.date_id = p.date_id
  WHERE p.doc_type = 'payment_schedule'
  GROUP BY p.branch_id, p.date_id, ik.kind),
loan AS (
  SELECT s.branch_id, s.date_id,
         sum((s.principal + s.interest)
             * CASE WHEN l.currency = 'UZS' THEN 1 ELSE fx.rate END) AS amount
  FROM dwh.fact_loan_schedule s
  JOIN days d ON d.date_id = s.date_id
  JOIN dwh.dim_loan l ON l.loan_id = s.loan_id
  LEFT JOIN LATERAL (
    SELECT r.rate FROM dwh.fx_rate r
    WHERE r.currency = l.currency AND r.date_id <= :as_of_id
    ORDER BY r.date_id DESC LIMIT 1) fx ON true
  GROUP BY s.branch_id, s.date_id),
src AS (
  SELECT branch_id, date_id, kind, amount FROM plan
  WHERE kind <> 'loans' OR branch_id NOT IN (SELECT branch_id FROM loan)
  UNION ALL
  SELECT branch_id, date_id, 'loans', amount FROM loan),
grid AS (
  SELECT b.branch_id, d.date_id, k.kind
  FROM dwh.dim_branch b CROSS JOIN days d CROSS JOIN kinds k)
SELECT :build_id, coalesce(g.branch_id, 0),
       CAST(to_char(CAST(:as_of AS date), 'YYYYMM') AS integer), :as_of_id, g.date_id, g.kind,
       coalesce(sum(s.amount), 0)
FROM grid g
LEFT JOIN src s ON s.branch_id = g.branch_id AND s.date_id = g.date_id AND s.kind = g.kind
GROUP BY GROUPING SETS ((g.branch_id, g.date_id, g.kind), (g.date_id, g.kind))
"""

DEBT_CONTRACTS = register(Aggregate("agg_debt_contracts", (_DEBT_CONTRACTS,)))
DEBT_MONTH = register(Aggregate("agg_debt_month", (_DEBT_MONTH,)))
CALENDAR_DAY = register(Aggregate("agg_calendar_day", (_CALENDAR,)))
```

```python
from collections.abc import Iterator

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.db import apply_rls, new_session
from app.core.errors import ValidationFailed
from app.sections.fmt import fmt_money, fmt_pct
from app.sections.period import resolve_period
from app.sections.queries import cash
from app.sections.scope import BranchSel
from tests.etl_helpers import TEST_AS_OF, new_source, purge_source
from tests.s1_helpers import Scenario, treasury_scenario


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def sc() -> Iterator[Scenario]:
    source = new_source()
    yield treasury_scenario(source, publish=True)
    purge_source(source)


@pytest.fixture
def db() -> Iterator[Session]:

def test_period_resolution() -> None:

    assert q3.prev_months == (202507, 202508, 202509)

    assert not resolve_period("m08", TEST_AS_OF).open_last
    with pytest.raises(ValidationFailed):
        resolve_period("m10", TEST_AS_OF)


def test_branch_sel_clauses() -> None:
    assert BranchSel(3, True).total() == ("branch_id = :sel_br", {"sel_br": 3})
    assert BranchSel(None, True).total("a.branch_id") == ("a.branch_id = 0", {})
    assert BranchSel(None, False).total() == ("branch_id <> 0", {})
    assert BranchSel(None, True).rows() == ("branch_id <> 0", {})

    assert (t.opening, t.inflow, t.outflow, t.net, t.closing, t.diff) == \
        (1000.0, 100.0, 40.0, 60.0, 1060.0, 0.0)
    assert (t.inflow_prev, t.outflow_prev, t.opening_prev, t.closing_prev) == (0.0, 0.0, None, 750.0)


def test_yoy_unclosed_month_uses_same_days(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    t = cash.cash_totals(db, _sel(sc), resolve_period("m09", TEST_AS_OF))
    assert (t.opening, t.inflow, t.outflow, t.closing) == (1060.0, 30.0, 12.0, 1078.0)
    # 2025-09-28 dagi 5 kirmaydi (24-sanadan keyin); qoldiq — 24-sentabr 2025 holati.
    assert (t.inflow_prev, t.outflow_prev, t.opening_prev, t.closing_prev) == (9.0, 7.0, 750.0, 755.0)
    assert t.diff_prev == 755.0 - (750.0 + 9.0 - 7.0)


def test_quarter_diff(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    t = cash.cash_totals(db, _sel(sc), resolve_period("q3", TEST_AS_OF))
    assert (t.opening, t.inflow, t.outflow, t.closing) == (747.0, 130.0, 52.0, 1078.0)
    assert t.diff == 1078.0 - (747.0 + 78.0)


def test_items_banks_branches_detail(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    period = resolve_period("m08", TEST_AS_OF)

    assert [(i.item_id, i.amount) for i in out] == [(sc.item_out_id, 40.0)]
    assert [(i.item_id, i.amount) for i in cash.items(db, _sel(sc), period, "in")] == [

    assert [(a.activity, a.amount) for a in split] == [
        ("centralized", 0.0), ("operating", 40.0), ("investing", 0.0)]
    banks = {b.bank_name.split()[1]: b for b in cash.banks(db, _sel(sc), period)}
    assert (banks["А"].inflow, banks["А"].closing) == (100.0, 700.0)
    assert (banks["Б"].outflow, banks["Б"].closing) == (40.0, 360.0)
    rows = cash.by_branch(db, _sel(sc), period)
    assert [(r.branch_id, r.opening, r.inflow, r.outflow, r.closing, r.diff) for r in rows] == [
        (sc.branch_id, 1000.0, 100.0, 40.0, 1060.0, 0.0)]
    detail = cash.detail(db, _sel(sc), period)
    assert [(d.direction, d.item_id, d.amount) for d in detail] == [
        ("in", sc.item_in_id, 100.0), ("out", sc.item_out_id, 40.0)]


def test_monthly_series(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    points = {p.month_id: p for p in cash.monthly(db, _sel(sc), TEST_AS_OF)}
    assert sorted(points) == [202600 + m for m in range(1, 10)]
    assert (points[202608].inflow, points[202608].outflow, points[202608].closing) == \
        (100.0, 40.0, 1060.0)
    assert (points[202609].inflow_prev, points[202609].outflow_prev) == (9.0, 7.0)


def test_item_branches_with_plan(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    info, rows = cash.item_branches(db, _sel(sc), resolve_period("m08", TEST_AS_OF),
                                    sc.item_out_id)
    assert info.direction == "out"
    assert [(r.branch_id, r.fact, r.plan) for r in rows] == [(sc.branch_id, 40.0, 50.0)]


def test_multi_branch_scope_sums_only_own_rows(sc: Scenario) -> None:
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, frozenset({sc.branch_id}))
        t = cash.cash_totals(s, BranchSel(None, all_scope=False),
                             resolve_period("m08", TEST_AS_OF))
    assert (t.inflow, t.outflow, t.opening, t.closing) == (100.0, 40.0, 1000.0, 1060.0)
```

`backend/app/sections/period.py`:
```python
"""Hisobot davri (TZ 6 «Фильтры»): `per` kodi → oylar ro'yxati. Kodlar `/meta/periods` bilan bir xil."""

from __future__ import annotations

import calendar
from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date

from app.core.errors import ValidationFailed
from app.dwh.dates import add_months, month_id, month_start
from app.meta.periods import build_periods


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ReportPeriod:
    code: str
    label: str
    date_from: date
    date_to: date
    today: date
    months: tuple[int, ...]

    @property
    def first_month(self) -> int:
        return self.months[0]

    @property
    def last_month(self) -> int:
        return self.months[-1]

    @property
    def open_last(self) -> bool:
        """Oxirgi oy yopilmagan (joriy) — o'tgan yil bilan shuncha kun bo'yicha solishtiriladi."""
        return self.last_month == month_id(self.today)

    @property
    def prev_months(self) -> tuple[int, ...]:
        return tuple(m - 100 for m in self.months)

    @property
    def month_equivalents(self) -> float:
        """Oylar soni; joriy oy o'tgan kunlar ulushi bilan (o'rtacha oylik расходование uchun)."""
        current = month_id(self.today)
        days = calendar.monthrange(self.today.year, self.today.month)[1]
        return sum(self.today.day / days if m == current else 1.0 for m in self.months)


def resolve_period(code: str, today: date) -> ReportPeriod:
    for period in build_periods(today):
        if period.code != code:
            continue
        months: list[int] = []
        cur = month_start(period.date_from)
        while cur <= period.date_to:
            months.append(month_id(cur))
            cur = add_months(cur, 1)
        return ReportPeriod(period.code, period.label, period.date_from, period.date_to, today,
                            tuple(months))
    raise ValidationFailed("validation_failed", fields=["per"])
```

`backend/app/sections/scope.py`:
```python
"""Qaysi agregat qatorlari o'qiladi (spec 6): `br` → shu filial; `scope_all` → jami (branch_id = 0);
cheklangan doira → o'z filiallari yig'indisi (RLS faqat ularni ko'rsatadi, 0-qator ko'rinmaydi)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import Any


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BranchSel:
    br: int | None
    all_scope: bool

    @classmethod
    def of(cls, br: int | None, branch_ids: frozenset[int] | None) -> BranchSel:
        return cls(br, branch_ids is None)

    def total(self, col: str = "branch_id") -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Jami ko'rsatkichlar uchun shart (yig'indi shu qatorlar bo'yicha olinadi)."""
        if self.br is not None:
            return f"{col} = :sel_br", {"sel_br": self.br}
        if self.all_scope:
            return f"{col} = 0", {}
        return f"{col} <> 0", {}

    def rows(self, col: str = "branch_id") -> tuple[str, dict[str, Any]]:
        """Filiallar kesimidagi jadvallar uchun shart (0-qator hech qachon)."""
        if self.br is not None:
            return f"{col} = :sel_br", {"sel_br": self.br}
        return f"{col} <> 0", {}
```

`backend/app/sections/fmt.py`:
```python
"""Server tomonidagi ruscha formatlar (qoidalar matni, xlsx sarlavhalari): spec 7.7 / TZ 9."""

from __future__ import annotations

NBSP = " "
MINUS = "−"


def fmt_num(value: float, digits: int = 1) -> str:
    text = f"{abs(value):,.{digits}f}".replace(",", NBSP).replace(".", ",")
    return (MINUS if value < 0 and text.strip("0, ") else "") + text


def fmt_money(mln: float, digits: int = 1) -> str:
    size = abs(mln)
    if size >= 1_000_000:
        return f"{fmt_num(mln / 1_000_000, digits)} трлн"
    if size >= 1_000:
        return f"{fmt_num(mln / 1_000, digits)} млрд"
    return f"{fmt_num(mln, digits)} млн"


def fmt_pct(ratio: float, digits: int = 1) -> str:
    return f"{fmt_num(ratio * 100, digits)}%"
```

`backend/app/sections/deps.py`:
```python
"""Bo'lim endpointlari uchun umumiy filtrlar: br, per, «bugun» (testlarda almashtiriladi)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from typing import Annotated, Any

from fastapi import Depends, Query

from app.auth.context import Ctx
from app.dwh.dates import today_tashkent
from app.sections.period import ReportPeriod, resolve_period
from app.sections.scope import BranchSel

PER_PATTERN = r"^(ytd|q[1-4]|m(0[1-9]|1[0-2]))$"


def get_today() -> date:
    return today_tashkent()


Today = Annotated[date, Depends(get_today)]


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class SectionQuery:
    br: int | None
    per: str
    today: date
    branch_ids: frozenset[int] | None

    @property
    def cache_filters(self) -> dict[str, Any]:
        # Davr sanaga bog'liq — "bugun" ham kesh kalitiga kiradi.
        return {"br": self.br, "per": self.per, "today": self.today.isoformat()}

    def period(self) -> ReportPeriod:
        return resolve_period(self.per, self.today)

    def sel(self) -> BranchSel:
        return BranchSel.of(self.br, self.branch_ids)


def section_query(
    ctx: Ctx,
    today: Today,
    br: Annotated[int | None, Query(ge=1)] = None,
    per: Annotated[str, Query(pattern=PER_PATTERN)] = "ytd",
) -> SectionQuery:
    ctx.assert_branch(br)  # doiradan tashqari → 403 scope_violation + audit (1-qavat RLS)
    resolve_period(per, today)  # joriy yilda yo'q davr → 422
    return SectionQuery(br, per, today, ctx.branch_ids)


SQ = Annotated[SectionQuery, Depends(section_query)]
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implementatsiya — pul oqimlari so'rovlari**

`backend/app/sections/queries/cash.py`:
```python
"""Pul oqimlari ko'rsatkichlari (TZ 4 «Денежные средства»/«Казначейство») — faqat pub view'lardan.

Ustun va shart fragmentlari faqat shu fayldagi konstantalar va BranchSel'dan (whitelist).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date
from decimal import Decimal
from typing import Any

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.errors import NotFoundError
from app.dwh.dates import month_id
from app.sections.period import ReportPeriod
from app.sections.scope import BranchSel

ACTIVITIES = ("centralized", "operating", "investing")
_FLOW_COL = {"in": "inflow", "out": "outflow"}


def num(value: Decimal | float | int | None) -> float:
    return 0.0 if value is None else float(value)


def opt(value: Decimal | float | int | None) -> float | None:
    return None if value is None else float(value)


def _diff(opening: float | None, net: float, closing: float | None) -> float | None:
    if opening is None or closing is None:
        return None
    return round(closing - (opening + net), 2)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CashTotals:
    opening: float | None
    inflow: float
    outflow: float
    closing: float | None
    opening_prev: float | None
    inflow_prev: float
    outflow_prev: float
    closing_prev: float | None
    has_data: bool

    @property
    def net(self) -> float:
        return round(self.inflow - self.outflow, 2)

    @property
    def diff(self) -> float | None:
        return _diff(self.opening, self.net, self.closing)

    @property
    def net_prev(self) -> float:
        return round(self.inflow_prev - self.outflow_prev, 2)

    @property
    def diff_prev(self) -> float | None:
        return _diff(self.opening_prev, self.net_prev, self.closing_prev)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class MonthPoint:
    month_id: int
    inflow: float
    outflow: float
    closing: float | None
    inflow_prev: float
    outflow_prev: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ItemAmount:
    item_id: int
    code: str
    name: str
    activity: str
    amount: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ActivityAmount:
    activity: str
    amount: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BranchCash:
    branch_id: int
    name: str
    short_name: str
    opening: float | None
    inflow: float
    outflow: float
    closing: float | None

    @property
    def net(self) -> float:
        return round(self.inflow - self.outflow, 2)

    @property
    def diff(self) -> float | None:
        return _diff(self.opening, self.net, self.closing)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BankAmount:
    bank_name: str
    inflow: float
    outflow: float
    closing: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ItemInfo:
    item_id: int
    code: str
    name: str
    activity: str
    direction: str


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ItemBranchRow:
    branch_id: int
    name: str
    fact: float
    plan: float | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DetailRow:
    branch_id: int
    branch_name: str
    direction: str
    item_id: int
    item_name: str
    activity: str
    amount: float


def _month_rows(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, months: list[int]) -> dict[int, Any]:
    where, params = sel.total()
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT month_id, sum(inflow) AS i, sum(outflow) AS o, sum(inflow_cmp) AS ic, "
        "sum(outflow_cmp) AS oc, sum(opening_balance) AS ob, sum(closing_balance) AS cb "
        f"FROM pub.agg_cash_month WHERE item_id = 0 AND {where} AND month_id = ANY(:months) "
        "GROUP BY month_id"), {**params, "months": months}).all()
    return {int(r.month_id): r for r in rows}


def _sum(by: dict[int, Any], months: tuple[int, ...] | list[int], attr: str) -> float:
    return round(sum(num(getattr(by[m], attr)) for m in months if m in by), 2)


def _closing_cmp(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, month: int) -> float | None:
    where, params = sel.total()
    return opt(db.execute(text(
        f"SELECT sum(closing_cmp) FROM pub.agg_cash_bank_month WHERE {where} AND month_id = :m"),
        {**params, "m": month}).scalar_one())


def cash_totals(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod) -> CashTotals:
    prev = period.prev_months
    by = _month_rows(db, sel, [*period.months, *prev])
    if period.open_last:
        inflow_prev = _sum(by, prev[:-1], "i") + _sum(by, prev[-1:], "ic")
        outflow_prev = _sum(by, prev[:-1], "o") + _sum(by, prev[-1:], "oc")
        closing_prev = _closing_cmp(db, sel, prev[-1])
    else:
        inflow_prev = _sum(by, prev, "i")
        outflow_prev = _sum(by, prev, "o")
        closing_prev = opt(by[prev[-1]].cb) if prev[-1] in by else None
    first, last = period.first_month, period.last_month
    return CashTotals(
        opening=opt(by[first].ob) if first in by else None,
        inflow=_sum(by, period.months, "i"),
        outflow=_sum(by, period.months, "o"),
        closing=opt(by[last].cb) if last in by else None,
        opening_prev=opt(by[prev[0]].ob) if prev[0] in by else None,
        inflow_prev=round(inflow_prev, 2),
        outflow_prev=round(outflow_prev, 2),
        closing_prev=closing_prev,
        has_data=any(m in by for m in period.months),
    )


def monthly(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, today: date) -> list[MonthPoint]:
    """Joriy yilning yanvaridan joriy oygacha + o'tgan yilning o'sha oylari (joriy oy — shuncha kun)."""
    current = month_id(today)
    months = [today.year * 100 + m for m in range(1, today.month + 1)]
    by = _month_rows(db, sel, months + [m - 100 for m in months])
    out: list[MonthPoint] = []
    for m in months:
        prev = by.get(m - 100)
        cur = by.get(m)
        cmp = m == current
        out.append(MonthPoint(
            month_id=m,
            inflow=num(cur.i) if cur else 0.0,
            outflow=num(cur.o) if cur else 0.0,
            closing=opt(cur.cb) if cur else None,
            inflow_prev=num((prev.ic if cmp else prev.i)) if prev else 0.0,
            outflow_prev=num((prev.oc if cmp else prev.o)) if prev else 0.0,
        ))
    return out


def items(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod, direction: str) -> list[ItemAmount]:
    col = _FLOW_COL[direction]
    where, params = sel.total("a.branch_id")
    rows = db.execute(text(
        f"SELECT a.item_id, i.code, i.name, i.activity, sum(a.{col}) AS amount "
        "FROM pub.agg_cash_month a JOIN pub.items i ON i.item_id = a.item_id "
        f"WHERE a.item_id <> 0 AND i.direction = :dir AND {where} AND a.month_id = ANY(:months) "
        f"GROUP BY a.item_id, i.code, i.name, i.activity HAVING sum(a.{col}) > 0 "
        "ORDER BY amount DESC, i.name"),
        {**params, "dir": direction, "months": list(period.months)}).all()
    return [ItemAmount(int(r.item_id), r.code, r.name, r.activity, num(r.amount)) for r in rows]


def activity_split(items_out: list[ItemAmount]) -> list[ActivityAmount]:
    totals = dict.fromkeys(ACTIVITIES, 0.0)
    for item in items_out:
        totals[item.activity] = round(totals[item.activity] + item.amount, 2)
    return [ActivityAmount(a, totals[a]) for a in ACTIVITIES]


def by_branch(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod) -> list[BranchCash]:
    where, params = sel.rows("a.branch_id")
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT a.branch_id, b.name, b.short_name, sum(a.inflow) AS i, sum(a.outflow) AS o, "
        "sum(a.opening_balance) FILTER (WHERE a.month_id = :first) AS ob, "
        "sum(a.closing_balance) FILTER (WHERE a.month_id = :last) AS cb "
        "FROM pub.agg_cash_month a JOIN pub.branches b ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id "
        f"WHERE a.item_id = 0 AND {where} AND a.month_id = ANY(:months) "
        "GROUP BY a.branch_id, b.name, b.short_name, b.sort_order "
        "ORDER BY b.sort_order, a.branch_id"),
        {**params, "months": list(period.months), "first": period.first_month,
         "last": period.last_month}).all()
    return [BranchCash(int(r.branch_id), r.name, r.short_name, opt(r.ob), num(r.i), num(r.o),
                       opt(r.cb)) for r in rows]


def banks(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod) -> list[BankAmount]:
    where, params = sel.total()
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT bank_name, sum(inflow) AS i, sum(outflow) AS o, "
        "sum(closing_balance) FILTER (WHERE month_id = :last) AS cb "
        f"FROM pub.agg_cash_bank_month WHERE {where} AND month_id = ANY(:months) "
        "GROUP BY bank_name ORDER BY bank_name"),
        {**params, "months": list(period.months), "last": period.last_month}).all()
    return [BankAmount(r.bank_name, num(r.i), num(r.o), opt(r.cb)) for r in rows]


def item_info(db: Session, item_id: int) -> ItemInfo:
    row = db.execute(text("SELECT item_id, code, name, activity, direction FROM pub.items "
                          "WHERE item_id = :i"), {"i": item_id}).one_or_none()
    if row is None:
        raise NotFoundError()
    return ItemInfo(int(row.item_id), row.code, row.name, row.activity, row.direction)


def item_branches(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod,
                  item_id: int) -> tuple[ItemInfo, list[ItemBranchRow]]:
    info = item_info(db, item_id)
    col = _FLOW_COL[info.direction]
    where, params = sel.rows("a.branch_id")
    bind = {**params, "i": item_id, "months": list(period.months)}
    facts = {int(r.branch_id): (r.name, num(r.v)) for r in db.execute(text(
        f"SELECT a.branch_id, b.name, sum(a.{col}) AS v FROM pub.agg_cash_month a "
        "JOIN pub.branches b ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id "
        f"WHERE a.item_id = :i AND {where} AND a.month_id = ANY(:months) "
        "GROUP BY a.branch_id, b.name"), bind).all()}
    plans: dict[int, tuple[str, float]] = {}
    if info.direction == "out":
        plans = {int(r.branch_id): (r.name, num(r.v)) for r in db.execute(text(
            "SELECT a.branch_id, b.name, sum(a.plan_amount) AS v FROM pub.agg_payments_month a "
            "JOIN pub.branches b ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id "
            f"WHERE a.item_id = :i AND {where} AND a.month_id = ANY(:months) "
            "GROUP BY a.branch_id, b.name"), bind).all()}
    rows: list[ItemBranchRow] = []
    for branch_id in set(facts) | set(plans):
        name = (facts.get(branch_id) or plans[branch_id])[0]
        fact = facts[branch_id][1] if branch_id in facts else 0.0
        plan = plans[branch_id][1] if branch_id in plans else (0.0 if plans else None)
        if info.direction == "in":
            plan = None
        if fact > 0 or (plan or 0) > 0:
            rows.append(ItemBranchRow(branch_id, name, fact, plan))
    rows.sort(key=lambda r: (-r.fact, r.name))
    return info, rows


def detail(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod) -> list[DetailRow]:
    where, params = sel.rows("a.branch_id")
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT a.branch_id, b.name AS branch_name, i.direction, i.item_id, i.name AS item_name, "
        "i.activity, sum(CASE WHEN i.direction = 'in' THEN a.inflow ELSE a.outflow END) AS amount "
        "FROM pub.agg_cash_month a JOIN pub.items i ON i.item_id = a.item_id "
        "JOIN pub.branches b ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id "
        f"WHERE a.item_id <> 0 AND {where} AND a.month_id = ANY(:months) "
        "GROUP BY a.branch_id, b.name, b.sort_order, i.direction, i.item_id, i.name, i.activity "
        "HAVING sum(CASE WHEN i.direction = 'in' THEN a.inflow ELSE a.outflow END) > 0 "
        "ORDER BY b.sort_order, a.branch_id, i.direction, amount DESC"),
        {**params, "months": list(period.months)}).all()
    return [DetailRow(int(r.branch_id), r.branch_name, r.direction, int(r.item_id), r.item_name,
                      r.activity, num(r.amount)) for r in rows]
```
