```python
from collections.abc import Iterator
from datetime import date

import pytest
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.core.db import apply_rls, new_session
from app.sections.period import resolve_period
from app.sections.queries import calendar, debt, funds, payments
from app.sections.scope import BranchSel
from tests.etl_helpers import TEST_AS_OF, new_source, purge_source
from tests.s1_helpers import Scenario, treasury_scenario


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def sc() -> Iterator[Scenario]:
    source = new_source()
    yield treasury_scenario(source, publish=True)
    purge_source(source)


@pytest.fixture
def db() -> Iterator[Session]:
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, None)
        yield s


def _sel(sc: Scenario) -> BranchSel:
    return BranchSel(sc.branch_id, all_scope=True)


def test_plan_fact(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    rows = payments.plan_fact(db, _sel(sc), resolve_period("m08", TEST_AS_OF))
    assert [(r.item_id, r.plan, r.fact, r.execution, r.deviation) for r in rows] == [
        (sc.item_out_id, 50.0, 40.0, 0.8, 10.0)]
    sep = payments.plan_fact(db, _sel(sc), resolve_period("m09", TEST_AS_OF))
    assert (sep[0].plan, sep[0].fact) == (30.0, 12.0)  # jadval qatorlari (6+4+3) rejaga kirmaydi


def test_orders_and_requests(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    statuses = payments.orders_by_status(db, _sel(sc), resolve_period("m08", TEST_AS_OF))
    assert [s.status for s in statuses] == list(payments.PO_STATUSES)
    got = {s.status: (s.count, s.amount) for s in statuses}
    assert got["исполнено"] == (2, 15.0) and got["отклонено"] == (1, 2.0)
    assert got["черновик"] == (0, 0.0)
    assert payments.requests_total(db, _sel(sc)) == (2, 11.0)
    assert [(r.branch_id, r.count, r.amount) for r in payments.requests_by_branch(db, _sel(sc))] \
        == [(sc.branch_id, 2, 11.0)]


def test_debt(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    summary = debt.debt_summary(db, _sel(sc), 202609)
    assert summary is not None
    assert (summary.receivable, summary.payable, summary.overdue_payable, summary.contracts) == \
        (0.0, 120.0, 60.0, 1)
    assert debt.debt_summary(db, _sel(sc), 202607) is None
    aug = debt.contracts(db, _sel(sc), 202608)
    assert [(c.kind, c.amount, c.overdue_days) for c in aug] == [
        ("receivable", 50.0, 20), ("payable", 100.0, 0)]
    assert aug[1].end_date == date(2026, 12, 31)
    assert [c.overdue_days for c in debt.overdue_contracts(db, _sel(sc), 202609, 90)] == [95]
    assert debt.overdue_contracts(db, _sel(sc), 202609, 95) == []
    assert [(r.branch_id, r.payable, r.overdue_payable) for r in
            debt.debt_by_branch(db, _sel(sc), 202609)] == [(sc.branch_id, 120.0, 60.0)]


def test_funds(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    period = resolve_period("q3", TEST_AS_OF)
    sources = funds.funds_by_source(db, _sel(sc), period)
    assert [(f.allocated, f.used, f.share) for f in sources] == [(20.0, 10.0, 0.5)]
    assert [(f.key, f.used) for f in funds.funds_by_branch(db, _sel(sc), period)] == [
        (sc.branch_id, 10.0)]


def test_calendar_and_balances(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    block = calendar.calendar(db, _sel(sc))
    assert block.as_of == TEST_AS_OF and block.start == date(2026, 9, 25)
    assert len(block.days) == 14 and block.total == 130.0
    assert (block.days[0].contracts, block.days[7].loans) == (6.0, 120.0)
    assert block.balance == 1078.0
    assert block.coverage is not None and abs(block.coverage - 1078 / 130) < 1e-9
    balances = calendar.bank_balances(db, _sel(sc), 202608)
    assert [(b.bank_name.split()[1], b.balance) for b in balances] == [("А", 700.0), ("Б", 360.0)]
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementatsiya — reja-fakt va topshiriqlar**

`backend/app/sections/queries/payments.py`:
```python
"""Исполнение плана платежей, отклонение по статье, платёжные поручения, заявки в работе (TZ 4)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.sections.period import ReportPeriod
from app.sections.queries.cash import num
from app.sections.scope import BranchSel

PO_STATUSES = ("черновик", "на согласовании", "утверждено", "отправлено в банк", "исполнено",
               "отклонено")
IN_WORK = ("на согласовании", "утверждено")


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class PlanFactRow:
    item_id: int
    name: str
    activity: str
    plan: float
    fact: float

    @property
    def execution(self) -> float | None:
        return round(self.fact / self.plan, 6) if self.plan else None

    @property
    def deviation(self) -> float:
        """TZ 4: план статьи − факт статьи."""
        return round(self.plan - self.fact, 2)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class StatusRow:
    status: str
    count: int
    amount: float


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RequestsRow:
    branch_id: int
    name: str
    count: int
    amount: float


def plan_fact(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod) -> list[PlanFactRow]:
    where, params = sel.total("a.branch_id")
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT a.item_id, i.name, i.activity, sum(a.plan_amount) AS p, sum(a.fact_amount) AS f "
        "FROM pub.agg_payments_month a JOIN pub.items i ON i.item_id = a.item_id "
        f"WHERE {where} AND a.month_id = ANY(:months) "
        "GROUP BY a.item_id, i.name, i.activity "
        "HAVING sum(a.plan_amount) > 0 OR sum(a.fact_amount) > 0 ORDER BY p DESC, i.name"),
        {**params, "months": list(period.months)}).all()
    return [PlanFactRow(int(r.item_id), r.name, r.activity, num(r.p), num(r.f)) for r in rows]


def orders_by_status(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod) -> list[StatusRow]:
    where, params = sel.total()
    got = {r.status: (int(r.n), num(r.s)) for r in db.execute(text(
        "SELECT status, sum(doc_count) AS n, sum(amount) AS s FROM pub.agg_payment_orders_month "
        f"WHERE {where} AND month_id = ANY(:months) GROUP BY status"),
        {**params, "months": list(period.months)}).all()}
    return [StatusRow(s, *got.get(s, (0, 0.0))) for s in PO_STATUSES]


def requests_total(db: Session, sel: BranchSel) -> tuple[int, float]:
    """Joriy holat: davrdan qat'i nazar «на согласовании» + «утверждено» (spec 4)."""
    where, params = sel.total()
    row = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT coalesce(sum(doc_count), 0) AS n, coalesce(sum(amount), 0) AS s "
        f"FROM pub.agg_payment_orders_month WHERE {where} AND status = ANY(:st)"),
        {**params, "st": list(IN_WORK)}).one()
    return int(row.n), num(row.s)


def requests_by_branch(db: Session, sel: BranchSel) -> list[RequestsRow]:
    where, params = sel.rows("a.branch_id")
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT a.branch_id, b.name, sum(a.doc_count) AS n, sum(a.amount) AS s "
        "FROM pub.agg_payment_orders_month a JOIN pub.branches b ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id "
        f"WHERE {where} AND a.status = ANY(:st) GROUP BY a.branch_id, b.name "
        "ORDER BY s DESC, b.name"), {**params, "st": list(IN_WORK)}).all()
    return [RequestsRow(int(r.branch_id), r.name, int(r.n), num(r.s)) for r in rows]
```

`backend/app/sections/queries/debt.py`:
```python
"""Дебиторская/кредиторская/просроченная задолженность (TZ 4) — davr oxiridagi oy holati."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass
from datetime import date

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.sections.queries.cash import num
from app.sections.scope import BranchSel

_CONTRACT_COLS = (
    "c.contract_id, c.contract_number, c.cp_name, c.branch_id, b.name AS branch_name, c.kind, "
    "c.receivable + c.payable AS amount, c.overdue, c.overdue_days, c.end_date"
)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DebtSummary:
    receivable: float
    payable: float
    overdue_receivable: float
    overdue_payable: float
    contracts: int


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class ContractRow:
    contract_id: int
    number: str
    cp_name: str
    branch_id: int
    branch_name: str
    kind: str
    amount: float
    overdue: float
    overdue_days: int
    end_date: date | None


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class DebtBranchRow:
    branch_id: int
    name: str
    receivable: float
    payable: float
    overdue_payable: float


def debt_summary(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, month: int) -> DebtSummary | None:
    where, params = sel.total()
    row = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT count(*) AS n, sum(receivable) AS r, sum(payable) AS p, "
        "sum(overdue_receivable) AS orr, sum(overdue_payable) AS op, sum(contracts) AS c "
        f"FROM pub.agg_debt_month WHERE {where} AND month_id = :m"), {**params, "m": month}).one()
    if not row.n:
        return None
    return DebtSummary(num(row.r), num(row.p), num(row.orr), num(row.op), int(row.c or 0))


def _contracts(db: Session, sql_tail: str, params: dict[str, object]) -> list[ContractRow]:
    rows = db.execute(text(
        f"SELECT {_CONTRACT_COLS} FROM pub.agg_debt_contracts c "
        f"JOIN pub.branches b ON b.branch_id = c.branch_id {sql_tail}"), params).all()
    return [ContractRow(int(r.contract_id), r.contract_number, r.cp_name, int(r.branch_id),
                        r.branch_name, r.kind, num(r.amount), num(r.overdue), int(r.overdue_days),
                        r.end_date) for r in rows]


def contracts(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, month: int, limit: int = 500) -> list[ContractRow]:
    """TZ 5: muddati o'tganlar birinchi (kunlar kamayishi bo'yicha), so'ng summa bo'yicha."""
    where, params = sel.rows("c.branch_id")
    return _contracts(db, f"WHERE {where} AND c.month_id = :m "
                          "ORDER BY (c.overdue_days > 0) DESC, c.overdue_days DESC, amount DESC, "
                          "c.contract_id LIMIT :lim", {**params, "m": month, "lim": limit})


def overdue_contracts(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, month: int,
                      min_days: float) -> list[ContractRow]:
    where, params = sel.rows("c.branch_id")
    return _contracts(db, f"WHERE {where} AND c.month_id = :m AND c.overdue_days > :d "
                          "ORDER BY c.overdue DESC, c.contract_id",
                      {**params, "m": month, "d": min_days})


def debt_by_branch(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, month: int) -> list[DebtBranchRow]:
    where, params = sel.rows("a.branch_id")
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT a.branch_id, b.name, a.receivable, a.payable, a.overdue_payable "
        "FROM pub.agg_debt_month a JOIN pub.branches b ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id "
        f"WHERE {where} AND a.month_id = :m ORDER BY a.payable DESC, b.name"),
        {**params, "m": month}).all()
    return [DebtBranchRow(int(r.branch_id), r.name, num(r.receivable), num(r.payable),
                          num(r.overdue_payable)) for r in rows]
```

`backend/app/sections/queries/funds.py`:
```python
"""Целевое использование средств: использовано / выделено (TZ 4)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.sections.period import ReportPeriod
from app.sections.queries.cash import num
from app.sections.scope import BranchSel


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class FundRow:
    key: int
    name: str
    allocated: float
    used: float

    @property
    def share(self) -> float | None:
        return round(self.used / self.allocated, 6) if self.allocated else None


def funds_by_source(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod) -> list[FundRow]:
    where, params = sel.total()
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT source_id AS k, source_name AS n, sum(allocated) AS a, sum(used) AS u "
        f"FROM pub.agg_target_funds_month WHERE {where} AND month_id = ANY(:months) "
        "GROUP BY source_id, source_name ORDER BY a DESC, n"),
        {**params, "months": list(period.months)}).all()
    return [FundRow(int(r.k), r.n, num(r.a), num(r.u)) for r in rows]


def funds_by_branch(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, period: ReportPeriod) -> list[FundRow]:
    where, params = sel.rows("a.branch_id")
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT a.branch_id AS k, b.name AS n, sum(a.allocated) AS al, sum(a.used) AS u "
        "FROM pub.agg_target_funds_month a JOIN pub.branches b ON b.branch_id = a.branch_id "
        f"WHERE {where} AND a.month_id = ANY(:months) GROUP BY a.branch_id, b.name "
        "ORDER BY u DESC, n"), {**params, "months": list(period.months)}).all()
    return [FundRow(int(r.k), r.n, num(r.al), num(r.u)) for r in rows]
```

`backend/app/sections/queries/calendar.py`:
```python
"""Платёжный календарь (14 kun × 4 tur), покрытие платежей остатком, остатки по банкам (TZ 4)."""

from __future__ import annotations

from dataclasses import dataclass, field
from datetime import date, timedelta

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.dwh.dates import from_date_id, month_id
from app.sections.queries.cash import num, opt
from app.sections.scope import BranchSel


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CalendarDayRow:
    day: date
    taxes: float = 0.0
    salary: float = 0.0
    loans: float = 0.0
    contracts: float = 0.0

    @property
    def total(self) -> float:
        return round(self.taxes + self.salary + self.loans + self.contracts, 2)


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class CalendarBlock:
    as_of: date | None
    days: list[CalendarDayRow] = field(default_factory=list)
    total: float = 0.0
    balance: float | None = None

    @property
    def start(self) -> date | None:
        return None if self.as_of is None else self.as_of + timedelta(days=1)

    @property
    def coverage(self) -> float | None:
        """Остаток на счетах / сумма платежей календаря."""
        if self.balance is None or not self.total:
            return None
        return self.balance / self.total


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class BankBalance:
    bank_name: str
    balance: float


def current_balance(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, month: int) -> float | None:
    where, params = sel.total()
    return opt(db.execute(text(
        f"SELECT sum(closing_balance) FROM pub.agg_cash_month WHERE item_id = 0 AND {where} "
        "AND month_id = :m"), {**params, "m": month}).scalar_one())


def calendar(db: Session, sel: BranchSel) -> CalendarBlock:
    where, params = sel.total()
    as_of_id = db.execute(text(f"SELECT max(as_of_id) FROM pub.agg_calendar_day WHERE {where}"),
                          params).scalar_one()
    if as_of_id is None:
        return CalendarBlock(None)
    as_of = from_date_id(int(as_of_id))
    amounts: dict[int, dict[str, float]] = {}
    for r in db.execute(text(
            "SELECT date_id, kind, sum(amount) AS v FROM pub.agg_calendar_day "
            f"WHERE {where} GROUP BY date_id, kind"), params).all():
        amounts.setdefault(int(r.date_id), {})[r.kind] = num(r.v)
    days = [CalendarDayRow(from_date_id(d), **amounts[d]) for d in sorted(amounts)]
    total = round(sum(d.total for d in days), 2)
    return CalendarBlock(as_of, days, total, current_balance(db, sel, month_id(as_of)))


def bank_balances(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, month: int) -> list[BankBalance]:
    where, params = sel.total()
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT bank_name, sum(closing_balance) AS v FROM pub.agg_cash_bank_month "
        f"WHERE {where} AND month_id = :m AND closing_balance IS NOT NULL "
        "GROUP BY bank_name ORDER BY v DESC, bank_name"), {**params, "m": month}).all()
    return [BankBalance(r.bank_name, num(r.v)) for r in rows]
```

**Files:**

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `SQ`/`SectionQuery`, `get_today`, `Cache`, `get_db`, `require_perm`, `section_perm`, `Section`, `queries.cash.*`, `queries.debt.debt_by_branch`, `published_synthetic`, `tests.helpers.login`, `make_user`, `tests.etl_helpers.*`.
- Produces:
  - `queries.cash.item_months(db, sel, months, direction) -> dict[int, dict[int, float]]` (item_id → month_id → summa).
  - `app.sections.schemas` (OpenAPI komponent nomlari noyob): `ReportPeriodOut(code, label, date_from, date_to, open_last)`, `CashKpisOut(opening, inflow, outflow, net, closing, diff, opening_prev, inflow_prev, outflow_prev, net_prev, closing_prev, diff_prev, has_data)`, `MonthPointOut(month_id, inflow, outflow, closing, inflow_prev, outflow_prev)`, `ItemAmountOut(item_id, code, name, activity, amount)`, `ActivityAmountOut(activity, amount)`, `BranchCashOut(branch_id, name, short_name, opening, inflow, outflow, net, closing, diff)`, `BranchAmountOut(branch_id, name, amount)`, `BankAmountOut(bank_name, amount, closing)`, `StationOut(branch_id, code, name, short_name, kind, capacity_mw, lat, lon, inflow, outflow, closing, receivable, payable, overdue_payable, production: dict[str, float] | None)`, `SeriesOut(key, name, values)`, `DetailRowOut(branch_id, branch_name, direction, item_id, item_name, activity, amount)`, `ItemInfoOut(item_id, code, name, activity, direction)`, `ItemBranchRowOut(branch_id, name, fact, plan)`, `ObzorSvodOut(period, kpis, months, activity, branches, items_in, items_out, stations)`, `ObzorFlowOut(period, kpis, direction, months, series, items, activity, banks, branches)`, `ObzorExcelOut(period, kpis, branches, total, detail)`, `ItemBranchesOut(period, item, total, rows)`; konvertorlar `period_out`, `kpis_out`, `branch_cash_out`, `items_out`.
  - `app.sections.obzor`: `router` (`/obzor`, router darajasida `require_perm("section:obzor:view")`), `build_svod(db, q)`, `build_flow(db, q, direction)`, `build_excel(db, q)`, `build_item(db, q, item_id)`, `stations(db, q, period)`. HTTP: `GET /obzor/svod`, `GET /obzor/postup`, `GET /obzor/rasxod`, `GET /obzor/excel`, `GET /obzor/items/{item_id}`.

`backend/tests/test_obzor_api.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator

import pytest
import redis
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.db import apply_rls, new_session
from app.main import create_app
from app.sections.deps import get_today
from tests.etl_helpers import (
    TEST_AS_OF,
    branch_row,
    merge_all,
    new_source,
    new_tag,
    open_run,
    purge_source,
    stage,
)
from tests.helpers import SeedUser, login
from tests.s1_helpers import rebuild

NEW_VIEWS = ("agg_cash_bank_month", "agg_payment_orders_month", "agg_target_funds_month",
             "agg_debt_contracts", "agg_debt_month", "agg_calendar_day")


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clean_cache() -> None:
    r = redis.Redis.from_url(get_settings().redis_url)
    for key in r.scan_iter("ugk:*"):
        r.delete(key)


@pytest.fixture
def app_factory() -> Iterator[Callable[[], TestClient]]:
    clients: list[TestClient] = []

    def make() -> TestClient:
        app = create_app()
        app.dependency_overrides[get_today] = lambda: TEST_AS_OF
        client = TestClient(app, base_url="https://testserver")
        client.__enter__()
        clients.append(client)
        return client

    yield make
    for client in clients:
        client.__exit__(None, None, None)


def _as(app_factory: Callable[[], TestClient], user: SeedUser) -> TestClient:
    return login(app_factory(), user)


def test_svod_shape_and_etag(app_factory: Callable[[], TestClient],
                             make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser], published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    client = _as(app_factory, make_user("rukovodstvo"))
    r = client.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"per": "m08"})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text
    body = r.json()
    assert body["period"]["code"] == "m08" and body["period"]["open_last"] is False
    assert body["kpis"]["inflow"] > 0 and body["kpis"]["has_data"] is True
    assert body["kpis"]["closing"] is not None
    assert [m["month_id"] for m in body["months"]] == [202600 + m for m in range(1, 10)]
    assert [a["activity"] for a in body["activity"]] == ["centralized", "operating", "investing"]
    assert {s["branch_id"] for s in body["stations"]} >= set(range(1, 17))
    assert all(s["production"] is None for s in body["stations"])
    assert body["items_out"] and body["items_in"]
    again = client.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"per": "m08"},
                       headers={"If-None-Match": r.headers["ETag"]})
    assert again.status_code == 304


def test_branch_user_cannot_request_other_branch(app_factory: Callable[[], TestClient],
                                                 make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser],
                                                 published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    client = _as(app_factory, make_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3,)))
    for path in ("svod", "postup", "rasxod", "excel"):
        r = client.get(f"/api/v1/obzor/{path}", params={"br": 4})
        assert r.status_code == 403 and r.json()["error"]["code"] == "scope_violation", path


def test_branch_user_sees_only_own_data(app_factory: Callable[[], TestClient],
                                        make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser],
                                        published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    econ = _as(app_factory, make_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3,)))
    boss = _as(app_factory, make_user("rukovodstvo"))
    mine = econ.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod").json()
    assert {b["branch_id"] for b in mine["branches"]} == {3}
    assert [s["branch_id"] for s in mine["stations"]] == [3]
    assert mine["kpis"] == boss.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"br": 3}).json()["kpis"]
    detail = econ.get("/api/v1/obzor/excel").json()["detail"]
    assert detail and {d["branch_id"] for d in detail} == {3}


def test_limited_scope_without_br_sees_own_totals(app_factory: Callable[[], TestClient],
                                                  make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser],
                                                  published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    econ = _as(app_factory, make_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3, 4)))
    boss = _as(app_factory, make_user("rukovodstvo"))
    both = econ.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"per": "q2"}).json()["kpis"]
    k3 = boss.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"per": "q2", "br": 3}).json()["kpis"]
    k4 = boss.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"per": "q2", "br": 4}).json()["kpis"]
    everyone = boss.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"per": "q2"}).json()["kpis"]
    assert both["inflow"] == pytest.approx(k3["inflow"] + k4["inflow"], abs=0.01)
    assert both["inflow"] < everyone["inflow"]


def test_invalid_period_is_422(app_factory: Callable[[], TestClient],
                               make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser]) -> None:
    client = _as(app_factory, make_user("rukovodstvo"))
    for per in ("m12", "bad", "q5"):
        assert client.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"per": per}).status_code == 422, per


def test_flow_excel_and_item_pages(app_factory: Callable[[], TestClient],
                                   make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser],
                                   published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    client = _as(app_factory, make_user("rukovodstvo"))
    postup = client.get("/api/v1/obzor/postup", params={"per": "q3"}).json()
    assert postup["direction"] == "in" and 1 <= len(postup["series"]) <= 4
    assert all(len(s["values"]) == len(postup["months"]) == 9 for s in postup["series"])
    assert postup["banks"] and postup["branches"] and postup["activity"] == []
    rasxod = client.get("/api/v1/obzor/rasxod", params={"per": "q3"}).json()
    assert [s["key"] for s in rasxod["series"]] == ["centralized", "operating", "investing"]
    assert len(rasxod["activity"]) == 3
    excel = client.get("/api/v1/obzor/excel", params={"per": "m08"}).json()
    assert excel["total"]["inflow"] == pytest.approx(excel["kpis"]["inflow"], abs=0.01)
    assert excel["total"]["branch_id"] == 0
    assert sum(b["inflow"] for b in excel["branches"]) == pytest.approx(excel["total"]["inflow"],
                                                                         abs=0.05)
    item_id = client.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"per": "m08"}).json()["items_out"][0][
        "item_id"]
    item = client.get(f"/api/v1/obzor/items/{item_id}", params={"per": "m08"}).json()
    assert item["item"]["direction"] == "out" and item["rows"]
    assert any(row["plan"] for row in item["rows"])
    assert client.get("/api/v1/obzor/items/999999").status_code == 404


def test_svod_without_balances_returns_nulls(app_factory: Callable[[], TestClient],
                                             make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser]) -> None:
    source = new_source()
    try:
        branch = branch_row(new_tag())
        run = open_run(source)
        stage(run, source, {"dim_branch": [branch], "fact_cashflow": [
            {"doc_type": "bank_entry", "src_id": "x1", "date_id": 20260805,
             "branch_code": branch["code"], "item_code": "PKM54-O09", "direction": "out",
             "amount": 5}]})
        merge_all(run, source)
        rebuild(run, publish=True)
        with new_session() as s:
            apply_rls(s, None)
            branch_id = s.execute(text("SELECT branch_id FROM pub.branches WHERE code = :c"),
                                  {"c": branch["code"]}).scalar_one()
        client = _as(app_factory, make_user("rukovodstvo"))
        kpis = client.get("/api/v1/obzor/svod", params={"per": "m08", "br": branch_id}).json()[
            "kpis"]
        assert (kpis["outflow"], kpis["opening"], kpis["closing"], kpis["diff"]) == \
            (5.0, None, None, None)
    finally:
        purge_source(source)


def test_new_pub_views_rls_direct_sql(published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    for view in NEW_VIEWS:
        with new_session() as s:
            apply_rls(s, frozenset({3}))
            seen = set(s.execute(text(f"SELECT DISTINCT branch_id FROM pub.{view}")).scalars())
        assert seen <= {3}, view
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, frozenset({3}))
        assert s.execute(text("SELECT count(*) FROM pub.agg_calendar_day")).scalar_one() == 56
    with new_session() as s:
        for view in NEW_VIEWS:
            assert s.execute(text(f"SELECT count(*) FROM pub.{view}")).scalar_one() == 0, view
```

`backend/app/sections/queries/cash.py` — fayl oxiriga qo'shing:
```python
def item_months(db: Session, sel: BranchSel, months: list[int],
                direction: str) -> dict[int, dict[int, float]]:
    """Statya × oy summalari (Поступления/Расходование oylik grafiklari uchun)."""
    col = _FLOW_COL[direction]
    where, params = sel.total("a.branch_id")
    out: dict[int, dict[int, float]] = {}
    for r in db.execute(text(
            f"SELECT a.item_id, a.month_id, sum(a.{col}) AS v FROM pub.agg_cash_month a "
            "JOIN pub.items i ON i.item_id = a.item_id "
            f"WHERE a.item_id <> 0 AND i.direction = :dir AND {where} AND a.month_id = ANY(:m) "
            "GROUP BY a.item_id, a.month_id"),
            {**params, "dir": direction, "m": months}).all():
        out.setdefault(int(r.item_id), {})[int(r.month_id)] = num(r.v)
    return out
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Javob sxemalari**

`backend/app/sections/schemas.py`:
```python
"""Bo'lim API javoblari (OpenAPI → frontend tiplari). Summalar mln so'm; None — ma'lumot yo'q."""

from __future__ import annotations

from datetime import date

from pydantic import BaseModel, ConfigDict

from app.sections.period import ReportPeriod
from app.sections.queries.cash import BranchCash, CashTotals, ItemAmount


class _Out(BaseModel):
    model_config = ConfigDict(extra="forbid")


class ReportPeriodOut(_Out):
    code: str
    label: str
    date_from: date
    date_to: date
    open_last: bool


class CashKpisOut(_Out):
    opening: float | None
    inflow: float
    outflow: float
    net: float
    closing: float | None
    diff: float | None
    opening_prev: float | None
    inflow_prev: float
    outflow_prev: float
    net_prev: float
    closing_prev: float | None
    diff_prev: float | None
    has_data: bool


class MonthPointOut(_Out):
    month_id: int
    inflow: float
    outflow: float
    closing: float | None
    inflow_prev: float
    outflow_prev: float


class ItemAmountOut(_Out):
    item_id: int
    code: str
    name: str
    activity: str
    amount: float


class ActivityAmountOut(_Out):
    activity: str
    amount: float


class BranchCashOut(_Out):
    branch_id: int
    name: str
    short_name: str
    opening: float | None
    inflow: float
    outflow: float
    net: float
    closing: float | None
    diff: float | None


class BranchAmountOut(_Out):
    branch_id: int
    name: str
    amount: float


class BankAmountOut(_Out):
    bank_name: str
    amount: float
    closing: float | None


class StationOut(_Out):
    branch_id: int
    code: str
    name: str
    short_name: str
    kind: str
    capacity_mw: float | None
    lat: float | None
    lon: float | None
    inflow: float
    outflow: float
    closing: float | None
    receivable: float | None
    payable: float | None
    overdue_payable: float | None
    # Ishlab chiqarish ko'rsatkichlari (выработка, план, КИУМ) — 3-bosqich rejasi to'ldiradi.
    production: dict[str, float] | None = None


class SeriesOut(_Out):
    key: str
    name: str
    values: list[float]


class DetailRowOut(_Out):
    branch_id: int
    branch_name: str
    direction: str
    item_id: int
    item_name: str
    activity: str
    amount: float


class ItemInfoOut(_Out):
    item_id: int
    code: str
    name: str
    activity: str
    direction: str


class ItemBranchRowOut(_Out):
    branch_id: int
    name: str
    fact: float
    plan: float | None


class ObzorSvodOut(_Out):
    period: ReportPeriodOut
    kpis: CashKpisOut
    months: list[MonthPointOut]
    activity: list[ActivityAmountOut]
    branches: list[BranchCashOut]
    items_in: list[ItemAmountOut]
    items_out: list[ItemAmountOut]
    stations: list[StationOut]


class ObzorFlowOut(_Out):
    period: ReportPeriodOut
    kpis: CashKpisOut
    direction: str
    months: list[int]
    series: list[SeriesOut]
    items: list[ItemAmountOut]
    activity: list[ActivityAmountOut]
    banks: list[BankAmountOut]
    branches: list[BranchAmountOut]


class ObzorExcelOut(_Out):
    period: ReportPeriodOut
    kpis: CashKpisOut
    branches: list[BranchCashOut]
    total: BranchCashOut
    detail: list[DetailRowOut]


class ItemBranchesOut(_Out):
    period: ReportPeriodOut
    item: ItemInfoOut
    total: float
    rows: list[ItemBranchRowOut]


def period_out(p: ReportPeriod) -> ReportPeriodOut:
    return ReportPeriodOut(code=p.code, label=p.label, date_from=p.date_from, date_to=p.date_to,
                           open_last=p.open_last)


def kpis_out(t: CashTotals) -> CashKpisOut:
    return CashKpisOut(
        opening=t.opening, inflow=t.inflow, outflow=t.outflow, net=t.net, closing=t.closing,
        diff=t.diff, opening_prev=t.opening_prev, inflow_prev=t.inflow_prev,
        outflow_prev=t.outflow_prev, net_prev=t.net_prev, closing_prev=t.closing_prev,
        diff_prev=t.diff_prev, has_data=t.has_data)


def branch_cash_out(r: BranchCash) -> BranchCashOut:
    return BranchCashOut(branch_id=r.branch_id, name=r.name, short_name=r.short_name,
                         opening=r.opening, inflow=r.inflow, outflow=r.outflow, net=r.net,
                         closing=r.closing, diff=r.diff)


def items_out(rows: list[ItemAmount]) -> list[ItemAmountOut]:
    return [ItemAmountOut(item_id=i.item_id, code=i.code, name=i.name, activity=i.activity,
                          amount=i.amount) for i in rows]
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Router va servis**

`backend/app/sections/obzor.py`:
```python
"""«Обзор» (TZ 5): Сводка, Поступления, Расходование, Таблица Excel, statya → filiallar.

Zanjir: router → queries (pub view'lar) → Cache.respond (Redis + ETag). «Требует внимания»
va xlsx eksport — alohida modullarda (app/alerts, app/sections/exports).
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Path, Response
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.auth.context import require_perm
from app.auth.permissions import Section, section_perm
from app.core.cache import Cache
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.sections.deps import SQ, SectionQuery
from app.sections.period import ReportPeriod
from app.sections.queries import cash, debt
from app.sections.queries.cash import ACTIVITIES
from app.sections.scope import BranchSel
from app.sections.schemas import (
    ActivityAmountOut,
    BankAmountOut,
    BranchAmountOut,
    BranchCashOut,
    DetailRowOut,
    ItemBranchesOut,
    ItemBranchRowOut,
    ItemInfoOut,
    MonthPointOut,
    ObzorExcelOut,
    ObzorFlowOut,
    ObzorSvodOut,
    SeriesOut,
    StationOut,
    branch_cash_out,
    items_out,
    kpis_out,
    period_out,
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/obzor", tags=["obzor"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(require_perm(section_perm(Section.OBZOR)))])
DB = Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)]
ACTIVITY_TITLES = {"centralized": "Централизованная", "operating": "Операционная",
                   "investing": "Инвестиционная"}
OTHERS = "Остальные статьи"


def _activity(rows: list[cash.ActivityAmount]) -> list[ActivityAmountOut]:
    return [ActivityAmountOut(activity=a.activity, amount=a.amount) for a in rows]


def stations(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, period: ReportPeriod) -> list[StationOut]:
    """Xaritadagi barcha ko'rinadigan stansiyalar (br tanlovidan qat'i nazar; tanlov — UI'da)."""
    every = BranchSel(None, q.branch_ids is None)
    money = {r.branch_id: r for r in cash.by_branch(db, every, period)}
    debts = {r.branch_id: r for r in debt.debt_by_branch(db, every, period.last_month)}
    out: list[StationOut] = []
    for b in db.execute(text(
            "SELECT branch_id, code, name, short_name, kind, capacity_mw, lat, lon FROM pub.branches "
            "ORDER BY sort_order, branch_id")).all():
        m, d = money.get(b.branch_id), debts.get(b.branch_id)
        out.append(StationOut(
            branch_id=b.branch_id, code=b.code, name=b.name, short_name=b.short_name, kind=b.kind,
            capacity_mw=cash.opt(b.capacity_mw), lat=cash.opt(b.lat), lon=cash.opt(b.lon),
            inflow=m.inflow if m else 0.0, outflow=m.outflow if m else 0.0,
            closing=m.closing if m else None,
            receivable=d.receivable if d else None, payable=d.payable if d else None,
            overdue_payable=d.overdue_payable if d else None))
    return out


def build_svod(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> ObzorSvodOut:
    period, sel = q.period(), q.sel()
    out_items = cash.items(db, sel, period, "out")
    return ObzorSvodOut(
        period=period_out(period),
        kpis=kpis_out(cash.cash_totals(db, sel, period)),
        months=[MonthPointOut(month_id=p.month_id, inflow=p.inflow, outflow=p.outflow,
                              closing=p.closing, inflow_prev=p.inflow_prev,
                              outflow_prev=p.outflow_prev)
                for p in cash.monthly(db, sel, q.today)],
        activity=_activity(cash.activity_split(out_items)),
        branches=[branch_cash_out(r) for r in cash.by_branch(db, sel, period)],
        items_in=items_out(cash.items(db, sel, period, "in")),
        items_out=items_out(out_items),
        stations=stations(db, q, period),
    )


def build_flow(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, direction: str) -> ObzorFlowOut:
    period, sel = q.period(), q.sel()
    months = [q.today.year * 100 + m for m in range(1, q.today.month + 1)]
    period_items = cash.items(db, sel, period, direction)
    by_item = cash.item_months(db, sel, months, direction)
    if direction == "in":
        top = [i.item_id for i in period_items[:3]]
        names = {i.item_id: i.name for i in period_items}
        series = [SeriesOut(key=str(i), name=names[i],
                            values=[by_item.get(i, {}).get(m, 0.0) for m in months]) for i in top]
        rest = [i for i in by_item if i not in top]
        if rest:
            series.append(SeriesOut(key="others", name=OTHERS, values=[
                round(sum(by_item[i].get(m, 0.0) for i in rest), 2) for m in months]))
        activity: list[ActivityAmountOut] = []
    else:
        info = {r.item_id: r.activity for r in cash.items(
            db, sel, _year_period(q, months), direction)}
        series = [SeriesOut(key=a, name=ACTIVITY_TITLES[a], values=[
            round(sum(v.get(m, 0.0) for i, v in by_item.items() if info.get(i) == a), 2)
            for m in months]) for a in ACTIVITIES]
        activity = _activity(cash.activity_split(period_items))
    flow = "inflow" if direction == "in" else "outflow"
    return ObzorFlowOut(
        period=period_out(period), kpis=kpis_out(cash.cash_totals(db, sel, period)),
        direction=direction, months=months, series=series, items=items_out(period_items),
        activity=activity,
        banks=[BankAmountOut(bank_name=b.bank_name, amount=getattr(b, flow), closing=b.closing)
               for b in cash.banks(db, sel, period) if getattr(b, flow) > 0],
        branches=sorted((BranchAmountOut(branch_id=r.branch_id, name=r.name,
                                         amount=getattr(r, flow))
                         for r in cash.by_branch(db, sel, period)),
                        key=lambda r: -r.amount),
    )


def _year_period(q: SectionQuery, months: list[int]) -> ReportPeriod:
    """Yil boshidan joriy oygacha (statyalarning faoliyat turini aniqlash uchun)."""
    period = q.period()
    return ReportPeriod("ytd", period.label, period.date_from, period.date_to, q.today,
                        tuple(months))


def build_excel(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> ObzorExcelOut:
    period, sel = q.period(), q.sel()
    totals = cash.cash_totals(db, sel, period)
    return ObzorExcelOut(
        period=period_out(period), kpis=kpis_out(totals),
        branches=[branch_cash_out(r) for r in cash.by_branch(db, sel, period)],
        total=BranchCashOut(branch_id=0, name="Итого", short_name="Итого", opening=totals.opening,
                            inflow=totals.inflow, outflow=totals.outflow, net=totals.net,
                            closing=totals.closing, diff=totals.diff),
        detail=[DetailRowOut(branch_id=d.branch_id, branch_name=d.branch_name,
                             direction=d.direction, item_id=d.item_id, item_name=d.item_name,
                             activity=d.activity, amount=d.amount)
                for d in cash.detail(db, sel, period)],
    )


def build_item(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, item_id: int) -> ItemBranchesOut:
    period = q.period()
    info, rows = cash.item_branches(db, q.sel(), period, item_id)
    return ItemBranchesOut(
        period=period_out(period),
        item=ItemInfoOut(item_id=info.item_id, code=info.code, name=info.name,
                         activity=info.activity, direction=info.direction),
        total=round(sum(r.fact for r in rows), 2),
        rows=[ItemBranchRowOut(branch_id=r.branch_id, name=r.name, fact=r.fact, plan=r.plan)
              for r in rows])


@router.get("/svod", response_model=ObzorSvodOut)
def svod(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("obzor.svod", q.cache_filters, lambda: build_svod(db, q))


@router.get("/postup", response_model=ObzorFlowOut)
def postup(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("obzor.postup", q.cache_filters, lambda: build_flow(db, q, "in"))


@router.get("/rasxod", response_model=ObzorFlowOut)
def rasxod(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("obzor.rasxod", q.cache_filters, lambda: build_flow(db, q, "out"))


@router.get("/excel", response_model=ObzorExcelOut)
def excel(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("obzor.excel", q.cache_filters, lambda: build_excel(db, q))


@router.get("/items/{item_id}", response_model=ItemBranchesOut)
def item(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ, item_id: Annotated[int, Path(ge=1)]) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("obzor.item", {**q.cache_filters, "item": item_id},
                         lambda: build_item(db, q, item_id))
```

`backend/tests/test_kazna_api.py`:
```python
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator

import pytest
import redis
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient

from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.main import create_app
from app.sections.deps import get_today
from tests.etl_helpers import TEST_AS_OF, new_source, purge_source
from tests.helpers import SeedUser, login
from tests.s1_helpers import Scenario, treasury_scenario

PAGES = ("svod", "platezhi", "calendar", "dogovory", "celevye")


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clean_cache() -> None:
    r = redis.Redis.from_url(get_settings().redis_url)
    for key in r.scan_iter("ugk:*"):
        r.delete(key)


@pytest.fixture
def client_for() -> Iterator[Callable[[SeedUser], TestClient]]:
    opened: list[TestClient] = []

    def make(user: SeedUser) -> TestClient:
        app = create_app()
        app.dependency_overrides[get_today] = lambda: TEST_AS_OF
        client = TestClient(app, base_url="https://testserver")
        client.__enter__()
        opened.append(client)
        return login(client, user)

    yield make
    for client in opened:
        client.__exit__(None, None, None)


@pytest.fixture
def sc() -> Iterator[Scenario]:
    source = new_source()
    yield treasury_scenario(source, publish=True)
    purge_source(source)


def test_kazna_requires_section_permission(client_for: Callable[[SeedUser], TestClient],
                                           make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser]) -> None:
    auditor = client_for(make_user("vnutr_kontrol"))
    for page in PAGES:
        assert auditor.get(f"/api/v1/kazna/{page}").status_code == 403, page


def test_kazna_pages_on_scenario(client_for: Callable[[SeedUser], TestClient],
                                 make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser], sc: Scenario) -> None:
    client = client_for(make_user("spec_kazna"))
    params = {"per": "m08", "br": sc.branch_id}
    svod = client.get("/api/v1/kazna/svod", params=params).json()
    k = svod["kpis"]
    assert (k["balance"], k["plan"], k["fact"], k["execution"]) == (1060.0, 50.0, 40.0, 0.8)
    assert (k["orders_count"], k["orders_amount"]) == (3, 17.0)
    assert (k["requests_count"], k["requests_amount"]) == (2, 11.0)
    assert (k["receivable"], k["payable"], k["overdue_payable"], k["banks"]) == (50.0, 100.0, 0.0, 2)
    assert k["balance_date"] == "2026-08-31"
    assert [o["status"] for o in svod["orders"]][4] == "исполнено"
    assert svod["plan_fact"][0]["deviation"] == 10.0
    assert svod["calendar"]["total"] == 130.0 and svod["calendar"]["start"] == "2026-09-25"
    platezhi = client.get("/api/v1/kazna/platezhi", params={"per": "m09", "br": sc.branch_id})
    assert platezhi.json()["plan_fact"][0]["execution"] == 0.4
    item = client.get(f"/api/v1/kazna/platezhi/items/{sc.item_out_id}", params=params).json()
    assert [(r["fact"], r["plan"]) for r in item["rows"]] == [(40.0, 50.0)]
    cal = client.get("/api/v1/kazna/calendar", params=params).json()
    assert len(cal["calendar"]["days"]) == 14 and cal["calendar"]["balance"] == 1078.0
    assert cal["calendar"]["coverage"] == pytest.approx(1078 / 130)
    assert [b["balance"] for b in cal["banks"]] == [700.0, 360.0]
    assert [(r["count"], r["amount"]) for r in cal["requests"]] == [(2, 11.0)]
    dog = client.get("/api/v1/kazna/dogovory", params={"per": "m09", "br": sc.branch_id}).json()
    assert [(c["overdue_days"], c["amount"]) for c in dog["contracts"]] == [(95, 120.0)]
    assert dog["by_branch"][0]["overdue_payable"] == 60.0
    cel = client.get("/api/v1/kazna/celevye", params={"per": "q3", "br": sc.branch_id}).json()
    assert [(s["allocated"], s["used"], s["share"]) for s in cel["sources"]] == [(20.0, 10.0, 0.5)]


def test_branch_user_scope_on_kazna(client_for: Callable[[SeedUser], TestClient],
                                    make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser],
                                    published_synthetic: int) -> None:
    econ = client_for(make_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3,)))
    for page in PAGES:
        denied = econ.get(f"/api/v1/kazna/{page}", params={"br": 5})
        assert denied.status_code == 403, page
        assert econ.get(f"/api/v1/kazna/{page}").status_code == 200, page
    dog = econ.get("/api/v1/kazna/dogovory").json()
    assert {c["branch_id"] for c in dog["contracts"]} <= {3}
    cal = econ.get("/api/v1/kazna/calendar").json()
    assert {r["branch_id"] for r in cal["requests"]} <= {3}
    etag = econ.get("/api/v1/kazna/svod").headers["ETag"]
    assert econ.get("/api/v1/kazna/svod", headers={"If-None-Match": etag}).status_code == 304
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Sxemalar**

`backend/app/sections/schemas.py` — fayl oxiriga qo'shing:
```python
class KaznaKpisOut(_Out):
    balance: float | None
    balance_date: date
    banks: int
    plan: float
    fact: float
    execution: float | None
    orders_count: int
    orders_amount: float
    requests_count: int
    requests_amount: float
    receivable: float | None
    payable: float | None
    overdue_payable: float | None


class PlanFactRowOut(_Out):
    item_id: int
    name: str
    activity: str
    plan: float
    fact: float
    execution: float | None
    deviation: float


class OrderStatusOut(_Out):
    status: str
    count: int
    amount: float


class CalendarDayOut(_Out):
    date: date
    taxes: float
    salary: float
    loans: float
    contracts: float
    total: float


class CalendarOut(_Out):
    as_of: date | None
    start: date | None
    days: list[CalendarDayOut]
    total: float
    balance: float | None
    coverage: float | None


class BankBalanceOut(_Out):
    bank_name: str
    balance: float


class ContractRowOut(_Out):
    contract_id: int
    number: str
    cp_name: str
    branch_id: int
    branch_name: str
    kind: str
    amount: float
    overdue: float
    overdue_days: int
    end_date: date | None


class DebtBranchRowOut(_Out):
    branch_id: int
    name: str
    receivable: float
    payable: float
    overdue_payable: float


class FundRowOut(_Out):
    key: int
    name: str
    allocated: float
    used: float
    share: float | None


class RequestsRowOut(_Out):
    branch_id: int
    name: str
    count: int
    amount: float


class KaznaSvodOut(_Out):
    period: ReportPeriodOut
    kpis: KaznaKpisOut
    plan_fact: list[PlanFactRowOut]
    orders: list[OrderStatusOut]
    calendar: CalendarOut
    banks: list[BankBalanceOut]
    contracts: list[ContractRowOut]


class KaznaPlatezhiOut(_Out):
    period: ReportPeriodOut
    kpis: KaznaKpisOut
    plan_fact: list[PlanFactRowOut]
    orders: list[OrderStatusOut]


class KaznaCalendarOut(_Out):
    period: ReportPeriodOut
    calendar: CalendarOut
    banks: list[BankBalanceOut]
    requests: list[RequestsRowOut]


class KaznaDogovoryOut(_Out):
    period: ReportPeriodOut
    kpis: KaznaKpisOut
    contracts: list[ContractRowOut]
    by_branch: list[DebtBranchRowOut]


class KaznaCelevyeOut(_Out):
    period: ReportPeriodOut
    sources: list[FundRowOut]
    branches: list[FundRowOut]
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Router va servis**

`backend/app/sections/kazna.py`:
```python
"""«Казначейство» (TZ 5): Сводка, Исполнение платежей, Платёжный календарь, Договоры и
задолженность, Целевые средства. Zanjir: router → queries (pub) → Cache.respond."""

from __future__ import annotations

from typing import Annotated

from fastapi import APIRouter, Depends, Path, Response
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.auth.context import require_perm
from app.auth.permissions import Section, section_perm
from app.core.cache import Cache
from app.core.db import get_db
from app.sections.deps import SQ, SectionQuery
from app.sections.obzor import build_item
from app.sections.queries import calendar, cash, debt, funds, payments
from app.sections.queries.calendar import CalendarBlock
from app.sections.queries.debt import ContractRow
from app.sections.queries.funds import FundRow
from app.sections.schemas import (
    BankBalanceOut,
    CalendarDayOut,
    CalendarOut,
    ContractRowOut,
    DebtBranchRowOut,
    FundRowOut,
    ItemBranchesOut,
    KaznaCalendarOut,
    KaznaCelevyeOut,
    KaznaDogovoryOut,
    KaznaKpisOut,
    KaznaPlatezhiOut,
    KaznaSvodOut,
    OrderStatusOut,
    PlanFactRowOut,
    RequestsRowOut,
    period_out,
)

router = APIRouter(prefix="/kazna", tags=["kazna"],
                   dependencies=[Depends(require_perm(section_perm(Section.KAZNA)))])
DB = Annotated[Session, Depends(get_db)]


def build_kpis(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> KaznaKpisOut:
    period, sel = q.period(), q.sel()
    totals = cash.cash_totals(db, sel, period)
    plan_rows = payments.plan_fact(db, sel, period)
    plan = round(sum(r.plan for r in plan_rows), 2)
    fact = round(sum(r.fact for r in plan_rows), 2)
    orders = payments.orders_by_status(db, sel, period)
    req_count, req_amount = payments.requests_total(db, sel)
    summary = debt.debt_summary(db, sel, period.last_month)
    return KaznaKpisOut(
        balance=totals.closing, balance_date=period.date_to,
        banks=len(calendar.bank_balances(db, sel, period.last_month)),
        plan=plan, fact=fact, execution=round(fact / plan, 6) if plan else None,
        orders_count=sum(o.count for o in orders),
        orders_amount=round(sum(o.amount for o in orders), 2),
        requests_count=req_count, requests_amount=req_amount,
        receivable=summary.receivable if summary else None,
        payable=summary.payable if summary else None,
        overdue_payable=summary.overdue_payable if summary else None,
    )


def _plan_fact(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> list[PlanFactRowOut]:
    return [PlanFactRowOut(item_id=r.item_id, name=r.name, activity=r.activity, plan=r.plan,
                           fact=r.fact, execution=r.execution, deviation=r.deviation)
            for r in payments.plan_fact(db, q.sel(), q.period())]


def _orders(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> list[OrderStatusOut]:
    return [OrderStatusOut(status=o.status, count=o.count, amount=o.amount)
            for o in payments.orders_by_status(db, q.sel(), q.period())]


def calendar_out(block: CalendarBlock) -> CalendarOut:
    return CalendarOut(
        as_of=block.as_of, start=block.start,
        days=[CalendarDayOut(date=d.day, taxes=d.taxes, salary=d.salary, loans=d.loans,
                             contracts=d.contracts, total=d.total) for d in block.days],
        total=block.total, balance=block.balance, coverage=block.coverage)


def _banks(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> list[BankBalanceOut]:
    return [BankBalanceOut(bank_name=b.bank_name, balance=b.balance)
            for b in calendar.bank_balances(db, q.sel(), q.period().last_month)]


def _contract(c: ContractRow) -> ContractRowOut:
    return ContractRowOut(contract_id=c.contract_id, number=c.number, cp_name=c.cp_name,
                          branch_id=c.branch_id, branch_name=c.branch_name, kind=c.kind,
                          amount=c.amount, overdue=c.overdue, overdue_days=c.overdue_days,
                          end_date=c.end_date)


def _fund(f: FundRow) -> FundRowOut:
    return FundRowOut(key=f.key, name=f.name, allocated=f.allocated, used=f.used, share=f.share)


def build_svod(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> KaznaSvodOut:
    return KaznaSvodOut(
        period=period_out(q.period()), kpis=build_kpis(db, q), plan_fact=_plan_fact(db, q),
        orders=_orders(db, q), calendar=calendar_out(calendar.calendar(db, q.sel())),
        banks=_banks(db, q),
        contracts=[_contract(c) for c in debt.contracts(db, q.sel(), q.period().last_month, 50)])


def build_platezhi(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> KaznaPlatezhiOut:
    return KaznaPlatezhiOut(period=period_out(q.period()), kpis=build_kpis(db, q),
                            plan_fact=_plan_fact(db, q), orders=_orders(db, q))


def build_calendar(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> KaznaCalendarOut:
    return KaznaCalendarOut(
        period=period_out(q.period()), calendar=calendar_out(calendar.calendar(db, q.sel())),
        banks=_banks(db, q),
        requests=[RequestsRowOut(branch_id=r.branch_id, name=r.name, count=r.count,
                                 amount=r.amount)
                  for r in payments.requests_by_branch(db, q.sel())])


def build_dogovory(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> KaznaDogovoryOut:
    month = q.period().last_month
    return KaznaDogovoryOut(
        period=period_out(q.period()), kpis=build_kpis(db, q),
        contracts=[_contract(c) for c in debt.contracts(db, q.sel(), month, 500)],
        by_branch=[DebtBranchRowOut(branch_id=r.branch_id, name=r.name, receivable=r.receivable,
                                    payable=r.payable, overdue_payable=r.overdue_payable)
                   for r in debt.debt_by_branch(db, q.sel(), month)])


def build_celevye(db: Session, q: SectionQuery) -> KaznaCelevyeOut:
    period, sel = q.period(), q.sel()
    return KaznaCelevyeOut(period=period_out(period),
                           sources=[_fund(f) for f in funds.funds_by_source(db, sel, period)],
                           branches=[_fund(f) for f in funds.funds_by_branch(db, sel, period)])


@router.get("/svod", response_model=KaznaSvodOut)
def svod(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("kazna.svod", q.cache_filters, lambda: build_svod(db, q))


@router.get("/platezhi", response_model=KaznaPlatezhiOut)
def platezhi(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("kazna.platezhi", q.cache_filters, lambda: build_platezhi(db, q))


@router.get("/platezhi/items/{item_id}", response_model=ItemBranchesOut)
def platezhi_item(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ, item_id: Annotated[int, Path(ge=1)]) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("kazna.item", {**q.cache_filters, "item": item_id},
                         lambda: build_item(db, q, item_id))


@router.get("/calendar", response_model=KaznaCalendarOut)
def calendar_page(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("kazna.calendar", q.cache_filters, lambda: build_calendar(db, q))


@router.get("/dogovory", response_model=KaznaDogovoryOut)
def dogovory(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("kazna.dogovory", q.cache_filters, lambda: build_dogovory(db, q))


@router.get("/celevye", response_model=KaznaCelevyeOut)
def celevye(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ) -> Response:
    return cache.respond("kazna.celevye", q.cache_filters, lambda: build_celevye(db, q))
```

### Task 9: «Требует внимания» — qoidalar reyestri, 5 ta g'aznachilik qoidasi, sahifa va hisoblagich

**Files:**
- Create: `backend/app/alerts/__init__.py`, `backend/app/alerts/types.py`, `backend/app/alerts/engine.py`, `backend/app/alerts/treasury.py`
- Modify: `backend/app/sections/fmt.py` (`plural`), `backend/app/sections/schemas.py`, `backend/app/sections/obzor.py` (2 route), `backend/tests/test_route_guards.py`
- Test: `backend/tests/test_alerts.py`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `core.alert_rules` (`code, title, section, page, threshold{metric, op, value}, level, enabled, is_current`), `ALERT_RULES` (seed tartibi), `Section`, `section_perm`, `SectionQuery`, `queries.cash/payments/debt/calendar`, `fmt_money`, `fmt_pct`, `Ctx`, `Cache`.
- Produces:
  - `app.alerts.types`: `RuleConfig(code, title, section, page, level, threshold)` + `.op`, `.value`; `AlertHit(code, level, title, message, section, page, value)`; `Evaluator = Callable[[Session, SectionQuery, RuleConfig], AlertHit | None]`; `REGISTRY: dict[str, Evaluator]`; `evaluator(code)` dekoratori (takror → `ValueError`); `breached(value, rule) -> bool`; `hit(rule, message, value) -> AlertHit`.
  - `app.alerts.engine`: `load_rules(db) -> list[RuleConfig]` (joriy, yoqilgan, seed tartibida), `evaluate(db, q, permissions) -> tuple[list[AlertHit], list[str]]` (natija — kritiklar birinchi; ikkinchisi — tekshirilgan qoidalar sarlavhalari), `allowed_sections(permissions) -> list[str]`, `build_alerts(db, q, permissions) -> AlertsOut`, `build_count(db, q, permissions) -> AlertCountOut`. Keyingi bo'limlar faqat `@evaluator("<kod>")` bilan modul qo'shadi va uni `app/alerts/__init__.py` da import qiladi.
  - `app.alerts.treasury`: `cash_below_monthly_outflow`, `payments_14d_exceed_balance`, `payment_plan_execution`, `contracts_overdue_90d`, `overdue_payables_share`.
  - Sxemalar: `AlertOut(code, level, title, message, section, page, value)`, `AlertsOut(period, items, checked)`, `AlertCountOut(count, critical)`.
  - HTTP: `GET /obzor/vnimanie`, `GET /obzor/vnimanie/count` (kesh filtrlariga foydalanuvchi ko'ra oladigan bo'limlar ro'yxati ham kiradi).
  - `app.sections.fmt.plural(n, one, few, many) -> str`.

```python
import json
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator
from typing import Any

import pytest
import redis
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.alerts.engine import evaluate, load_rules
from app.alerts.types import REGISTRY, RuleConfig
from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.db import apply_rls, get_engine, new_session
from app.main import create_app
from app.sections.deps import SectionQuery, get_today
from app.sections.fmt import plural
from tests.etl_helpers import (
    TEST_AS_OF,
    branch_row,
    merge_all,
    new_source,
    new_tag,
    open_run,
    purge_source,
    stage,
)
from tests.helpers import SeedUser, login
from tests.s1_helpers import Scenario, rebuild, treasury_scenario

ALL = frozenset({"section:obzor:view", "section:kazna:view"})


@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)
def _clean_cache() -> None:
    r = redis.Redis.from_url(get_settings().redis_url)
    for key in r.scan_iter("ugk:*"):
        r.delete(key)


@pytest.fixture(scope="module")
def sc() -> Iterator[Scenario]:
    source = new_source()
    yield treasury_scenario(source, publish=True)
    purge_source(source)


@pytest.fixture
def db() -> Iterator[Session]:
    with new_session() as s:
        apply_rls(s, None)
        yield s


def _q(branch_id: int, per: str = "m09") -> SectionQuery:
    return SectionQuery(branch_id, per, TEST_AS_OF, None)


def _rule(code: str, op: str, value: float) -> RuleConfig:
    return RuleConfig(code, code, "kazna", "svod", "critical", {"metric": "x", "op": op,
                                                                  "value": value})


def _put_rule(code: str, **changes: Any) -> None:
    with get_engine("app").begin() as conn:
        row = conn.execute(text("SELECT * FROM core.alert_rules WHERE code = :c AND is_current"),
                           {"c": code}).mappings().one()
        data = {**dict(row), **changes}
        conn.execute(text("UPDATE core.alert_rules SET is_current = false "
                          "WHERE code = :c AND is_current"), {"c": code})
        conn.execute(text(
            "INSERT INTO core.alert_rules (code, version, title, section, page, threshold, level, "
            "enabled) VALUES (:code, :v, :title, :section, :page, CAST(:th AS jsonb), :level, :en)"),
            {"code": code, "v": row["version"] + 1, "title": data["title"],
             "section": data["section"], "page": data["page"],
             "th": json.dumps(data["threshold"]), "level": data["level"],
             "en": data["enabled"]})


def test_plural() -> None:
    assert [plural(n, "договор", "договора", "договоров") for n in (1, 3, 5, 11, 21, 22)] == [
        "договор", "договора", "договоров", "договоров", "договор", "договора"]


def test_registry_has_treasury_rules() -> None:
    assert {"cash_below_monthly_outflow", "payments_14d_exceed_balance",
            "payment_plan_execution", "contracts_overdue_90d",
            "overdue_payables_share"} <= set(REGISTRY)


def test_default_rules_on_scenario_critical_first(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    hits, checked = evaluate(db, _q(sc.branch_id), ALL)
    assert [h.code for h in hits] == ["contracts_overdue_90d", "payment_plan_execution",
                                      "overdue_payables_share"]
    assert hits[0].level == "critical" and hits[0].page == "dogovory"
    assert hits[0].message == (f"1 договор просрочен более 90 дней на сумму 60,0 млн, "
                               f"крупнейший: ООО «Тест Поставщик {sc.tag}»")
    assert hits[1].message == "исполнение 40,0%, отклонение 18,0 млн"
    assert hits[2].message == "60,0 млн из 120,0 млн кредиторской задолженности (50,0%)"
    assert len(checked) == 5


def test_balance_rules_fire_with_custom_thresholds(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    cash_hit = REGISTRY["cash_below_monthly_outflow"](
        db, _q(sc.branch_id), _rule("cash_below_monthly_outflow", "<", 100))
    assert cash_hit is not None and cash_hit.value == pytest.approx(1078 / 15)
    assert cash_hit.message == "1,1 млрд при среднем расходовании 15,0 млн в месяц"
    cal_hit = REGISTRY["payments_14d_exceed_balance"](
        db, _q(sc.branch_id), _rule("payments_14d_exceed_balance", ">", 0.1))
    assert cal_hit is not None and cal_hit.value == pytest.approx(130 / 1078)
    assert cal_hit.message == "к оплате 130,0 млн за 14 дней, на счетах 1,1 млрд"


def test_balance_rules_skip_without_balances(db: Session) -> None:
    source = new_source()
    try:
        branch = branch_row(new_tag())
        run = open_run(source)
        stage(run, source, {"dim_branch": [branch], "fact_cashflow": [
            {"doc_type": "bank_entry", "src_id": "n1", "date_id": 20260905,
             "branch_code": branch["code"], "item_code": "PKM54-O09", "direction": "out",
             "amount": 5}], "fact_payment_plan": [
            {"doc_type": "payment_schedule", "src_id": "n2", "date_id": 20260928,
             "branch_code": branch["code"], "item_code": "PKM54-O09", "amount": 9}]})
        merge_all(run, source)
        rebuild(run, publish=True)
        branch_id = db.execute(text("SELECT branch_id FROM pub.branches WHERE code = :c"),
                               {"c": branch["code"]}).scalar_one()
        for code in ("cash_below_monthly_outflow", "payments_14d_exceed_balance"):
            assert REGISTRY[code](db, _q(branch_id), _rule(code, "<", 1e9)) is None
            assert REGISTRY[code](db, _q(branch_id), _rule(code, ">", -1e9)) is None
    finally:
        purge_source(source)


def test_threshold_change_and_disable(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    rule = next(r for r in load_rules(db) if r.code == "contracts_overdue_90d")
    try:
        _put_rule("contracts_overdue_90d", threshold={**rule.threshold, "value": 95})
        assert "contracts_overdue_90d" not in [h.code for h in evaluate(db, _q(sc.branch_id),
                                                                        ALL)[0]]
        _put_rule("contracts_overdue_90d", threshold=rule.threshold, enabled=False)
        codes = [r.code for r in load_rules(db)]
        assert "contracts_overdue_90d" not in codes
    finally:
        _put_rule("contracts_overdue_90d", threshold=rule.threshold, enabled=True)


def test_rules_of_forbidden_sections_are_hidden(sc: Scenario, db: Session) -> None:
    hits, checked = evaluate(db, _q(sc.branch_id), frozenset({"section:obzor:view"}))
    assert hits == [] and checked == []


@pytest.fixture
def client_for() -> Iterator[Callable[[SeedUser], TestClient]]:
    opened: list[TestClient] = []

    def make(user: SeedUser) -> TestClient:
        app = create_app()
        app.dependency_overrides[get_today] = lambda: TEST_AS_OF
        client = TestClient(app, base_url="https://testserver")
        client.__enter__()
        opened.append(client)
        return login(client, user)

    yield make
    for client in opened:
        client.__exit__(None, None, None)


def test_vnimanie_endpoints(sc: Scenario, client_for: Callable[[SeedUser], TestClient],
                            make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser]) -> None:
    boss = client_for(make_user("rukovodstvo"))
    params = {"per": "m09", "br": sc.branch_id}
    body = boss.get("/api/v1/obzor/vnimanie", params=params).json()
    assert [i["code"] for i in body["items"]] == ["contracts_overdue_90d",
                                                  "payment_plan_execution",
                                                  "overdue_payables_share"]
    assert body["items"][0]["section"] == "kazna" and body["period"]["code"] == "m09"
    assert boss.get("/api/v1/obzor/vnimanie/count", params=params).json() == {
        "count": 3, "critical": 1}
    # Bir xil doira (hammasi), boshqa ruxsatlar: kesh boshqa foydalanuvchining natijasini bermaydi.
    profile = client_for(make_user("profil_sluzhba"))
    assert profile.get("/api/v1/obzor/vnimanie", params=params).json()["items"] == []
    assert profile.get("/api/v1/obzor/vnimanie/count", params=params).json()["count"] == 0
    econ = client_for(make_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3,)))
    assert econ.get("/api/v1/obzor/vnimanie", params=params).status_code == 403
```

- [ ] **Step 3: Implementatsiya — tiplar va dvigatel**

`backend/app/sections/fmt.py` — oxiriga qo'shing:
```python
def plural(n: int, one: str, few: str, many: str) -> str:
    """Ruscha ko'plik: 1 договор, 2 договора, 5 договоров, 21 договор."""
    tail, tens = n % 10, n % 100
    if tail == 1 and tens != 11:
        return one
    if 2 <= tail <= 4 and not 12 <= tens <= 14:
        return few
    return many
```

```python
"""Qoida konfiguratsiyasi (core.alert_rules) va natija tiplari."""

from __future__ import annotations

import operator
from collections.abc import Callable
from dataclasses import dataclass
from typing import TYPE_CHECKING, Any

if TYPE_CHECKING:
    from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

    from app.sections.deps import SectionQuery

_OPS: dict[str, Callable[[float, float], bool]] = {
    "<": operator.lt, "<=": operator.le, ">": operator.gt, ">=": operator.ge,
}


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class RuleConfig:
    code: str
    title: str
    section: str
    page: str
    level: str  # critical | warning
    threshold: dict[str, Any]

    @property
    def op(self) -> str:
        return str(self.threshold.get("op", ">"))

    @property
    def value(self) -> float:
        return float(self.threshold["value"])


@dataclass(frozen=True, slots=True)
class AlertHit:
    code: str
    level: str
    title: str
    message: str
    section: str
    page: str
    value: float | None


Evaluator = Callable[["Session", "SectionQuery", RuleConfig], AlertHit | None]
REGISTRY: dict[str, Evaluator] = {}


def evaluator(code: str) -> Callable[[Evaluator], Evaluator]:
    def register(fn: Evaluator) -> Evaluator:
        if code in REGISTRY:
            raise ValueError(f"evaluator allaqachon bor: {code}")
        REGISTRY[code] = fn
        return fn

    return register


def breached(value: float, rule: RuleConfig) -> bool:
    return _OPS[rule.op](value, rule.value)


def hit(rule: RuleConfig, message: str, value: float | None) -> AlertHit:
    finite = value if value is None or value not in (float("inf"), float("-inf")) else None
    return AlertHit(rule.code, rule.level, rule.title, message, rule.section, rule.page, finite)
```

`backend/app/alerts/engine.py`:
```python
"""Qoidalar har so'rovda filtrlar bilan tekshiriladi (TZ 4): chegaralar DB'dan, kritiklar birinchi.

Foydalanuvchi ko'ra olmaydigan bo'lim qoidalari hisoblanmaydi va ko'rsatilmaydi (qatorni bosish
o'sha bo'limga olib boradi). Evaluatori hali yo'q qoidalar (keyingi bo'limlar) o'tkazib yuboriladi.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy import text
from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

import app.alerts  # noqa: F401  (evaluatorlar ro'yxatga olinadi)
from app.alerts.types import REGISTRY, AlertHit, RuleConfig
from app.auth.permissions import Section, section_perm
from app.dwh.reference import ALERT_RULES
from app.sections.deps import SectionQuery
from app.sections.schemas import AlertCountOut, AlertOut, AlertsOut, period_out

_ORDER = {rule.code: i for i, rule in enumerate(ALERT_RULES)}


def load_rules(db: Session) -> list[RuleConfig]:
    rows = db.execute(text(
        "SELECT code, title, section, page, level, threshold FROM core.alert_rules "
        "WHERE is_current AND enabled")).all()
    rules = [RuleConfig(r.code, r.title, r.section, r.page, r.level, dict(r.threshold))
             for r in rows]
    return sorted(rules, key=lambda r: (_ORDER.get(r.code, len(_ORDER)), r.code))


def allowed_sections(permissions: frozenset[str]) -> list[str]:
    return sorted(s.value for s in Section if section_perm(s) in permissions)


def evaluate(db: Session, q: SectionQuery,
             permissions: frozenset[str]) -> tuple[list[AlertHit], list[str]]:
    sections = set(allowed_sections(permissions))
    hits: list[AlertHit] = []
    checked: list[str] = []
    for rule in load_rules(db):
        fn = REGISTRY.get(rule.code)
        if fn is None or rule.section not in sections:
            continue
        checked.append(rule.title)
        found = fn(db, q, rule)
        if found is not None:
            hits.append(found)
    hits.sort(key=lambda h: 0 if h.level == "critical" else 1)  # barqaror: ichida seed tartibi
    return hits, checked


def build_alerts(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, permissions: frozenset[str]) -> AlertsOut:
    hits, checked = evaluate(db, q, permissions)
    return AlertsOut(period=period_out(q.period()), checked=checked,
                     items=[AlertOut(code=h.code, level=h.level, title=h.title,
                                     message=h.message, section=h.section, page=h.page,
                                     value=h.value) for h in hits])


def build_count(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, permissions: frozenset[str]) -> AlertCountOut:
    hits, _ = evaluate(db, q, permissions)
    return AlertCountOut(count=len(hits), critical=sum(1 for h in hits if h.level == "critical"))
```

- [ ] **Step 4: Implementatsiya — g'aznachilik qoidalari**

`backend/app/alerts/treasury.py`:
```python
"""TZ 4 «Требует внимания» — g'aznachilik qoidalari (chegaralar core.alert_rules dan).

Ma'lumot bo'lmasa (qoldiqlar, reja, qarzdorlik) qoida None qaytaradi — yolg'on signal yo'q.
"""

from __future__ import annotations

from sqlalchemy.orm import Session

from app.alerts.types import AlertHit, RuleConfig, breached, evaluator, hit
from app.sections.deps import SectionQuery
from app.sections.fmt import fmt_money, fmt_pct, plural
from app.sections.queries import calendar, cash, debt, payments


@evaluator("cash_below_monthly_outflow")
def cash_below_monthly_outflow(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, rule: RuleConfig) -> AlertHit | None:
    period = q.period()
    totals = cash.cash_totals(db, q.sel(), period)
    months = period.month_equivalents
    if totals.closing is None or totals.outflow <= 0 or months <= 0:
        return None
    average = totals.outflow / months
    ratio = totals.closing / average
    if not breached(ratio, rule):
        return None
    return hit(rule, f"{fmt_money(totals.closing)} при среднем расходовании "
                     f"{fmt_money(average)} в месяц", ratio)


@evaluator("payments_14d_exceed_balance")
def payments_14d_exceed_balance(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, rule: RuleConfig) -> AlertHit | None:
    block = calendar.calendar(db, q.sel())
    if block.balance is None or block.total <= 0:
        return None
    ratio = block.total / block.balance if block.balance > 0 else float("inf")
    if not breached(ratio, rule):
        return None
    return hit(rule, f"к оплате {fmt_money(block.total)} за 14 дней, на счетах "
                     f"{fmt_money(block.balance)}", ratio)


@evaluator("payment_plan_execution")
def payment_plan_execution(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, rule: RuleConfig) -> AlertHit | None:
    rows = payments.plan_fact(db, q.sel(), q.period())
    plan = sum(r.plan for r in rows)
    if plan <= 0:
        return None
    fact = sum(r.fact for r in rows)
    ratio = fact / plan
    if not breached(ratio, rule):
        return None
    return hit(rule, f"исполнение {fmt_pct(ratio)}, отклонение {fmt_money(plan - fact)}", ratio)


@evaluator("contracts_overdue_90d")
def contracts_overdue_90d(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, rule: RuleConfig) -> AlertHit | None:
    rows = [c for c in debt.overdue_contracts(db, q.sel(), q.period().last_month, 0)
            if breached(c.overdue_days, rule)]
    if not rows:
        return None
    total = sum(c.overdue for c in rows)
    biggest = max(rows, key=lambda c: c.overdue)
    n = len(rows)
    word = plural(n, "договор", "договора", "договоров")
    verb = "просрочен" if word == "договор" else "просрочены"
    return hit(rule, f"{n} {word} {verb} более {int(rule.value)} дней на сумму "
                     f"{fmt_money(total)}, крупнейший: {biggest.cp_name}", float(n))


@evaluator("overdue_payables_share")
def overdue_payables_share(db: Session, q: SectionQuery, rule: RuleConfig) -> AlertHit | None:
    summary = debt.debt_summary(db, q.sel(), q.period().last_month)
    if summary is None or summary.payable <= 0:
        return None
    share = summary.overdue_payable / summary.payable
    if not breached(share, rule):
        return None
    return hit(rule, f"{fmt_money(summary.overdue_payable)} из {fmt_money(summary.payable)} "
                     f"кредиторской задолженности ({fmt_pct(share)})", share)
```

```python
def _alert_filters(q: SectionQuery, permissions: frozenset[str]) -> dict[str, object]:
    from app.alerts.engine import allowed_sections

    # Natija ruxsatlarga bog'liq — bir xil doiradagi boshqa rol keshini olmasligi uchun.
    return {**q.cache_filters, "sections": allowed_sections(permissions)}


@router.get("/vnimanie", response_model=AlertsOut)
def vnimanie(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ, ctx: Ctx) -> Response:
    from app.alerts.engine import build_alerts

    return cache.respond("obzor.vnimanie", _alert_filters(q, ctx.permissions),
                         lambda: build_alerts(db, q, ctx.permissions))


@router.get("/vnimanie/count", response_model=AlertCountOut)
def vnimanie_count(cache: Cache, db: DB, q: SQ, ctx: Ctx) -> Response:
    from app.alerts.engine import build_count

    return cache.respond("obzor.vnimanie_count", _alert_filters(q, ctx.permissions),
                         lambda: build_count(db, q, ctx.permissions))
```

```python
import io
import time
import zipfile
from collections.abc import Callable, Iterator

import pytest
import redis
from fastapi.testclient import TestClient
from openpyxl import load_workbook
from sqlalchemy import text

from app.core.config import get_settings
from app.core.db import get_engine
from app.main import create_app

from app.sections.period import resolve_period
from app.sections.queries.cash import MonthPoint
from app.sections.queries.payments import PlanFactRow
from app.sections.schemas import BranchCashOut, CashKpisOut, DetailRowOut, ObzorExcelOut, period_out

from tests.etl_helpers import TEST_AS_OF, new_source, purge_source

@pytest.fixture(autouse=True)

@pytest.fixture

    opened: list[TestClient] = []

    def make(user: SeedUser) -> TestClient:
        app = create_app()
        app.dependency_overrides[get_today] = lambda: TEST_AS_OF
        client = TestClient(app, base_url="https://testserver")
        client.__enter__()
        opened.append(client)

@pytest.fixture

    purge_source(source)


def _last_export() -> dict[str, object]:
    with get_engine("migrator").connect() as conn:
        row = conn.execute(text("SELECT user_id, details FROM core.audit_log "
                                "WHERE action = 'data.exported' ORDER BY id DESC LIMIT 1")).one()
    return {"user_id": row.user_id, **row.details}


def test_xlsx_four_sheets_with_scenario_values(client_for: Callable[[SeedUser], TestClient],
                                               make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser],
                                               sc: Scenario) -> None:
    user = make_user("rukovodstvo")
    client = client_for(user)
    r = client.get("/api/v1/obzor/excel.xlsx", params={"per": "m08", "br": sc.branch_id})
    assert r.status_code == 200, r.text

                       "Чистый поток", "Конечный остаток", "Разница"]
    assert svod[1][1:] == [1000.0, 100.0, 40.0, 60.0, 1060.0, 0.0]
    assert svod[-1][0] == "Итого"

    assert plan[0][1:] == ["Операционная", 50.0, 40.0, 80.0, 10.0]
    months = [[c.value for c in row] for row in wb["По месяцам"].iter_rows(min_row=2)]
    assert months[7] == ["Август", 100.0, 40.0, 60.0] and len(months) == 9
    audit = _last_export()

def test_xlsx_within_ten_seconds_on_synthetic(client_for: Callable[[SeedUser], TestClient],
                                              make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser],

def test_formula_like_names_are_text() -> None:
    period = resolve_period("m08", TEST_AS_OF)
    kpis = CashKpisOut(opening=None, inflow=1, outflow=0, net=1, closing=None, diff=None,
                       opening_prev=None, inflow_prev=0, outflow_prev=0, net_prev=0,

    data = ObzorExcelOut(
        period=period_out(period), kpis=kpis, branches=[branch],
        total=branch.model_copy(update={"branch_id": 0, "name": "Итого"}),
        detail=[DetailRowOut(branch_id=9, branch_name="@SUM(A1)", direction="in", item_id=1,
                             item_name='=HYPERLINK("http://x","y")', activity="operating",
                             amount=1)])
    body = build_workbook(data, [PlanFactRow(1, "-2+3", "operating", 1, 1)],
                          [MonthPoint(202608, 1, 0, None, 0, 0)], "все филиалы")
    with zipfile.ZipFile(io.BytesIO(body)) as z:
        sheets = [n for n in z.namelist() if n.startswith("xl/worksheets/sheet")]
        assert sheets and all(b"<f>" not in z.read(n) for n in sheets)
    wb = load_workbook(io.BytesIO(body))
    assert wb["Детализация"]["C2"].value == '=HYPERLINK("http://x","y")'
    assert wb["Детализация"]["C2"].data_type == "s"
    assert wb["Сводка"]["A4"].value == "+cmd|' /C calc'!A0"
    assert wb["План-факт"]["A2"].value == "-2+3"


def test_export_log(client_for: Callable[[SeedUser], TestClient],
                    make_user: Callable[..., SeedUser]) -> None:
    user = make_user("rukovodstvo")
    client = client_for(user)
    body = {"kind": "csv", "section": "obzor", "page": "svod",
            "card": "Расходование по филиалам", "per": "m08", "rows": 5}
    assert client.post("/api/v1/exports/log", json=body).status_code == 204
    audit = _last_export()
    assert (audit["user_id"], audit["format"], audit["card"], audit["rows"]) == (
        user.id, "csv", "Расходование по филиалам", 5)
    assert client.post("/api/v1/exports/log", json={**body, "extra": 1}).status_code == 422
    assert client.post("/api/v1/exports/log", json={**body, "kind": "pdf"}).status_code == 422
    auditor = client_for(make_user("vnutr_kontrol"))
    assert auditor.post("/api/v1/exports/log", json=body).status_code == 403
    profile = client_for(make_user("profil_sluzhba"))
    assert profile.post("/api/v1/exports/log",
                        json={**body, "section": "kazna"}).status_code == 403
    econ = client_for(make_user("economist_filiala", branch_ids=(3,)))
    assert econ.post("/api/v1/exports/log", json={**body, "br": 4}).status_code == 403
```
