### Task 16: ГЭС xaritasi — prototip geometriyasi, stansiya belgilari va tanlangan stansiya paneli

**Files:**
- Create: `frontend/scripts/extract-geo.mjs`, `frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/uz-geo.json` (generatsiya), `frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/geo.ts`, `frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/GesMap.tsx`, `frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/StationPanel.tsx`, `frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/map.css`
- Modify: `frontend/src/i18n/ru.ts`
- Test: `frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/geo.test.ts`, `frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/GesMap.test.tsx`, `frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/StationPanel.test.tsx`

**Interfaces:**
- Consumes: `StationOut`, `FilterState`, `formatMoney`, `formatNumber`, `formatPct`, `Rank`, `Button`, `cx`, `t`, `SVOD` fixture, `expectNoAxeViolations`.
- Produces:
  - `geo.ts`: `UzGeo`, `UZ_GEO`, `mainXY(lon, lat)`, `insetXY(lon, lat)`, `inInset(lon, lat)`, `HIDDEN_LABELS`, `LABEL_SIDE: Record<string, 'l' | 'r' | 't' | 'b'>` (filial kodi bo'yicha, prototip `GES` jadvalidan), `markerRadius(capacity, inset, hq)`.
  - `GesMap({ stations: StationOut[]; selected: number | null; onSelect(id: number | null): void })` — SVG (`role="group"`), har stansiya `role="button"` + `aria-pressed`, Enter/Space, `<title>` — tooltip; kvadrat — bosh tashkilot; qizil — `production.execution < 0.95` (S1'da `production` null → hammasi ko'k).
  - `StationPanel({ stations; selected; onSelect; filters; onBranch })` — tanlanmagan: stansiyalar ro'yxati; tanlangan: moliyaviy ko'rsatkichlar, ishlab chiqarish bloki o'rnida izoh, «Открыть данные станции» → `onBranch(id)`.

Geometriya manbai — tasdiqlangan prototipning `GEO` obyekti (`.superpowers/sdd/planning/prototype.js`, 342-qator: `W, H, reg[] (n, d, x?, y?, city?), ins[], inset, main, insp`). Unda TZ talab qilgan Toshkent viloyati alohida kattalashtirilgan ramkasi va stansiyalarni joylash proyeksiyasi bor; eski dashboarddagi `map-data.js` (878×573, ramkasiz, koordinatalar kalibrlanmagan) ishlatilmaydi.

- [ ] **Step 1: Geometriyani ajratib olish**

`frontend/scripts/extract-geo.mjs`:
```js
// Prototipdagi `const GEO={...};` qatoridan xarita geometriyasini JSON sifatida ajratadi.
// Foydalanish: node frontend/scripts/extract-geo.mjs <prototype.js> <chiqish.json>
import { readFileSync, writeFileSync } from 'node:fs';

const [source, target] = process.argv.slice(2);
if (!source || !target) throw new Error('usage: extract-geo.mjs <prototype.js> <out.json>');
const line = readFileSync(source, 'utf8').split('\n').find((l) => l.startsWith('const GEO='));
if (!line) throw new Error('GEO topilmadi');
const geo = JSON.parse(line.slice('const GEO='.length).trim().replace(/;$/, ''));
for (const key of ['W', 'H', 'reg', 'ins', 'inset', 'main', 'insp']) {
  if (!(key in geo)) throw new Error(`GEO.${key} yo'q`);
}
if (geo.reg.length !== geo.ins.length) throw new Error('reg va ins uzunligi mos emas');
const out = {
  W: geo.W, H: geo.H, inset: geo.inset, main: geo.main, insp: geo.insp,
  reg: geo.reg.map((r) => ({ n: r.n, d: r.d, ...(r.x === undefined ? {} : { x: r.x, y: r.y }), ...(r.city ? { city: true } : {}) })),
  ins: geo.ins,
};
writeFileSync(target, JSON.stringify(out));
console.log(`regions=${out.reg.length} bytes=${JSON.stringify(out).length}`);
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/geo.test.ts`:
```ts
import { describe, expect, it } from 'vitest';
import { inInset, insetXY, LABEL_SIDE, mainXY, markerRadius, UZ_GEO } from './geo';

describe('uz geo', () => {
  it('contains the prototype regions and inset', () => {
    expect(UZ_GEO.reg).toHaveLength(14);
    expect(UZ_GEO.reg.map((r) => r.n)).toContain('Ташкентская');
    expect(UZ_GEO.ins).toHaveLength(14);
    expect(UZ_GEO.inset).toHaveLength(4);
  });
  it('projects stations inside the canvas and the inset frame', () => {
    const [x, y] = mainXY(61.41, 41.24);
    expect(x).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(x).toBeLessThan(UZ_GEO.W);
    expect(y).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(y).toBeLessThan(UZ_GEO.H);
    expect(inInset(69.2797, 41.3111)).toBe(true);
    expect(inInset(61.41, 41.24)).toBe(false);
    const [ix, iy] = insetXY(70.029, 41.626);
    const [fx, fy, fw, fh] = UZ_GEO.inset;
    expect(ix).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(fx);
    expect(ix).toBeLessThanOrEqual(fx + fw);
    expect(iy).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(fy);
    expect(iy).toBeLessThanOrEqual(fy + fh);
  });
  it('sizes markers by capacity', () => {
    expect(markerRadius(666, false, false)).toBeGreaterThan(markerRadius(21, false, false));
    expect(markerRadius(null, true, true)).toBe(6);
    expect(LABEL_SIDE['UGE-02']).toBe('t');
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { render, screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import userEvent from '@testing-library/user-event';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../../../test/axe';
import { SVOD } from '../../../test/s1Fixtures';
import { GesMap } from './GesMap';

describe('GesMap', () => {
  it('renders selectable stations, the HQ square and toggles selection', async () => {
    const onSelect = vi.fn();
    const user = userEvent.setup();
    const { container, rerender } = render(<GesMap stations={SVOD.stations} selected={null} onSelect={onSelect} />);
    expect(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Карта Узбекистана с расположением ГЭС' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button')).toHaveLength(3);
    expect(container.querySelector('rect.ges__dot--hq')).not.toBeNull();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Чарвакская ГЭС: 666 МВт/ }));
    expect(onSelect).toHaveBeenLastCalledWith(2);
    rerender(<GesMap stations={SVOD.stations} selected={2} onSelect={onSelect} />);
    const charvak = screen.getByRole('button', { name: /Чарвакская ГЭС/ });
    expect(charvak).toHaveAttribute('aria-pressed', 'true');
    charvak.focus();
    await user.keyboard('{Enter}');

    const { container } = render(<GesMap stations={stations} selected={null} onSelect={() => undefined} />);
    expect(screen.getAllByRole('button')).toHaveLength(1);
    expect(container.querySelector('.ges__dot.is-low')).not.toBeNull();
  });
});
```

- [ ] **Step 5: Implementatsiya**

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/geo.ts`:
```ts
import raw from './uz-geo.json';

export interface UzRegion {
  n: string;
  d: string;
  x?: number;
  y?: number;
  city?: boolean;
}

export interface UzGeo {
  W: number;
  H: number;
  reg: UzRegion[];
  ins: string[];
  inset: [number, number, number, number];
  main: [number, number, number, number, number];
  insp: [number, number, number, number, number, number];
}

export const UZ_GEO = raw as unknown as UzGeo;

/** Prototip `mXY`: asosiy xarita proyeksiyasi. */
export function mainXY(lon: number, lat: number): [number, number] {
  const [l0, a0, c, k, pd] = UZ_GEO.main;
  return [(lon - l0) * c * k + pd, (a0 - lat) * k + pd];
}

/** Prototip `iXY`: Toshkent viloyati kattalashtirilgan ramkasi. */
export function insetXY(lon: number, lat: number): [number, number] {
  const [l0, a0, c, k, ix, iy] = UZ_GEO.insp;
  return [ix + (lon - l0) * c * k, iy + (a0 - lat) * k];
}

export function inInset(lon: number, lat: number): boolean {
  return lon >= 68.95 && lon <= 70.15 && lat >= 40.95 && lat <= 41.75;
}

export const HIDDEN_LABELS = ['г. Ташкент', 'Хорезм', 'Андижан', 'Ташкентская'];

export const LABEL_SIDE: Record<string, 'l' | 'r' | 't' | 'b'> = {
  'UGE-01': 'r', 'UGE-02': 't', 'UGE-03': 'l', 'UGE-04': 't', 'UGE-05': 'l', 'UGE-06': 'b', 'UGE-07': 'l', 'UGE-08': 'b',
  'UGE-09': 'b', 'UGE-10': 'r', 'UGE-11': 'l', 'UGE-12': 'r', 'UGE-13': 'r', 'UGE-14': 'b', 'UGE-15': 'r', 'UGE-16': 'r',
};

export function markerRadius(capacity: number | null, inset: boolean, hq: boolean): number {
  const cap = capacity ?? 40;
  if (inset) return hq ? 6 : 3.5 + Math.sqrt(cap) * 0.33;
  return 4 + Math.sqrt(cap) * 0.55;
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/GesMap.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useId, type KeyboardEvent } from 'react';
import type { StationOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { formatMoney } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { cx } from '../../../ui/cx';
import { HIDDEN_LABELS, inInset, insetXY, LABEL_SIDE, mainXY, markerRadius, UZ_GEO } from './geo';
import './map.css';

export interface GesMapProps {
  stations: readonly StationOut[];
  selected: number | null;
  onSelect(id: number | null): void;
}

function tip(s: StationOut): string {
  if (s.kind === 'head') return t('obzor.map.tipHq', { name: s.name, balance: formatMoney(s.closing) });
  return t('obzor.map.tip', { name: s.name, capacity: formatNumber(s.capacity_mw ?? 0), outflow: formatMoney(s.outflow) });
}

function lowPlan(s: StationOut): boolean {
  const execution = s.production?.execution;
  return execution !== undefined && execution < 0.95;
}

function Marker({ s, x, y, r, on, onSelect }: { s: StationOut; x: number; y: number; r: number; on: boolean; onSelect(id: number | null): void }) {
  const hq = s.kind === 'head';
  const side = LABEL_SIDE[s.code] ?? 'r';
  const off = r + 5;
  const lx = side === 'l' ? x - off : side === 'r' ? x + off : x;
  const ly = side === 't' ? y - off - 2 : side === 'b' ? y + off + 11 : y + 4.5;
  const anchor = side === 'l' ? 'end' : side === 'r' ? 'start' : 'middle';
  const text = tip(s);
  const toggle = () => onSelect(on ? null : s.branch_id);
  const onKeyDown = (event: KeyboardEvent<SVGGElement>) => {
    if (event.key === 'Enter' || event.key === ' ') {
      event.preventDefault();
      toggle();
    }
  };
  return (
    <g className={cx('ges', on && 'is-on')} role="button" tabIndex={0} aria-pressed={on} aria-label={text} onClick={toggle} onKeyDown={onKeyDown}>
      <title>{text}</title>
      {on ? <circle className="ges__sel" cx={x} cy={y} r={r + 6} /> : null}
      <circle className="ges__ring" cx={x} cy={y} r={r + 3} />
      {hq ? (
        <rect className="ges__dot ges__dot--hq" x={x - r} y={y - r} width={2 * r} height={2 * r} rx={3} />
      ) : (
        <circle className={cx('ges__dot', lowPlan(s) && 'is-low')} cx={x} cy={y} r={r} />
      )}
      <text x={lx} y={ly} textAnchor={anchor}>
        {s.short_name}
      </text>
    </g>
  );
}

/** TZ 6 «Карта ГЭС»: stansiyalar koordinatalar bo'yicha, Toshkent viloyati — alohida ramkada. */
export function GesMap({ stations, selected, onSelect }: GesMapProps) {
  const clipId = `ins${useId().replace(/[^a-zA-Z0-9]/g, '')}`;
  const [ix, iy, iw, ih] = UZ_GEO.inset;
  const [bx0, by0] = mainXY(68.95, 41.75);
  const [bx1, by1] = mainXY(70.15, 40.95);
  const placed = stations.filter((s) => s.lat !== null && s.lon !== null);
  const anyLow = placed.some(lowPlan);
  return (
    <div className="mapwrap">
      <svg viewBox={`0 0 ${UZ_GEO.W} ${UZ_GEO.H}`} role="group" aria-label={t('obzor.map.aria')}>
        <defs>
          <clipPath id={clipId}>
            <rect x={ix} y={iy} width={iw} height={ih} rx={8} />
          </clipPath>
        </defs>
        {UZ_GEO.reg.map((r) => (
          <path key={r.n} className={cx('reg', r.city && 'reg--city')} d={r.d} />
        ))}
        {UZ_GEO.reg
          .filter((r) => r.x !== undefined && !HIDDEN_LABELS.includes(r.n))
          .map((r) => (
            <text key={`l-${r.n}`} className="regl" x={r.x} y={r.y}>
              {r.n}
            </text>
          ))}
        <rect className="map__box" x={bx0} y={by0} width={bx1 - bx0} height={by1 - by0} />
        <path className="map__box" d={`M${bx1},${by0} L${ix},${iy + ih}`} />
        {placed
          .filter((s) => inInset(s.lon ?? 0, s.lat ?? 0))
          .map((s) => {
            const [x, y] = mainXY(s.lon ?? 0, s.lat ?? 0);
            return <circle key={`c-${s.branch_id}`} className="map__cluster" cx={x} cy={y} r={2.6} />;
          })}
        <rect className="map__inset" x={ix} y={iy} width={iw} height={ih} rx={8} />
        <g clipPath={`url(#${clipId})`}>
          {UZ_GEO.ins.map((d, i) => (
            <path key={`i-${i}`} className={cx('reg', UZ_GEO.reg[i]?.city && 'reg--city')} d={d} />
          ))}
        </g>
        <text className="regl regl--start" x={ix + 8} y={iy + ih - 8}>
          {t('obzor.map.inset')}
        </text>
        {placed.map((s) => {
          const lon = s.lon ?? 0;
          const lat = s.lat ?? 0;
          const inset = inInset(lon, lat);
          const [x, y] = inset ? insetXY(lon, lat) : mainXY(lon, lat);
          return (
            <Marker key={s.branch_id} s={s} x={x} y={y} r={markerRadius(s.capacity_mw, inset, s.kind === 'head')} on={selected === s.branch_id} onSelect={onSelect} />
          );
        })}
      </svg>
      <div className="maplegend">
        <span>
          <i className="maplegend__ok" />
          {t('obzor.map.legendOk')}
        </span>
        {anyLow ? (
          <span>
            <i className="maplegend__low" />
            {t('obzor.map.legendLow')}
          </span>
        ) : null}
        <span>
          <i className="maplegend__hq" />
          {t('obzor.map.legendHq')}
        </span>
      </div>
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/StationPanel.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { StationOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { formatMoney } from '../../../dash/money';
import { Rank } from '../../../dash/Rank';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import type { FilterState } from '../../../shell/useFilters';
import { Button } from '../../../ui/Button';
import './map.css';

export interface StationPanelProps {
  stations: readonly StationOut[];
  selected: number | null;
  onSelect(id: number | null): void;
  filters: FilterState;
  onBranch(br: number | null): void;
}

function Row({ label, value, negative }: { label: string; value: number | null; negative?: boolean }) {
  return (
    <li>
      <span>{label}</span>
      <b className={negative ? 'neg' : undefined}>{formatMoney(value)}</b>
    </li>
  );
}

export function StationPanel({ stations, selected, onSelect, filters, onBranch }: StationPanelProps) {
  const station = stations.find((s) => s.branch_id === selected);
  if (!station) {
    const list = stations.filter((s) => s.kind !== 'head').sort((a, b) => b.outflow - a.outflow);
    return (
      <div className="gp">
        <div className="gp-h">
          <div>
            <h2>{t('obzor.station.all')}</h2>
            <p className="note">{t('obzor.station.allNote', { count: list.length })}</p>
          </div>
        </div>
        <Rank label={t('obzor.station.byOutflow')} items={list.map((s) => ({ id: s.branch_id, name: s.name, value: s.outflow }))} onSelect={(id) => onSelect(Number(id))} />
      </div>
    );
  }
  const hq = station.kind === 'head';
  const overdueShare = station.payable ? (station.overdue_payable ?? 0) / station.payable : 0;
  return (
    <div className="gp">
      <div className="gp-h">
        <h2>{station.name}</h2>
        <button type="button" className="gp-x" onClick={() => onSelect(null)}>
          {t('obzor.station.all')}
        </button>
      </div>
      {hq ? (
        <p className="note">{t('obzor.station.hqNote')}</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="gp-grid">
            <div>
              <span>{t('obzor.station.capacity')}</span>
              <b>
                {formatNumber(station.capacity_mw ?? 0)}
                <small>{t('obzor.station.mw')}</small>
              </b>
            </div>
          </div>
          <p className="note">{t('obzor.station.noProduction')}</p>
        </>
      )}
      <ul className="gp-rows">
        <Row label={t('obzor.station.inflow')} value={station.inflow} />
        <Row label={t('obzor.station.outflow')} value={station.outflow} />
        <Row label={t('obzor.station.closing')} value={station.closing} />
        <Row label={t('obzor.station.receivable')} value={station.receivable} />
        <Row label={t('obzor.station.payable')} value={station.payable} />
        <Row label={t('obzor.station.overdue')} value={station.overdue_payable} negative={overdueShare > 0.2} />
      </ul>
      {filters.br === station.branch_id ? null : (
        <Button variant="secondary" onClick={() => onBranch(station.branch_id)}>
          {hq ? t('obzor.station.openHq') : t('obzor.station.openStation')}
        </Button>
      )}
    </div>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/map/map.css` (prototip «GES map» bloki):
```css
.mapwrap { position: relative; }
.mapwrap svg { display: block; width: 100%; height: auto; }
.reg { fill: var(--soft); stroke: var(--mapline); stroke-width: 1; }
.reg--city { fill: var(--card); }
.regl { font-size: 12px; fill: var(--muted); text-anchor: middle; }
.regl--start { text-anchor: start; }
.map__box { fill: none; stroke: var(--brand2); stroke-width: 1.4; stroke-dasharray: 4 3; }
.map__cluster { fill: var(--brand2); }
.map__inset { fill: var(--card); stroke: var(--brand2); stroke-width: 1.5; }
.ges { cursor: pointer; }
.ges:focus { outline: none; }
.ges:focus-visible .ges__ring { stroke: var(--focus); stroke-width: 3; }
.ges__ring { fill: transparent; }
.ges__sel { fill: none; stroke: var(--brand2); stroke-width: 2.5; }
.ges__dot { fill: var(--brand2); stroke: var(--card); stroke-width: 2; }
.ges__dot.is-low { fill: var(--neg); }
.ges__dot--hq { fill: var(--brand); }
.ges:hover .ges__dot { stroke: var(--ink); }
.ges text { font-size: 13px; font-weight: 600; fill: var(--ink); paint-order: stroke; stroke: var(--card); stroke-width: 4px; stroke-linejoin: round; }
.ges.is-on text { fill: var(--brand2); }
.maplegend { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 0.4rem 1.3rem; font-size: 0.9rem; color: var(--muted); margin-top: 0.6rem; }
.maplegend span { display: inline-flex; align-items: center; gap: 0.4rem; }
.maplegend i { display: inline-block; width: 0.8rem; height: 0.8rem; border-radius: 50%; }
.maplegend__ok { background: var(--brand2); }
.maplegend__low { background: var(--neg); }
.maplegend__hq { background: var(--brand); border-radius: 0.15rem !important; }
.gp { display: flex; flex-direction: column; gap: 0.6rem; height: 100%; }
.gp-h { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; align-items: flex-start; gap: 0.8rem; }
.gp-h h2 { margin: 0; font-size: 1.35rem; }
.gp-x { border: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--card); border-radius: 0.45rem; padding: 0.35rem 0.7rem; cursor: pointer; color: var(--brand2); font-weight: 600; white-space: nowrap; min-height: 44px; }
.gp-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: 1fr 1fr; gap: 0.8rem 1rem; }
.gp-grid span { display: block; font-size: 0.88rem; color: var(--muted); }
.gp-grid b { font-size: 1.45rem; font-weight: 700; white-space: nowrap; }
.gp-grid small { font-size: 0.6em; color: var(--muted); margin-left: 0.2rem; }
.gp-rows { list-style: none; margin: 0; padding: 0; }
.gp-rows li { display: flex; justify-content: space-between; gap: 1rem; padding: 0.5rem 0; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); }
.gp-rows li:last-child { border-bottom: 0; }
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/svod.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../../test/axe';
import { ME_ADMIN } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { apiError, json, mockApi } from '../../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../test/render';
import { ITEM_BRANCHES, SVOD } from '../../test/s1Fixtures';
import ObzorSvod from './svod';

const filters = { br: null, per: 'm08' };

describe('Обзор / Сводка', () => {
  it('shows six KPIs with YoY and formula hints, comparison, map and rankings', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/svod': json(SVOD), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    const { container } = renderWithProviders(<ObzorSvod section="obzor" page="svod" filters={filters} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Начальный остаток')).toBeInTheDocument();
    for (const label of ['Поступления', 'Расходование', 'Чистый денежный поток', 'Конечный остаток', 'Разница']) {
      expect(screen.getAllByText(label).length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    }
    expect(screen.getByText(/к 2025: \+25,0%/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Разница = конечный остаток − \(начальный остаток \+ ЧДП\)/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Сравнение с 2025 годом' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('group', { name: 'Карта Узбекистана с расположением ГЭС' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('img', { name: 'Движение денежных средств по месяцам' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });

  it('selects a branch from the ranking and opens an item breakdown', async () => {
    const onBranch = vi.fn();
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/svod': json(SVOD), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN), 'GET /obzor/items/22': json(ITEM_BRANCHES) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<ObzorSvod section="obzor" page="svod" filters={filters} onBranch={onBranch} />);
    const ranking = await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Расходование по филиалам' });
    await user.click(within(ranking).getByRole('button', { name: /Чарвакская ГЭС/ }));
    expect(onBranch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
    const top = screen.getByRole('list', { name: 'Топ-5 статей расходования' });
    await user.click(within(top).getByRole('button', { name: /Прочие операционные расходы/ }));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Прочие операционные расходы' });
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('Каскад Чирчикских ГЭС')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the error banner with retry', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/svod': apiError(500, 'internal_error', 'Внутренняя ошибка сервера'), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    renderWithProviders(<ObzorSvod section="obzor" page="svod" filters={filters} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(await screen.findByRole('alert')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Повторить' })).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows no-data states when the period is empty and balances are missing', async () => {
    const empty = { ...SVOD, kpis: { ...SVOD.kpis, has_data: false, opening: null, closing: null, diff: null } };
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/svod': json(empty), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    renderWithProviders(<ObzorSvod section="obzor" page="svod" filters={filters} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Нет данных за выбранный период. Выберите другой период или филиал в фильтрах.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByText('нет данных').length).toBeGreaterThanOrEqual(3);
  });
});
```

```ts
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../i18n';

export function monthShort(monthId: number): string {
  return t(`dash.m${monthId % 100}` as MessageKey);
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/parts/CashKpis.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { CashKpisOut, ReportPeriodOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { formula } from '../../../dash/formulas';
import { formatDelta, moneyParts, yoyRatio } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatDate } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { KpiGrid, KpiTile } from '../../../ui/KpiTile';
import type { IconName } from '../../../ui/icons';

function yoy(cur: number | null, prev: number | null, year: number): { text: string; down: boolean } | null {
  if (cur === null) return null;
  const ratio = yoyRatio(cur, prev);
  return ratio === null ? null : { text: t('dash.yoy', { year, delta: formatDelta(ratio) }), down: ratio < 0 };
}

function Tile({ icon, label, value, note, change, hint }: { icon: IconName; label: string; value: number | null; note: string; change: { text: string; down: boolean } | null; hint: string }) {
  const parts = value === null ? null : moneyParts(value);
  return (
    <KpiTile
      icon={icon}
      label={label}
      value={parts ? parts.value : t('dash.noValue')}
      unit={parts?.unit}
      negative={value !== null && value < 0}
      delta={change ? `${note} · ${change.text}` : note}
      deltaDown={change?.down}
      hint={`${hint}. ${formula('yoy')}`}
    />
  );
}

/** TZ 5 «Сводка»: шесть показателей движения денежных средств с изменением к прошлому году. */
export function CashKpis({ kpis, period }: { kpis: CashKpisOut; period: ReportPeriodOut }) {
  const year = Number(period.date_from.slice(0, 4)) - 1;
  return (
    <KpiGrid>
      <Tile icon="cal" label={t('obzor.kpi.opening')} value={kpis.opening} note={t('obzor.kpi.at', { date: formatDate(period.date_from) })} change={yoy(kpis.opening, kpis.opening_prev, year)} hint={formula('opening')} />
      <Tile icon="in" label={t('obzor.kpi.inflow')} value={kpis.inflow} note={t('obzor.kpi.period')} change={yoy(kpis.inflow, kpis.inflow_prev, year)} hint={formula('inflow')} />
      <Tile icon="out" label={t('obzor.kpi.outflow')} value={kpis.outflow} note={t('obzor.kpi.period')} change={yoy(kpis.outflow, kpis.outflow_prev, year)} hint={formula('outflow')} />
      <Tile icon="net" label={t('obzor.kpi.net')} value={kpis.net} note={t('obzor.kpi.netNote')} change={yoy(kpis.net, kpis.net_prev, year)} hint={formula('net')} />
      <Tile icon="check" label={t('obzor.kpi.closing')} value={kpis.closing} note={t('obzor.kpi.closingAt', { date: formatDate(period.date_to) })} change={yoy(kpis.closing, kpis.closing_prev, year)} hint={formula('closing')} />
      <Tile icon="diff" label={t('obzor.kpi.diff')} value={kpis.diff} note={t('obzor.kpi.diffNote')} change={yoy(kpis.diff, kpis.diff_prev, year)} hint={formula('diff')} />
    </KpiGrid>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/parts/CompareCards.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import type { CashKpisOut, MonthPointOut, ReportPeriodOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import { barConfig, ChartCanvas, COLORS, type ChartHandle } from '../../../dash/chart';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { formatDelta, formatMoney, yoyRatio } from '../../../dash/money';
import { monthShort } from '../../../dash/months';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';

export function CompareCards({ kpis, months, period, ctx }: { kpis: CashKpisOut; months: MonthPointOut[]; period: ReportPeriodOut; ctx: ExportContext }) {
  const year = Number(period.date_from.slice(0, 4));
  const rows: Array<[string, number, number]> = [
    [t('obzor.kpi.inflow'), kpis.inflow_prev, kpis.inflow],
    [t('obzor.kpi.outflow'), kpis.outflow_prev, kpis.outflow],
    [t('obzor.kpi.net'), kpis.net_prev, kpis.net],
  ];
  const title = t('obzor.compare.title', { year: year - 1 });
  const chartTitle = t('obzor.compare.chartTitle', { year, prev: year - 1 });
  const chartRef = useRef<ChartHandle | null>(null);
  const config = useMemo(
    () =>
      barConfig({
        labels: months.map((m) => monthShort(m.month_id)),
        series: [
          { label: String(year), data: months.map((m) => m.inflow), color: COLORS.in() },
          { label: String(year - 1), data: months.map((m) => m.inflow_prev), color: COLORS.prev() },
        ],
      }),
    [months, year],
  );
  const table = {
    headers: [t('dash.month'), String(year), String(year - 1)],
    rows: months.map((m) => [monthShort(m.month_id), m.inflow, m.inflow_prev]),
  };
  return (
    <>
      <Card
        className="c6"
        title={title}
        sub={period.open_last ? t('obzor.compare.subOpen') : t('obzor.compare.sub')}
        exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} csv={() => ({ headers: [t('obzor.compare.indicator'), String(year - 1), String(year)], rows: rows.map(([n, a, b]) => [n, a, b]) })} />}
      >
        <table className="cmp">
          <caption className="visually-hidden">{title}</caption>
          <thead>
            <tr>
              <th scope="col">{t('obzor.compare.indicator')}</th>
              <th scope="col">{year - 1}</th>
              <th scope="col">{year}</th>
              <th scope="col">{t('obzor.compare.change')}</th>
            </tr>
          </thead>
          <tbody>
            {rows.map(([name, prev, cur]) => {
              const ratio = yoyRatio(cur, prev);
              return (
                <tr key={name}>
                  <th scope="row">{name}</th>
                  <td>{formatMoney(prev)}</td>
                  <td>
                    <b>{formatMoney(cur)}</b>
                  </td>
                  <td className={ratio !== null && ratio < 0 ? 'neg' : undefined}>{ratio === null ? '—' : formatDelta(ratio)}</td>
                </tr>
              );
            })}
          </tbody>
        </table>
      </Card>
      <Card className="c6" title={chartTitle} sub={t('obzor.compare.chartSub')} exportSlot={<CardExport title={chartTitle} ctx={ctx} chartRef={chartRef} csv={() => table} />}>
        <ChartCanvas config={config} label={chartTitle} table={table} size="sm" chartRef={chartRef} />
      </Card>
    </>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/parts/FlowCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import type { MonthPointOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import { barConfig, ChartCanvas, COLORS, type ChartHandle } from '../../../dash/chart';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { monthShort } from '../../../dash/months';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';

export function FlowCard({ months, ctx, className }: { months: MonthPointOut[]; ctx: ExportContext; className?: string }) {
  const title = t('obzor.flow.title');
  const chartRef = useRef<ChartHandle | null>(null);
  const config = useMemo(
    () =>
      barConfig({
        labels: months.map((m) => monthShort(m.month_id)),
        series: [
          { label: t('obzor.kpi.inflow'), data: months.map((m) => m.inflow), color: COLORS.in() },
          { label: t('obzor.kpi.outflow'), data: months.map((m) => m.outflow), color: COLORS.out() },
          { label: t('obzor.flow.balance'), data: months.map((m) => m.closing ?? Number.NaN), color: COLORS.bal(), type: 'line' },
        ],
      }),
    [months],
  );
  const table = {
    headers: [t('dash.month'), t('obzor.kpi.inflow'), t('obzor.kpi.outflow'), t('obzor.flow.balance')],
    rows: months.map((m) => [monthShort(m.month_id), m.inflow, m.outflow, m.closing]),
  };
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title} sub={t('obzor.flow.sub')} exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} chartRef={chartRef} csv={() => table} />}>
      <div className="legend">
        <span><i style={{ background: COLORS.in() }} />{t('obzor.kpi.inflow')}</span>
        <span><i style={{ background: COLORS.out() }} />{t('obzor.kpi.outflow')}</span>
        <span><i className="is-line" style={{ background: COLORS.bal() }} />{t('obzor.flow.balance')}</span>
      </div>
      <ChartCanvas config={config} label={title} table={table} chartRef={chartRef} />
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/parts/ActivityCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ActivityAmountOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { formatMoney, formatPct } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../../../i18n';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';

const COLORS: Record<string, string> = { centralized: 'var(--v0)', operating: 'var(--v1)', investing: 'var(--v2)' };

export function ActivityCard({ activity, className }: { activity: ActivityAmountOut[]; className?: string }) {
  const total = activity.reduce((s, a) => s + a.amount, 0);
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={t('obzor.activity.title')} sub={t('obzor.activity.sub')}>
      <div className="split" aria-hidden="true">
        {activity
          .filter((a) => a.amount > 0)
          .map((a) => (
            <div key={a.activity} style={{ flexGrow: a.amount, background: COLORS[a.activity] }}>
              {total && a.amount / total > 0.08 ? formatPct(a.amount / total, 0) : ''}
            </div>
          ))}
      </div>
      <ul className="vt">
        {activity.map((a) => (
          <li key={a.activity}>
            <i style={{ background: COLORS[a.activity] }} />
            <span>
              {t(`dash.activity.${a.activity}` as MessageKey)}
              <small>{t('obzor.activity.share', { share: formatPct(total ? a.amount / total : null) })}</small>
            </span>
            <b>{formatMoney(a.amount)}</b>
          </li>
        ))}
      </ul>
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/parts/BranchAmountTable.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { BranchAmountOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { formatMoney, formatPct } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import type { FilterState } from '../../../shell/useFilters';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';
import { Table, type Column } from '../../../ui/Table';
import { toggleBranch } from '../../useSection';

export interface BranchAmountTableProps {
  title: string;
  label: string;
  rows: BranchAmountOut[];
  filters: FilterState;
  onBranch(br: number | null): void;
  ctx: ExportContext;
  className?: string;
}

/** TZ 6: filial qatorini bosish filtrni tanlaydi, qayta bosish — olib tashlaydi. */
export function BranchAmountTable({ title, label, rows, filters, onBranch, ctx, className }: BranchAmountTableProps) {
  const total = rows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.amount, 0);
  const max = Math.max(1e-9, ...rows.map((r) => r.amount));
  const columns: Column<BranchAmountOut>[] = [
    { key: 'name', header: t('dash.branch'), cell: (r) => r.name, sortValue: (r) => r.name, wrap: true },
    { key: 'amount', header: label, cell: (r) => formatMoney(r.amount), sortValue: (r) => r.amount, align: 'right' },
    {
      key: 'share',
      header: t('dash.share'),
      align: 'right',
      cell: (r) => (
        <>
          {formatPct(total ? r.amount / total : null)}
          <span className="mini" aria-hidden="true">
            <i style={{ width: `${(r.amount / max) * 100}%` }} />
          </span>
        </>
      ),
    },
  ];
  return (
    <Card
      className={className}
      title={title}
      sub={t('obzor.branches.sub')}
      exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} csv={() => ({ headers: [t('dash.branch'), label], rows: rows.map((r) => [r.name, r.amount]) })} />}
    >
      <Table caption={title} columns={columns} rows={rows} rowKey={(r) => r.branch_id} onRowClick={(r) => toggleBranch(filters, r.branch_id, onBranch)} activeKey={filters.br} free />
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/parts/ItemDialog.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { ItemAmountOut, ItemBranchesOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { Rank } from '../../../dash/Rank';
import { formatMoney, formatPct } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import type { FilterState } from '../../../shell/useFilters';
import { ErrorBanner } from '../../../ui/ErrorBanner';
import { Modal } from '../../../ui/Modal';
import { Skeleton } from '../../../ui/Skeleton';
import { useSection } from '../../useSection';

/** Prototip `openList`: statya → filiallar (reja bilan) — «Обзор» va «Исполнение платежей». */
export function ItemDialog({ path, filters, onClose }: { path: string; filters: FilterState; onClose(): void }) {
  const query = useSection<ItemBranchesOut>(path, filters);
  const data = query.data;
  const out = data?.item.direction === 'out';
  const max = Math.max(1, ...(data?.rows.map((r) => r.fact) ?? [1]));
  return (
    <Modal
      open
      onClose={onClose}
      title={data?.item.name ?? '…'}
      note={data ? t('obzor.item.note', { direction: out ? t('obzor.item.out') : t('obzor.item.in'), period: data.period.label.toLowerCase() }) : undefined}
    >
      {query.isError ? <ErrorBanner error={query.error} onRetry={() => void query.refetch()} /> : null}
      {!data ? (
        <Skeleton variant="card" />
      ) : data.rows.length === 0 ? (
        <p className="note">{t('obzor.item.empty')}</p>
      ) : (
        <>
          <p className="bigline">
            <b>{formatMoney(data.total, 2)}</b>
          </p>
          <table className="cmp">
            <caption className="visually-hidden">{data.item.name}</caption>
            <thead>
              <tr>
                <th scope="col">{t('dash.branch')}</th>
                {out ? <th scope="col">{t('dash.plan')}</th> : null}
                <th scope="col">{t('dash.fact')}</th>
                <th scope="col">{t('dash.share')}</th>
              </tr>
            </thead>
            <tbody>
              {data.rows.map((r) => (
                <tr key={r.branch_id}>
                  <th scope="row">{r.name}</th>
                  {out ? <td>{formatMoney(r.plan)}</td> : null}
                  <td>{formatMoney(r.fact)}</td>
                  <td>
                    {formatPct(data.total ? r.fact / data.total : null)}
                    <span className="mini" aria-hidden="true">
                      <i style={{ width: `${(r.fact / max) * 100}%` }} />
                    </span>
                  </td>
                </tr>
              ))}
            </tbody>
          </table>
        </>
      )}
    </Modal>
  );
}

export function AllItemsDialog({ title, items, onPick, onClose }: { title: string; items: ItemAmountOut[]; onPick(id: number): void; onClose(): void }) {
  return (
    <Modal open onClose={onClose} title={title} note={t('obzor.top.sub')}>
      <Rank label={title} items={items.map((i) => ({ id: i.item_id, name: i.name, value: i.amount }))} onSelect={(id) => onPick(Number(id))} />
    </Modal>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { ObzorSvodOut } from '../../api/types';
import { NoData } from '../../dash/DataState';
import { Rank } from '../../dash/Rank';
import { t } from '../../i18n';
import { buildHash } from '../../router/hash';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import type { SectionPageProps } from '../registry';
import { SectionData } from '../SectionData';
import { toggleBranch, useSection } from '../useSection';
import { GesMap } from './map/GesMap';
import { StationPanel } from './map/StationPanel';
import { ActivityCard } from './parts/ActivityCard';
import { CashKpis } from './parts/CashKpis';
import { CompareCards } from './parts/CompareCards';
import { FlowCard } from './parts/FlowCard';
import { AllItemsDialog, ItemDialog } from './parts/ItemDialog';

export default function ObzorSvod({ section, page, filters, onBranch }: SectionPageProps) {
  const query = useSection<ObzorSvodOut>('/obzor/svod', filters);
  const [item, setItem] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const [all, setAll] = useState<'in' | 'out' | null>(null);
  const [mapSel, setMapSel] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const ctx = { section, page, br: filters.br, per: filters.per };
  return (
    <SectionData query={query}>
      {(data) => {
        const topBranches = [...data.branches].sort((a, b) => b.outflow - a.outflow).slice(0, 8);
        return (
          <>
            <CashKpis kpis={data.kpis} period={data.period} />
            {data.kpis.has_data ? null : (
              <Card>
                <NoData kind="period" />
              </Card>
            )}
            <div className="grid">
              <CompareCards kpis={data.kpis} months={data.months} period={data.period} ctx={ctx} />
            </div>
            <div className="grid">
              <Card className="c8 wide" title={t('obzor.map.title')} sub={t('obzor.map.sub')}>
                <GesMap stations={data.stations} selected={mapSel ?? filters.br} onSelect={setMapSel} />
              </Card>
              <Card className="c4 wide">
                <StationPanel stations={data.stations} selected={mapSel ?? filters.br} onSelect={setMapSel} filters={filters} onBranch={onBranch} />
              </Card>
            </div>
            <div className="grid">
              <FlowCard months={data.months} ctx={ctx} className="c8" />
              <ActivityCard activity={data.activity} className="c4" />
              <Card className="c4" title={t('obzor.branchesOut.title')} sub={filters.br === null ? t('obzor.branchesOut.subAll') : t('obzor.branchesOut.subOne')}>
                <Rank
                  label={t('obzor.branchesOut.title')}
                  items={topBranches.map((b) => ({ id: b.branch_id, name: b.name, value: b.outflow }))}
                  onSelect={(id) => toggleBranch(filters, Number(id), onBranch)}
                  activeId={filters.br}
                />
                {filters.br === null ? (
                  <a className="more" href={buildHash({ kind: 'section', section: 'obzor', page: 'rasxod', br: null, per: filters.per })}>
                    {t('obzor.allBranches')}
                  </a>
                ) : null}
              </Card>
              <Card className="c4" title={t('obzor.topIn.title')} sub={t('obzor.top.sub')}>
                <Rank label={t('obzor.topIn.title')} items={data.items_in.slice(0, 5).map((i) => ({ id: i.item_id, name: i.name, value: i.amount }))} onSelect={(id) => setItem(Number(id))} />
                <button type="button" className="more" onClick={() => setAll('in')}>
                  {t('obzor.allItems')}
                </button>
              </Card>
              <Card className="c4" title={t('obzor.topOut.title')} sub={t('obzor.top.sub')}>
                <Rank label={t('obzor.topOut.title')} items={data.items_out.slice(0, 5).map((i) => ({ id: i.item_id, name: i.name, value: i.amount }))} onSelect={(id) => setItem(Number(id))} />
                <button type="button" className="more" onClick={() => setAll('out')}>
                  {t('obzor.allItems')}
                </button>
              </Card>
            </div>
            {all ? (
              <AllItemsDialog
                title={all === 'in' ? t('obzor.allItemsIn') : t('obzor.allItemsOut')}
                items={all === 'in' ? data.items_in : data.items_out}
                onPick={(id) => {
                  setAll(null);
                  setItem(id);
                }}
                onClose={() => setAll(null)}
              />
            ) : null}
            {item !== null ? <ItemDialog path={`/obzor/items/${item}`} filters={filters} onClose={() => setItem(null)} /> : null}
          </>
        );
      }}
    </SectionData>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/parts/SeriesChart.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import type { ObzorFlowOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import { barConfig, ChartCanvas, COLORS, type ChartHandle } from '../../../dash/chart';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { monthShort } from '../../../dash/months';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';

const IN_SHADES = [COLORS.bal, COLORS.v1, COLORS.v2, COLORS.prev];
const OUT_SHADES = [COLORS.v0, COLORS.v1, COLORS.v2];

/** Oylik stacked grafik: Поступления — 3 statya + qolganlari, Расходование — faoliyat turlari. */
export function SeriesChart({ data, title, sub, ctx, className }: { data: ObzorFlowOut; title: string; sub: string; ctx: ExportContext; className: string }) {
  const chartRef = useRef<ChartHandle | null>(null);
  const colors = useMemo(() => {
    const shades = data.direction === 'out' ? OUT_SHADES : IN_SHADES;
    return data.series.map((_, i) => (shades[i] ?? COLORS.prev)());
  }, [data]);
  const config = useMemo(
    () => barConfig({ labels: data.months.map(monthShort), series: data.series.map((s, i) => ({ label: s.name, data: s.values, color: colors[i] ?? '#999999' })), stacked: true }),
    [data, colors],
  );
  const table = { headers: [t('dash.month'), ...data.series.map((s) => s.name)], rows: data.months.map((m, i) => [monthShort(m), ...data.series.map((s) => s.values[i] ?? 0)]) };
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={title} sub={sub} exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} chartRef={chartRef} csv={() => table} />}>
      <div className="legend">
        {data.series.map((s, i) => (
          <span key={s.key}>
            <i style={{ background: colors[i] }} />
            {s.name}
          </span>
        ))}
      </div>
      <ChartCanvas config={config} label={title} table={table} chartRef={chartRef} />
    </Card>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { ObzorFlowOut } from '../../api/types';
import { NoData } from '../../dash/DataState';
import { Rank } from '../../dash/Rank';
import { t } from '../../i18n';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import type { SectionPageProps } from '../registry';
import { SectionData } from '../SectionData';
import { useSection } from '../useSection';
import { BranchAmountTable } from './parts/BranchAmountTable';
import { CashKpis } from './parts/CashKpis';
import { ItemDialog } from './parts/ItemDialog';
import { SeriesChart } from './parts/SeriesChart';

export default function ObzorPostup({ section, page, filters, onBranch }: SectionPageProps) {
  const query = useSection<ObzorFlowOut>('/obzor/postup', filters);
  const [item, setItem] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const ctx = { section, page, br: filters.br, per: filters.per };
  return (
    <SectionData query={query}>
      {(data) => (
        <>
          <CashKpis kpis={data.kpis} period={data.period} />
          <div className="grid">
            <SeriesChart data={data} title={t('obzor.postup.monthly')} sub={t('obzor.postup.monthlySub')} ctx={ctx} className="c8" />
            <Card className="c4" title={t('obzor.postup.items')} sub={t('obzor.top.sub')}>
              {data.items.length ? (
                <Rank label={t('obzor.postup.items')} items={data.items.map((i) => ({ id: i.item_id, name: i.name, value: i.amount }))} onSelect={(id) => setItem(Number(id))} />
              ) : (
                <NoData kind="period" />
              )}
            </Card>
            <Card className="c6" title={t('obzor.postup.banks')} sub={t('obzor.postup.banksSub')}>
              <Rank label={t('obzor.postup.banks')} items={data.banks.map((b) => ({ id: b.bank_name, name: b.bank_name, value: b.amount }))} />
            </Card>
            <BranchAmountTable className="c6" title={t('obzor.postup.branches')} label={t('obzor.kpi.inflow')} rows={data.branches} filters={filters} onBranch={onBranch} ctx={ctx} />
          </div>
          {item !== null ? <ItemDialog path={`/obzor/items/${item}`} filters={filters} onClose={() => setItem(null)} /> : null}
        </>
      )}
    </SectionData>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { screen } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../../test/axe';
import { json, mockApi } from '../../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../test/render';
import { ALERTS } from '../../test/s1Fixtures';
import ObzorVnimanie from './vnimanie';

describe('Обзор / Требует внимания', () => {
  it('lists fired rules critical first and navigates with the same filters', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/vnimanie': json(ALERTS) });
    const { user, container } = renderWithProviders(<ObzorVnimanie section="obzor" page="vnimanie" filters={{ br: 3, per: 'm08' }} onBranch={vi.fn()} />, { hash: '#obzor/vnimanie?br=3&per=m08' });
    const buttons = await screen.findAllByRole('button', { name: /Открыть/ });
    expect(buttons[0]).toHaveAccessibleName(/Критично.*Просроченные договоры/);
    expect(screen.getByText('Проверено правил: 2')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
    await user.click(buttons[1]!);
    expect(window.location.hash).toBe('#kazna/platezhi?br=3&per=m08');
  });

  it('says that nothing fired', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/vnimanie': json({ ...ALERTS, items: [] }) });
    renderWithProviders(<ObzorVnimanie section="obzor" page="vnimanie" filters={{ br: null, per: 'ytd' }} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Отклонений не обнаружено: проверенные правила не сработали.')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import { act, screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { ME_ADMIN, ME_BRANCH } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { json, mockApi } from '../../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../test/render';
import { EXCEL } from '../../test/s1Fixtures';
import ObzorExcel from './excel';

describe('Обзор / Таблица Excel', () => {
  it('shows the branch summary with totals and an xlsx link', async () => {
    const onBranch = vi.fn();
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/excel': json(EXCEL), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<ObzorExcel section="obzor" page="excel" filters={{ br: null, per: 'm08' }} onBranch={onBranch} />);
    const summary = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Сводка по филиалам' });
    expect(within(summary).getByText('Итого')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(summary).getAllByText('1 060,0').length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    const link = screen.getByRole('link', { name: 'Скачать Excel' });
    expect(link.getAttribute('href')).toBe('/api/v1/obzor/excel.xlsx?per=m08');
    await user.click(within(summary).getByText('Чарвакская ГЭС'));
    expect(onBranch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(2);
  });

  it('filters detail by direction and search', async () => {
    vi.useFakeTimers({ shouldAdvanceTime: true });
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/excel': json(EXCEL), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<ObzorExcel section="obzor" page="excel" filters={{ br: null, per: 'm08' }} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    const detail = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Детализация по статьям' });
    expect(within(detail).getByText('Строк: 3')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Расходование' }));
    expect(within(detail).getByText('Строк: 2')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.type(screen.getByRole('searchbox', { name: 'Поиск по филиалу или статье' }), 'чирчик');
    await act(() => vi.advanceTimersByTimeAsync(300));
    expect(within(detail).getByText('Строк: 1')).toBeInTheDocument();
    vi.useRealTimers();
  });

  it('hides the xlsx link without export permission', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /obzor/excel': json(EXCEL), 'GET /auth/me': json({ ...ME_BRANCH, permissions: ['section:obzor:view'] }) });
    renderWithProviders(<ObzorExcel section="obzor" page="excel" filters={{ br: 3, per: 'm08' }} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Сводка по филиалам' });
    expect(screen.queryByRole('link', { name: 'Скачать Excel' })).toBeNull();
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/vnimanie.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { AlertsOut } from '../../api/types';
import { t } from '../../i18n';
import { navigate } from '../../router/store';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import type { SectionPageProps } from '../registry';
import { SectionData } from '../SectionData';
import { useSection } from '../useSection';

/** TZ 5: все сработавшие правила, критичные первыми; нажатие ведёт в раздел с теми же фильтрами. */
export default function ObzorVnimanie({ filters }: SectionPageProps) {
  const query = useSection<AlertsOut>('/obzor/vnimanie', filters);
  return (
    <SectionData query={query}>
      {(data) => (
        <Card
          sub={
            data.items.length
              ? t('obzor.alerts.sub', {
                  count: data.items.length,
                  scope: filters.br === null ? t('obzor.alerts.scopeAll') : t('obzor.alerts.scopeOne'),
                  period: data.period.label.toLowerCase(),
                })
              : undefined
          }
        >
          {data.items.length ? (
            <ul className="alert-list">
              {data.items.map((a) => (
                <li key={a.code}>
                  <button
                    type="button"
                    aria-label={`${a.level === 'critical' ? t('obzor.alerts.critical') : t('obzor.alerts.warning')}: ${a.title}. ${a.message}. ${t('obzor.alerts.open')}`}
                    onClick={() => navigate({ kind: 'section', section: a.section, page: a.page, br: filters.br, per: filters.per })}
                  >
                    <i className={a.level === 'critical' ? 'is-critical' : undefined} aria-hidden="true" />
                    <span>
                      <span className="at">{a.title}</span>
                      <span className="am">{a.message}</span>
                    </span>
                    <span className="ag" aria-hidden="true">
                      {t('obzor.alerts.open')}
                    </span>
                  </button>
                </li>
              ))}
            </ul>
          ) : (
            <p className="note">{t('obzor.alerts.none')}</p>
          )}
          <p className="note">{t('obzor.alerts.checked', { count: data.checked.length })}</p>
        </Card>
      )}
    </SectionData>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/obzor/excel.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useEffect, useMemo, useState } from 'react';
import { apiUrl } from '../../api/client';
import type { BranchCashOut, DetailRowOut, ObzorExcelOut } from '../../api/types';
import { useMe } from '../../auth/api';
import { CardExport } from '../../dash/CardExport';
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../../i18n';
import { formatNumber } from '../../i18n/format';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import { Table, type Column } from '../../ui/Table';
import type { SectionPageProps } from '../registry';
import { SectionData } from '../SectionData';
import { sectionQuery, toggleBranch, useSection } from '../useSection';
import { CashKpis } from './parts/CashKpis';

type Dir = 'all' | 'in' | 'out';
const m1 = (v: number | null) => (v === null ? '—' : formatNumber(v, 1));

function summaryColumns(): Column<BranchCashOut>[] {
  const num = (key: keyof BranchCashOut, header: string): Column<BranchCashOut> => ({
    key,
    header,
    align: 'right',
    sortValue: (r) => r[key] as number | null,
    cell: (r) => {
      const v = r[key] as number | null;
      return <span className={v !== null && v < 0 && (key === 'net' || key === 'diff') ? 'neg' : undefined}>{m1(v)}</span>;
    },
  });
  return [
    { key: 'name', header: t('dash.branch'), cell: (r) => r.name, sortValue: (r) => r.name, wrap: true },
    num('opening', t('obzor.kpi.opening')),
    num('inflow', t('obzor.kpi.inflow')),
    num('outflow', t('obzor.kpi.outflow')),
    num('net', t('obzor.kpi.net')),
    num('closing', t('obzor.kpi.closing')),
    num('diff', t('obzor.kpi.diff')),
  ];
}

function detailColumns(): Column<DetailRowOut>[] {
  return [
    { key: 'branch', header: t('dash.branch'), cell: (r) => r.branch_name, sortValue: (r) => r.branch_name, wrap: true },
    { key: 'dir', header: t('obzor.excel.direction'), cell: (r) => (r.direction === 'in' ? t('obzor.excel.dirInOne') : t('obzor.excel.dirOutOne')), sortValue: (r) => r.direction },
    { key: 'item', header: t('dash.item'), cell: (r) => r.item_name, sortValue: (r) => r.item_name, wrap: true },
    { key: 'activity', header: t('obzor.excel.activity'), cell: (r) => (r.direction === 'in' ? '–' : t(`dash.activity.${r.activity}` as MessageKey)), sortValue: (r) => r.activity },
    { key: 'amount', header: t('obzor.excel.amount'), cell: (r) => m1(r.amount), sortValue: (r) => r.amount, align: 'right' },
  ];
}

function Detail({ rows, ctx }: { rows: DetailRowOut[]; ctx: { section: string; page: string; br: number | null; per: string } }) {
  const [dir, setDir] = useState<Dir>('all');
  const [text, setText] = useState('');
  const [q, setQ] = useState('');
  useEffect(() => {
    const id = window.setTimeout(() => setQ(text.trim().toLowerCase()), 250);
    return () => window.clearTimeout(id);
  }, [text]);
  const filtered = useMemo(
    () => rows.filter((r) => (dir === 'all' || r.direction === dir) && (!q || `${r.branch_name} ${r.item_name}`.toLowerCase().includes(q))),
    [rows, dir, q],
  );
  const saldo = filtered.reduce((s, r) => s + (r.direction === 'in' ? r.amount : -r.amount), 0);
  const title = t('obzor.excel.detail');
  const dirs: Array<[Dir, MessageKey]> = [['all', 'obzor.excel.dirAll'], ['in', 'obzor.excel.dirIn'], ['out', 'obzor.excel.dirOut']];
  return (
    <Card
      title={title}
      actions={
        <div className="toolbar">
          <div className="seg" role="group" aria-label={t('obzor.excel.direction')}>
            {dirs.map(([value, key]) => (
              <button key={value} type="button" aria-pressed={dir === value} onClick={() => setDir(value)}>
                {t(key)}
              </button>
            ))}
          </div>
          <input className="search" type="search" value={text} onChange={(e) => setText(e.target.value)} placeholder={t('obzor.excel.search')} aria-label={t('obzor.excel.search')} />
        </div>
      }
      exportSlot={
        <CardExport
          title={title}
          ctx={ctx}
          csv={() => ({
            headers: [t('dash.branch'), t('obzor.excel.direction'), t('dash.item'), t('obzor.excel.activity'), t('obzor.excel.amount')],
            rows: filtered.map((r) => [r.branch_name, r.direction === 'in' ? t('obzor.excel.dirInOne') : t('obzor.excel.dirOutOne'), r.item_name, r.direction === 'in' ? '–' : t(`dash.activity.${r.activity}` as MessageKey), r.amount]),
          })}
        />
      }
    >
      <Table
        caption={title}
        columns={detailColumns()}
        rows={filtered}
        rowKey={(r) => `${r.branch_id}-${r.item_id}-${r.direction}`}
        initialSort={{ key: 'amount', dir: 'desc' }}
        empty={<p className="note">{t('obzor.excel.nothing')}</p>}
        footer={
          <tr>
            <td colSpan={4}>{t('obzor.excel.rows', { count: filtered.length })}</td>
            <td>{t('obzor.excel.saldo', { value: m1(saldo) })}</td>
          </tr>
        }
      />
    </Card>
  );
}

export default function ObzorExcel({ section, page, filters, onBranch }: SectionPageProps) {
  const query = useSection<ObzorExcelOut>('/obzor/excel', filters);
  const me = useMe();
  const ctx = { section, page, br: filters.br, per: filters.per };
  const canXlsx = me.data?.permissions.includes('export:xlsx') ?? false;
  return (
    <SectionData query={query}>
      {(data) => {
        const title = t('obzor.excel.summary');
        const cols = summaryColumns();
        return (
          <>
            <CashKpis kpis={data.kpis} period={data.period} />
            <Card
              title={title}
              sub={t('obzor.excel.units', { period: data.period.label })}
              actions={
                canXlsx ? (
                  <a className="btn btn--primary" href={apiUrl('/obzor/excel.xlsx', sectionQuery(filters))} download>
                    {t('obzor.excel.download')}
                  </a>
                ) : null
              }
              exportSlot={
                <CardExport
                  title={title}
                  ctx={ctx}
                  csv={() => ({
                    headers: cols.map((c) => c.header),
                    rows: [...data.branches, data.total].map((b) => [b.name, b.opening, b.inflow, b.outflow, b.net, b.closing, b.diff]),
                  })}
                />
              }
            >
              <Table
                caption={title}
                columns={cols}
                rows={data.branches}
                rowKey={(r) => r.branch_id}
                onRowClick={(r) => toggleBranch(filters, r.branch_id, onBranch)}
                activeKey={filters.br}
                free
                footer={
                  <tr>
                    <td>{data.total.name}</td>
                    <td>{m1(data.total.opening)}</td>
                    <td>{m1(data.total.inflow)}</td>
                    <td>{m1(data.total.outflow)}</td>
                    <td>{m1(data.total.net)}</td>
                    <td>{m1(data.total.closing)}</td>
                    <td className={(data.total.diff ?? 0) < 0 ? 'neg' : undefined}>{m1(data.total.diff)}</td>
                  </tr>
                }
              />
            </Card>
            <Detail rows={data.detail} ctx={ctx} />
          </>
        );
      }}
    </SectionData>
  );
}
```
(`apiUrl` 3-reja `client.ts` dan: `undefined` qiymatlarni tashlab yuboradi, shuning uchun `br = null` bo'lsa URL `?per=m08`. `.btn .btn--primary` — 3-reja `Button` klasslari.)

`frontend/src/sections/kazna/kazna.test.tsx`:
```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../../test/axe';
import { ME_ADMIN } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { json, mockApi } from '../../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../test/render';
import { EMPTY_CALENDAR, ITEM_BRANCHES, KAZNA_CALENDAR, KAZNA_PLATEZHI, KAZNA_SVOD } from '../../test/s1Fixtures';
import KaznaCalendar from './calendar';
import KaznaPlatezhi from './platezhi';
import KaznaSvod from './svod';

const filters = { br: null, per: 'm08' };

describe('Казначейство', () => {
  it('Сводка: KPIs, plan-fact top, orders, calendar, banks and debt', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /kazna/svod': json(KAZNA_SVOD), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    const { container } = renderWithProviders(<KaznaSvod section="kazna" page="svod" filters={filters} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Исполнение плана платежей')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('80,0')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Платёжные поручения' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText('в 8,3 раза')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByRole('img', { name: 'Платёжный календарь' })).toBeInTheDocument();
    const debt = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Задолженность по договорам' });
    expect(within(debt).getByText('просрочка 20 дн.')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(debt).getByText('до 31.12.2026')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });

  it('Сводка: debt filter shows overdue only', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /kazna/svod': json(KAZNA_SVOD), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<KaznaSvod section="kazna" page="svod" filters={filters} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    await user.click(await screen.findByRole('button', { name: 'Просроченные' }));
    const debt = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Задолженность по договорам' });
    expect(within(debt).queryByText('ООО «Гидромонтаж»')).toBeNull();
    expect(within(debt).getByText('ООО «Тест Поставщик»')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('Исполнение платежей: item row opens branch breakdown', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /kazna/platezhi': json(KAZNA_PLATEZHI), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN), 'GET /kazna/platezhi/items/22': json(ITEM_BRANCHES) });
    const { user } = renderWithProviders(<KaznaPlatezhi section="kazna" page="platezhi" filters={filters} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    const table = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Исполнение плана платежей по статьям' });
    expect(within(table).getByText('Итого по всем статьям')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(within(table).getByText('Прочие операционные расходы'));
    const dialog = await screen.findByRole('dialog', { name: 'Прочие операционные расходы' });
    expect(await within(dialog).findByText('Каскад Чирчикских ГЭС')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('Платёжный календарь: requests by branch toggle the filter; empty calendar shows a source note', async () => {
    const onBranch = vi.fn();
    mockApi({ 'GET /kazna/calendar': json(KAZNA_CALENDAR), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    const { user, unmount } = renderWithProviders(<KaznaCalendar section="kazna" page="calendar" filters={filters} onBranch={onBranch} />);
    const requests = await screen.findByRole('list', { name: 'Заявки на оплату в работе' });
    expect(within(requests).getByText('заявок: 2')).toBeInTheDocument();
    await user.click(within(requests).getByRole('button', { name: /Каскад Чирчикских ГЭС/ }));
    expect(onBranch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(3);
    unmount();
    mockApi({ 'GET /kazna/calendar': json({ ...KAZNA_CALENDAR, calendar: EMPTY_CALENDAR, banks: [] }), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    renderWithProviders(<KaznaCalendar section="kazna" page="calendar" filters={filters} onBranch={onBranch} />);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Источник данных не подключён: плановые платежи/)).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getByText(/Источник данных не подключён: остатки по банковским выпискам/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

`frontend/src/sections/kazna/parts/KaznaKpis.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { KaznaKpisOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { formula } from '../../../dash/formulas';
import { formatMoney, moneyParts } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatDate, formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { KpiGrid, KpiTile } from '../../../ui/KpiTile';

function money(value: number | null): { value: string; unit?: string } {
  if (value === null) return { value: t('dash.noValue') };
  const parts = moneyParts(value);
  return { value: parts.value, unit: parts.unit };
}

export function KaznaKpis({ kpis }: { kpis: KaznaKpisOut }) {
  const date = formatDate(kpis.balance_date);
  const balance = money(kpis.balance);
  const receivable = money(kpis.receivable);
  const payable = money(kpis.payable);
  return (
    <KpiGrid>
      <KpiTile icon="bank" label={t('kazna.kpi.balance')} value={balance.value} unit={balance.unit} delta={t('kazna.kpi.balanceNote', { date, banks: kpis.banks })} hint={formula('balance')} />
      <KpiTile
        icon="pct"
        label={t('kazna.kpi.execution')}
        value={kpis.execution === null ? t('dash.noValue') : formatNumber(kpis.execution * 100, 1)}
        unit={kpis.execution === null ? undefined : '%'}
        delta={t('kazna.kpi.executionNote', { fact: formatMoney(kpis.fact), plan: formatMoney(kpis.plan) })}
        hint={`${formula('planExecution')}. ${formula('deviation')}`}
      />
      <KpiTile icon="doc" label={t('kazna.kpi.orders')} value={formatNumber(kpis.orders_count)} delta={t('kazna.kpi.ordersNote', { amount: formatMoney(kpis.orders_amount) })} hint={formula('orders')} />
      <KpiTile icon="clock" label={t('kazna.kpi.requests')} value={formatNumber(kpis.requests_count)} delta={t('kazna.kpi.requestsNote', { amount: formatMoney(kpis.requests_amount) })} hint={formula('requests')} />
      <KpiTile icon="in" label={t('kazna.kpi.receivable')} value={receivable.value} unit={receivable.unit} delta={t('kazna.kpi.at', { date })} hint={formula('receivable')} />
      <KpiTile
        icon="out"
        label={t('kazna.kpi.payable')}
        value={payable.value}
        unit={payable.unit}
        delta={kpis.overdue_payable ? <span className="neg">{t('kazna.kpi.overdue', { amount: formatMoney(kpis.overdue_payable) })}</span> : t('kazna.kpi.at', { date })}
        hint={`${formula('payable')}. ${formula('overdue')}`}
      />
    </KpiGrid>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/kazna/parts/PlanFactCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { PlanFactRowOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { formatMoney, formatPct } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { buildHash } from '../../../router/hash';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';
import { Table, type Column } from '../../../ui/Table';

function Exec({ value }: { value: number | null }) {
  return (
    <span className="exec">
      {formatPct(value, 0)}
      <i aria-hidden="true">
        <b style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (value ?? 0) * 100)}%` }} />
      </i>
    </span>
  );
}

function Deviation({ value }: { value: number }) {
  // TZ 4: план − факт; перерасход (факт > план) — отрицательное отклонение, красным.
  return <span className={value < 0 ? 'neg' : undefined}>{formatMoney(value)}</span>;
}

export function PlanFactCard({ rows, limit, onItem, ctx, className }: { rows: PlanFactRowOut[]; limit?: number; onItem(id: number): void; ctx: ExportContext; className?: string }) {
  const shown = limit ? rows.slice(0, limit) : rows;
  const plan = rows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.plan, 0);
  const fact = rows.reduce((s, r) => s + r.fact, 0);
  const title = t('kazna.planFact.title');
  const columns: Column<PlanFactRowOut>[] = [
    { key: 'name', header: t('dash.item'), cell: (r) => r.name, sortValue: (r) => r.name, wrap: true },
    { key: 'plan', header: t('dash.plan'), cell: (r) => formatMoney(r.plan), sortValue: (r) => r.plan, align: 'right' },
    { key: 'fact', header: t('dash.fact'), cell: (r) => formatMoney(r.fact), sortValue: (r) => r.fact, align: 'right' },
    { key: 'exec', header: t('kazna.planFact.execution'), cell: (r) => <Exec value={r.execution} />, sortValue: (r) => r.execution, align: 'right' },
    { key: 'dev', header: t('kazna.planFact.deviation'), cell: (r) => <Deviation value={r.deviation} />, sortValue: (r) => r.deviation, align: 'right' },
  ];
  return (
    <Card
      className={className}
      title={title}
      sub={limit ? t('kazna.planFact.subTop', { count: limit }) : t('kazna.planFact.sub')}
      exportSlot={
        <CardExport
          title={title}
          ctx={ctx}
          csv={() => ({ headers: [t('dash.item'), t('dash.plan'), t('dash.fact'), t('kazna.planFact.execution'), t('kazna.planFact.deviation')], rows: rows.map((r) => [r.name, r.plan, r.fact, r.execution === null ? null : Math.round(r.execution * 1000) / 10, r.deviation]) })}
        />
      }
    >
      <Table
        caption={title}
        columns={columns}
        rows={shown}
        rowKey={(r) => r.item_id}
        onRowClick={(r) => onItem(r.item_id)}
        free
        footer={
          <tr>
            <td>{t('kazna.planFact.total')}</td>
            <td>{formatMoney(plan)}</td>
            <td>{formatMoney(fact)}</td>
            <td>
              <Exec value={plan ? fact / plan : null} />
            </td>
            <td>
              <Deviation value={plan - fact} />
            </td>
          </tr>
        }
      />
      {limit ? (
        <a className="more" href={buildHash({ kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'platezhi', br: ctx.br, per: ctx.per })}>
          {t('kazna.planFact.all')}
        </a>
      ) : null}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { OrderStatusOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { NoData } from '../../../dash/DataState';
import { formatMoney } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';

const COLORS = ['#c3d7ee', '#9dc0e8', '#5fd0e0', '#2f8ef5', '#0b4fa8', 'var(--neg)'];

export function OrdersCard({ orders, className }: { orders: OrderStatusOut[]; className?: string }) {
  const count = orders.reduce((s, o) => s + o.count, 0);
  const amount = orders.reduce((s, o) => s + o.amount, 0);
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={t('kazna.orders.title')} sub={t('kazna.orders.sub')}>
      {count === 0 ? (
        <NoData kind="source" source={t('dash.source.orders')} />
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="bigline">
            <b>{formatNumber(count)}</b>
            <span>{formatMoney(amount)}</span>
          </div>
          <div className="pipe" aria-hidden="true">
            {orders.map((o, i) => (o.count ? <div key={o.status} style={{ flexGrow: o.count, background: COLORS[i] }} /> : null))}
          </div>
          <ul className="st-list">
            {orders.map((o, i) => (
              <li key={o.status}>
                <i style={{ background: COLORS[i] }} aria-hidden="true" />
                <span>{o.status.charAt(0).toUpperCase() + o.status.slice(1)}</span>
                <b>{formatNumber(o.count)}</b>
                <small>{formatMoney(o.amount)}</small>
              </li>
            ))}
          </ul>
        </>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/kazna/parts/CalendarCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useMemo, useRef } from 'react';
import type { CalendarOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import { barConfig, ChartCanvas, type ChartHandle } from '../../../dash/chart';
import { NoData } from '../../../dash/DataState';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { formula } from '../../../dash/formulas';
import { formatMoney, formatPct } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatDate, formatNumber } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';

const KINDS = ['taxes', 'salary', 'loans', 'contracts'] as const;
const COLORS = ['#0b4fa8', '#2f8ef5', '#5fd0e0', '#9dc0e8'];
const WEEKDAYS = ['Вс', 'Пн', 'Вт', 'Ср', 'Чт', 'Пт', 'Сб'];

function dayLabel(iso: string): string[] {
  const d = new Date(`${iso}T00:00:00Z`);
  return [`${iso.slice(8, 10)}.${iso.slice(5, 7)}`, WEEKDAYS[d.getUTCDay()] ?? ''];
}

/** TZ 5: 14 дней вперёд по видам платежей, сумма к оплате, остаток и во сколько раз он покрывает платежи. */
export function CalendarCard({ calendar, ctx, className }: { calendar: CalendarOut; ctx: ExportContext; className?: string }) {
  const title = t('kazna.calendar.title');
  const chartRef = useRef<ChartHandle | null>(null);
  const config = useMemo(
    () =>
      barConfig({
        labels: calendar.days.map((d) => dayLabel(d.date)),
        series: KINDS.map((k, i) => ({ label: t(`kazna.calendar.${k}` as MessageKey), data: calendar.days.map((d) => d[k]), color: COLORS[i] ?? '#999999' })),
        stacked: true,
      }),
    [calendar],
  );
  const table = {
    headers: [t('kazna.calendar.day'), ...KINDS.map((k) => t(`kazna.calendar.${k}` as MessageKey))],
    rows: calendar.days.map((d) => [formatDate(d.date), ...KINDS.map((k) => d[k])]),
  };
  const empty = calendar.as_of === null || calendar.total === 0;
  const cover = calendar.coverage;
  return (
    <Card
      className={className}
      title={title}
      sub={calendar.start ? t('kazna.calendar.sub', { date: formatDate(calendar.start) }) : undefined}
      exportSlot={empty ? undefined : <CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} chartRef={chartRef} csv={() => table} />}
    >
      {empty ? (
        <NoData kind="source" source={t('dash.source.calendar')} />
      ) : (
        <>
          <div className="cover" title={formula('coverage')}>
            <div>
              <span>{t('kazna.calendar.toPay')}</span>
              <b>{formatMoney(calendar.total)}</b>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>{t('kazna.calendar.balance')}</span>
              <b>{formatMoney(calendar.balance)}</b>
            </div>
            <div>
              <span>{t('kazna.calendar.coverage')}</span>
              <b className={cover !== null && cover < 1 ? 'neg' : undefined}>
                {cover === null ? '—' : cover >= 1 ? t('kazna.calendar.times', { value: formatNumber(cover, 1) }) : t('kazna.calendar.part', { value: formatPct(cover, 0) })}
              </b>
            </div>
          </div>
          <div className="legend">
            {KINDS.map((k, i) => (
              <span key={k}>
                <i style={{ background: COLORS[i] }} />
                {t(`kazna.calendar.${k}` as MessageKey)}
              </span>
            ))}
          </div>
          <ChartCanvas config={config} label={title} table={table} chartRef={chartRef} />
        </>
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/kazna/parts/DebtCard.tsx`:
```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { ContractRowOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import { NoData } from '../../../dash/DataState';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { formatMoney } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t, type MessageKey } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatDate } from '../../../i18n/format';
import { buildHash } from '../../../router/hash';
import { Badge } from '../../../ui/Badge';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';
import { Table, type Column } from '../../../ui/Table';

type DebtFilter = 'all' | 'receivable' | 'payable' | 'overdue';
const FILTERS: DebtFilter[] = ['all', 'receivable', 'payable', 'overdue'];

function term(c: ContractRowOut): string {
  if (c.overdue_days > 0) return t('kazna.debt.late', { days: c.overdue_days });
  return c.end_date ? t('kazna.debt.until', { date: formatDate(c.end_date) }) : '—';
}

/** TZ 5: реестр крупнейших договоров с фильтром; просроченные первыми (tartib — serverdan). */
export function DebtCard({ contracts, limit, hasData, ctx, className }: { contracts: ContractRowOut[]; limit?: number; hasData: boolean; ctx: ExportContext; className?: string }) {
  const [filter, setFilter] = useState<DebtFilter>('all');
  const title = t('kazna.debt.title');
  const rows = contracts
    .filter((c) => filter === 'all' || (filter === 'overdue' ? c.overdue_days > 0 : c.kind === filter))
    .slice(0, limit ?? contracts.length);
  const columns: Column<ContractRowOut>[] = [
    { key: 'cp', header: t('kazna.debt.cp'), cell: (c) => c.cp_name, wrap: true },
    { key: 'number', header: t('kazna.debt.contract'), cell: (c) => c.number },
    { key: 'branch', header: t('dash.branch'), cell: (c) => c.branch_name, wrap: true },
    { key: 'kind', header: t('kazna.debt.kind'), cell: (c) => t(`kazna.debt.${c.kind}` as MessageKey) },
    { key: 'amount', header: t('dash.amount'), cell: (c) => <b>{formatMoney(c.amount)}</b>, align: 'right' },
    { key: 'term', header: t('kazna.debt.term'), cell: (c) => <Badge tone={c.overdue_days > 0 ? 'neg' : 'neutral'}>{term(c)}</Badge> },
  ];
  return (
    <Card
      className={className}
      title={title}
      sub={limit ? t('kazna.debt.subTop') : t('kazna.debt.sub')}
      actions={
        <div className="seg" role="group" aria-label={t('kazna.debt.filter')}>
          {FILTERS.map((f) => (
            <button key={f} type="button" aria-pressed={filter === f} onClick={() => setFilter(f)}>
              {t(`kazna.debt.${f}` as MessageKey)}
            </button>
          ))}
        </div>
      }
      exportSlot={
        hasData ? (
          <CardExport
            title={title}
            ctx={ctx}
            csv={() => ({
              headers: [t('kazna.debt.cp'), t('kazna.debt.contract'), t('dash.branch'), t('kazna.debt.kind'), t('dash.amount'), t('kazna.debt.term')],
              rows: rows.map((c) => [c.cp_name, c.number, c.branch_name, t(`kazna.debt.${c.kind}` as MessageKey), c.amount, term(c)]),
            })}
          />
        ) : undefined
      }
    >
      {hasData ? (
        <Table caption={title} columns={columns} rows={rows} rowKey={(c) => c.contract_id} free empty={<p className="note">{t('kazna.debt.none')}</p>} />
      ) : (
        <NoData kind="source" source={t('dash.source.debt')} />
      )}
      {limit ? (
        <a className="more" href={buildHash({ kind: 'section', section: 'kazna', page: 'dogovory', br: ctx.br, per: ctx.per })}>
          {t('kazna.debt.allContracts')}
        </a>
      ) : null}
    </Card>
  );
}
```
