```tsx
import { useState } from 'react';
import type { KaznaSvodOut } from '../../api/types';
import { ItemDialog } from '../obzor/parts/ItemDialog';
import type { SectionPageProps } from '../registry';
import { SectionData } from '../SectionData';
import { useSection } from '../useSection';
import { BanksCard } from './parts/BanksCard';
import { CalendarCard } from './parts/CalendarCard';
import { DebtCard } from './parts/DebtCard';
import { KaznaKpis } from './parts/KaznaKpis';
import { OrdersCard } from './parts/OrdersCard';
import { PlanFactCard } from './parts/PlanFactCard';

export default function KaznaSvod({ section, page, filters }: SectionPageProps) {
  const query = useSection<KaznaSvodOut>('/kazna/svod', filters);
  const [item, setItem] = useState<number | null>(null);
  const ctx = { section, page, br: filters.br, per: filters.per };
  return (
    <SectionData query={query}>
      {(data) => (
        <>
          <KaznaKpis kpis={data.kpis} />
          <div className="grid">
            <PlanFactCard className="c8 wide" rows={data.plan_fact} limit={5} onItem={setItem} ctx={ctx} />
            <OrdersCard className="c4 wide" orders={data.orders} />
            <CalendarCard className="c7" calendar={data.calendar} ctx={ctx} />
            <BanksCard className="c5" banks={data.banks} date={data.kpis.balance_date} />
            <DebtCard className="c12" contracts={data.contracts} limit={6} hasData={data.kpis.payable !== null || data.kpis.receivable !== null} ctx={ctx} />
          </div>
          {item !== null ? <ItemDialog path={`/kazna/platezhi/items/${item}`} filters={filters} onClose={() => setItem(null)} /> : null}
        </>
      )}
    </SectionData>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import type { KaznaCalendarOut } from '../../api/types';
import { Rank } from '../../dash/Rank';
import { formatMoney } from '../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../i18n';
import { Card } from '../../ui/Card';
import type { SectionPageProps } from '../registry';
import { SectionData } from '../SectionData';
import { toggleBranch, useSection } from '../useSection';
import { BanksCard } from './parts/BanksCard';
import { CalendarCard } from './parts/CalendarCard';

export default function KaznaCalendar({ section, page, filters, onBranch }: SectionPageProps) {
  const query = useSection<KaznaCalendarOut>('/kazna/calendar', filters);
  const ctx = { section, page, br: filters.br, per: filters.per };
  return (
    <SectionData query={query}>
      {(data) => (
        <div className="grid">
          <CalendarCard className="c8 wide" calendar={data.calendar} ctx={ctx} />
          <BanksCard className="c4 wide" banks={data.banks} date={data.period.date_to} />
          <Card className="c12" title={t('kazna.requests.title')} sub={t('kazna.requests.sub')}>
            <Rank
              label={t('kazna.requests.title')}
              items={data.requests.map((r) => ({ id: r.branch_id, name: r.name, value: r.amount, note: t('kazna.requests.note', { count: r.count }) }))}
              format={(v) => formatMoney(v)}
              onSelect={(id) => toggleBranch(filters, Number(id), onBranch)}
              activeId={filters.br}
            />
          </Card>
        </div>
      )}
    </SectionData>
  );
}
```

```tsx
import { screen, within } from '@testing-library/react';
import { describe, expect, it, vi } from 'vitest';
import { expectNoAxeViolations } from '../../test/axe';
import { ME_ADMIN } from '../../test/fixtures';
import { json, mockApi } from '../../test/mockApi';
import { renderWithProviders } from '../../test/render';
import { KAZNA_CELEVYE, KAZNA_DOGOVORY } from '../../test/s1Fixtures';
import KaznaCelevye from './celevye';
import KaznaDogovory from './dogovory';

describe('Казначейство / Договоры и Целевые средства', () => {
  it('lists contracts and branch debt; clicking a branch selects it', async () => {
    const onBranch = vi.fn();
    mockApi({ 'GET /kazna/dogovory': json(KAZNA_DOGOVORY), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    const { user, container } = renderWithProviders(<KaznaDogovory section="kazna" page="dogovory" filters={{ br: null, per: 'm08' }} onBranch={onBranch} />);
    const branches = await screen.findByRole('table', { name: 'Задолженность по филиалам' });
    expect(within(branches).getByText('30,0 млн')).toHaveClass('neg');
    await user.click(within(branches).getByText('Каскад Чирчикских ГЭС'));
    expect(onBranch).toHaveBeenCalledWith(3);
    await user.click(screen.getByRole('button', { name: 'Кредиторская' }));
    const debt = screen.getByRole('table', { name: 'Задолженность по договорам' });
    expect(within(debt).getByText('ООО «Гидромонтаж»')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(within(debt).queryByText('ООО «Тест Поставщик»')).toBeNull();
    await expectNoAxeViolations(container);
  });

  it('shows target funds by source and branch', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /kazna/celevye': json(KAZNA_CELEVYE), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    renderWithProviders(<KaznaCelevye section="kazna" page="celevye" filters={{ br: null, per: 'q3' }} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(await screen.findByText('Бюджетные средства на капитальные вложения')).toBeInTheDocument();
    expect(screen.getAllByText('50%').length).toBeGreaterThan(0);
    expect(screen.getByText('50% от выделенного')).toBeInTheDocument();
  });

  it('shows the source note when funds are not loaded', async () => {
    mockApi({ 'GET /kazna/celevye': json({ ...KAZNA_CELEVYE, sources: [], branches: [] }), 'GET /auth/me': json(ME_ADMIN) });
    renderWithProviders(<KaznaCelevye section="kazna" page="celevye" filters={{ br: null, per: 'q3' }} onBranch={vi.fn()} />);
    expect(await screen.findByText(/Источник данных не подключён: целевые средства/)).toBeInTheDocument();
  });
});
```

```tsx
import type { DebtBranchRowOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { CardExport } from '../../../dash/CardExport';
import type { ExportContext } from '../../../dash/exportLog';
import { formatMoney } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatDate } from '../../../i18n/format';
import type { FilterState } from '../../../shell/useFilters';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';
import { Table, type Column } from '../../../ui/Table';
import { toggleBranch } from '../../useSection';

export function DebtBranchCard({ rows, date, filters, onBranch, ctx, className }: { rows: DebtBranchRowOut[]; date: string; filters: FilterState; onBranch(br: number | null): void; ctx: ExportContext; className?: string }) {
  const title = t('kazna.debtBranch.title');
  const columns: Column<DebtBranchRowOut>[] = [
    { key: 'name', header: t('dash.branch'), cell: (r) => r.name, sortValue: (r) => r.name, wrap: true },
    { key: 'rec', header: t('kazna.debt.receivable'), cell: (r) => formatMoney(r.receivable), sortValue: (r) => r.receivable, align: 'right' },
    { key: 'pay', header: t('kazna.debt.payable'), cell: (r) => formatMoney(r.payable), sortValue: (r) => r.payable, align: 'right' },
    {
      key: 'over',
      header: t('kazna.debtBranch.overdue'),
      align: 'right',
      sortValue: (r) => r.overdue_payable,
      cell: (r) => <span className={r.payable && r.overdue_payable / r.payable > 0.2 ? 'neg' : undefined}>{formatMoney(r.overdue_payable)}</span>,
    },
  ];
  return (
    <Card
      className={className}
      title={title}
      sub={t('kazna.debtBranch.sub', { date: formatDate(date) })}
      exportSlot={<CardExport title={title} ctx={ctx} csv={() => ({ headers: columns.map((c) => c.header), rows: rows.map((r) => [r.name, r.receivable, r.payable, r.overdue_payable]) })} />}
    >
      <Table caption={title} columns={columns} rows={rows} rowKey={(r) => r.branch_id} onRowClick={(r) => toggleBranch(filters, r.branch_id, onBranch)} activeKey={filters.br} free />
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/kazna/parts/FundsCards.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { FundRowOut } from '../../../api/types';
import { NoData } from '../../../dash/DataState';
import { formula } from '../../../dash/formulas';
import { Rank } from '../../../dash/Rank';
import { formatMoney, formatPct } from '../../../dash/money';
import { t } from '../../../i18n';
import { formatDate } from '../../../i18n/format';
import type { FilterState } from '../../../shell/useFilters';
import { Card } from '../../../ui/Card';
import { toggleBranch } from '../../useSection';

export function FundsBySource({ rows, date, className }: { rows: FundRowOut[]; date: string; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={t('kazna.funds.title')} sub={t('kazna.funds.sub', { date: formatDate(date) })}>
      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <NoData kind="source" source={t('dash.source.funds')} />
      ) : (
        rows.map((f) => (
          <div key={f.key} className="prog" title={formula('targetUse')}>
            <div className="prog__h">
              <span>{f.name}</span>
              <b>{formatPct(f.share, 0)}</b>
            </div>
            <div className="prog__t" aria-hidden="true">
              <i style={{ width: `${Math.min(100, (f.share ?? 0) * 100)}%` }} />
            </div>
            <div className="prog__f">
              <span>{t('kazna.funds.used', { amount: formatMoney(f.used) })}</span>
              <span>{t('kazna.funds.allocated', { amount: formatMoney(f.allocated) })}</span>
            </div>
          </div>
        ))
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}

export function FundsByBranch({ rows, filters, onBranch, className }: { rows: FundRowOut[]; filters: FilterState; onBranch(br: number | null): void; className?: string }) {
  return (
    <Card className={className} title={t('kazna.funds.branches')} sub={t('kazna.funds.branchesSub')}>
      {rows.length === 0 ? (
        <NoData kind="source" source={t('dash.source.funds')} />
      ) : (
        <Rank
          label={t('kazna.funds.branches')}
          items={rows.map((f) => ({ id: f.key, name: f.name, value: f.used, note: t('kazna.funds.share', { share: formatPct(f.share, 0) }) }))}
          onSelect={(id) => toggleBranch(filters, Number(id), onBranch)}
          activeId={filters.br}
        />
      )}
    </Card>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/kazna/dogovory.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { KaznaDogovoryOut } from '../../api/types';
import type { SectionPageProps } from '../registry';
import { SectionData } from '../SectionData';
import { useSection } from '../useSection';
import { DebtBranchCard } from './parts/DebtBranchCard';
import { DebtCard } from './parts/DebtCard';
import { KaznaKpis } from './parts/KaznaKpis';

export default function KaznaDogovory({ section, page, filters, onBranch }: SectionPageProps) {
  const query = useSection<KaznaDogovoryOut>('/kazna/dogovory', filters);
  const ctx = { section, page, br: filters.br, per: filters.per };
  return (
    <SectionData query={query}>
      {(data) => (
        <>
          <KaznaKpis kpis={data.kpis} />
          <div className="grid">
            <DebtCard className="c12" contracts={data.contracts} hasData={data.kpis.payable !== null || data.kpis.receivable !== null} ctx={ctx} />
            <DebtBranchCard className="c12" rows={data.by_branch} date={data.kpis.balance_date} filters={filters} onBranch={onBranch} ctx={ctx} />
          </div>
        </>
      )}
    </SectionData>
  );
}
```

`frontend/src/sections/kazna/celevye.tsx`:
```tsx
import type { KaznaCelevyeOut } from '../../api/types';
import type { SectionPageProps } from '../registry';
import { SectionData } from '../SectionData';
import { useSection } from '../useSection';
import { FundsByBranch, FundsBySource } from './parts/FundsCards';

export default function KaznaCelevye({ filters, onBranch }: SectionPageProps) {
  const query = useSection<KaznaCelevyeOut>('/kazna/celevye', filters);
  return (
    <SectionData query={query}>
      {(data) => (
        <div className="grid">
          <FundsBySource className="c6" rows={data.sources} date={data.period.date_to} />
          <FundsByBranch className="c6" rows={data.branches} filters={filters} onBranch={onBranch} />
        </div>
      )}
    </SectionData>
  );
}
```

```ts
import { expect, test, type Page } from '@playwright/test';
import { adminFor, expectNoHorizontalScroll, signIn } from '../fixtures';

function watchCsp(page: Page): string[] {
  const violations: string[] = [];
  page.on('console', (msg) => {
    const text = msg.text();
    if (/Content Security Policy|Refused to/i.test(text)) violations.push(text);
  });
  return violations;
}

async function open(page: Page, hash: string, heading: string | RegExp): Promise<void> {
  await page.goto(`/${hash}`);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: heading })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('alert')).toHaveCount(0);
}

test('Обзор и Казначейство: все страницы, внимание, выгрузка Excel', async ({ page }, info) => {
  const csp = watchCsp(page);
  await page.goto('/#obzor/svod');
  await signIn(page, adminFor(info.project.name));
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Обзор: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByText('Начальный остаток')).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.getByRole('group', { name: 'Карта Узбекистана с расположением ГЭС' })).toBeVisible();
  await expect(page.locator('canvas[role="img"]').first()).toBeVisible();
  await expectNoHorizontalScroll(page);

  await page.getByRole('button', { name: /Чарвакская ГЭС/ }).first().click();
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { name: 'Чарвакская ГЭС' })).toBeVisible();

  await open(page, '#obzor/vnimanie', 'Требует внимания');
  await expect(page.getByText(/Проверено правил: \d+/)).toBeVisible();
  await open(page, '#obzor/postup', 'Поступления');
  await open(page, '#obzor/rasxod', 'Расходование');
  await open(page, '#obzor/excel', 'Таблица Excel');
  const download = page.waitForEvent('download');
  await page.getByRole('link', { name: 'Скачать Excel' }).click();
  expect((await download).suggestedFilename()).toMatch(/^UGEKAZNA_cashflow_all_ytd_\d{8}\.xlsx$/);

  await open(page, '#kazna/svod', 'Казначейство: сводка');
  await expect(page.getByText('Исполнение плана платежей').first()).toBeVisible();
  await open(page, '#kazna/platezhi', 'Исполнение платежей');
  await open(page, '#kazna/calendar', 'Платёжный календарь');
  await expect(page.getByText('К оплате за 14 дней')).toBeVisible();
  await open(page, '#kazna/dogovory', 'Договоры и задолженность');
  await page.getByRole('button', { name: 'Просроченные' }).click();
  await open(page, '#kazna/celevye', 'Целевые средства');
  await expectNoHorizontalScroll(page);
  expect(csp).toEqual([]);
});

test('Экономист филиала не видит другие филиалы ни в интерфейсе, ни через API', async ({ page }, info) => {
  test.skip(info.project.name !== 'desktop-1440', 'bitta kenglik yetarli');
  await page.goto('/#obzor/svod?br=4');
  await signIn(page, 'e2e_branch');
  await expect(page).toHaveURL(/br=3/);
  await expect(page.getByRole('heading', { level: 1, name: 'Обзор: сводка' })).toBeVisible();
  const denied = await page.request.get('/api/v1/obzor/svod?br=4');
  expect(denied.status()).toBe(403);
  const own = await page.request.get('/api/v1/kazna/dogovory');
  expect(own.status()).toBe(200);
  const body = (await own.json()) as { contracts: Array<{ branch_id: number }> };
  expect(body.contracts.every((c) => c.branch_id === 3)).toBe(true);
});
```
